# TurtleBot 4 (ROS 2 Jazzy, Nav2) — Autonomy Meter dossier

**Scope.** TurtleBot 4 Standard and Lite: iRobot Create 3 base (closed firmware, release I.0.0) + Raspberry Pi 4B + TurtleBot 4 software 2.x on ROS 2 Jazzy + Nav2 1.3.14. Taxonomy: `definitions.md` v0.2 and `event.schema.json` v0.2 (attached to the brief). This dossier is a new run for this robot only; the SO-101 dossier is not revised here.

**Pinned sources** (branch head on 2026-10-31; zip archives of these exact commits were read on disk, and every `#L` anchor below points at them; five files per track were re-fetched from raw.githubusercontent.com and were byte-identical):
- `turtlebot4` jazzy @ `7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00` (= tag 2.1.1; packages 2.1.1)
- `turtlebot4_robot` jazzy @ `28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0`
- `turtlebot4_desktop` jazzy @ `d831a5a3d2323d2db1ce79e5b909beecbeed3dcf`
- `turtlebot4_simulator` jazzy @ `b7d0f3b973258481f6cd8137bef9240fb567f566`
- `turtlebot4_setup` jazzy @ `5fe58657aeff57d28cb2cfb4f0c6cc6c62a4b96c`
- `turtlebot4-user-manual` gh-pages @ `4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0`
- `irobot_create_msgs` jazzy @ `b4986ef5dc2e07d8aa7471f29c56b617473352c5`
- `create3_docs` main @ `9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca`
- `create3_sim` jazzy @ `6c305843afcfe094bf8eb2a0c6997f531e526b9d`
- `create3_examples` jazzy @ `fe3d408a266b755b94cb7334a92f2d58dd661c13`
- `navigation2` jazzy @ `d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed`
- `docs.nav2.org` rolling @ `74f751f161154075af6c2b4b9cd60bebf417cab8`
Upstream ROS files cited by the Nav2 and stack tracks (ros2/rcl_interfaces, ros2/rcl, ros2/rclcpp, ros2/rosbag2, ros2/teleop_twist_joy, BehaviorTree.CPP) are pinned to the branch-head SHAs given inline; BehaviorTree.CPP was read on `master`, not the Jazzy release, and the facts that depend on it are tagged CLAIM.

**Access dates.** Pinned trees and rendered documentation pages: 2026-10-31. Live web fetches made by the Evidence track and the final re-verification pass: 2026-11-03 (stated inline where they occur). **Tags.** VERIFIED = the primary text was read at the pinned version; CLAIM = maker/community statement, abstract-only, or a source that could not be re-opened from the sandbox; UNKNOWN = not established; every UNKNOWN is also listed at the end. Every fact carries its tag and URL inline in the form `[TAG: URL, acc. date]`. Companion files: `signal_map.yaml` (72 signals B1–B20, T1–T22, N1–N24, E1–E6), `sources.csv` (1,107 distinct URLs), `integration_notes.md` (simulation steps and event-logging recipe).

**Final check (2026-11-03).** Every VERIFIED citation was re-opened by two verification passes after the merge. Pinned GitHub `blob@commit` anchors: 1,768 citations across 274 distinct files, all present at their commits, 1,664 confirmed on the cited line, 104 with the identifier 1-3 lines from the anchor (anchors widened to ranges in this version where a specific line was named; the rest are paraphrase-supported and left), 0 contradicted. Rendered documentation, papers, dataset pages and regulation texts: 122 citations across 72 pages, 112 confirmed, 0 contradicted, 6 wording mismatches corrected inline (noted "re-checked 2026-11-03"), 3 eur-lex URLs unreachable (bot challenge; the co-cited Publications Office Cellar copies were confirmed), 1 OpenAlex query not re-run. The `recheck_2026-11-03` column of `sources.csv` carries the per-URL result (file-level for anchored URLs). Nothing was downgraded from VERIFIED on factual grounds; one figure (`wheel_vels` 62.5 Hz) was downgraded to CLAIM because its documentation line is an upstream copy error.

**How this file was produced.** Four parallel evidence tracks (Create 3 base; TB4 stack; Nav2; external evidence) each read their sources at the pins and wrote their sections with inline citations; the lead merged them in the brief's section order, reconciled overlaps (noted as _lead note_), built the consolidated signal table in Section 6, and decided the uncovered cases at the end. Track blocks are marked `_[... track]_` in their headings. Declared limits that travel with the numbers: no live ROS 2 system was run (all rates and QoS are configured or compiled values, not measured); the simulator recipe is untested on this host; no list price was obtainable from an official page; firmware behaviours are VERIFIED as documentation statements only because the Create 3 firmware is closed; no public TurtleBot 4 / Create 3 dataset with human-vs-autonomous labels exists, so no human share was computed for this platform.

## 10-line summary

1. TurtleBot 4 is Clearpath Robotics' education/research robot on an iRobot Create 3 base with a Raspberry Pi 4B, Apache-2.0 software, closed Create 3 firmware; the Create 3 docs name it "a derivative product of iRobot's Create 3 robot made by Clearpath Robotics" [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/faq/faq.md#L253, acc. 2026-10-31]. No list price could be read from an official page [UNKNOWN].
2. **There is no hardware emergency stop.** The only "EStop" is the Create 3 `e_stop` ROS 2 service that disables wheel power ("The robot will not respond to velocity commands when `e_stop_on` is true") [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/safety.md#L79, acc. 2026-10-31]; its state is visible as `wheel_status.wheels_enabled` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/irobot_create_msgs/blob/b4986ef5dc2e07d8aa7471f29c56b617473352c5/msg/WheelStatus.msg#L8, acc. 2026-10-31] and TurtleBot 4 binds it to the Create 3 power button (short press) and the controller's B button as a **toggle** [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/config/turtlebot4.yaml#L43; https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/src/turtlebot4.cpp#L523, acc. 2026-10-31].
3. **There is no arbitration between human and AI commands anywhere in the stack**: no twist_mux or priority node exists in any TB4 repository (grep of the pinned trees, only hit `use_realtime_priority` in nav2.yaml), and the Create 3 firmware "will execute the last received message" on `/cmd_vel` until a 0.5 s stale timeout (`wheels_stop_threshold_sec`) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/moving-the-robot.md#L26; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/releases/h_2_5.md#L29, acc. 2026-10-31]. Human teleop (keyboard, Bluetooth controller) and Nav2 therefore interleave on the same topic, and control attribution must be done per message from the DDS publisher identity.
4. The AI chain is `bt_navigator → controller_server (MPPI, 20 Hz) → /cmd_vel_nav → velocity_smoother (1.0 s timeout) → /cmd_vel_smoothed → collision_monitor → /cmd_vel → create3_republisher → /_do_not_use/cmd_vel_stamped → firmware` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/config/nav2.yaml#L299; https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/config/nav2.yaml#L300; https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/launch/robot.launch.py#L86; https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/launch/robot.launch.py#L87, acc. 2026-10-31]; the Nav2 docking server, if ever used, publishes straight to `/cmd_vel` and bypasses the smoother and collision monitor (Nav2 track, Section 3.7).
5. Firmware **reflexes** (bump, cliff, wheel-drop, stuck, panic, proximity slowdown, virtual wall, gyro-cal; all on by default except dock-avoid) "temporarily override any user-provided command" [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/reflexes.md#L5, acc. 2026-10-31], firmware actions (dock, undock, wall-follow, drive goals) pre-empt `/cmd_vel`, and no topic reports reflex activity; the taxonomy has no state for an evasive safety manoeuvre inside human time (Uncovered case UC-4).
6. **The default TurtleBot 4 configuration hides the base's safety signals**: `republisher.yaml` relays `battery_state, dock_status, imu, interface_buttons, odom, tf, wheel_status` but leaves `hazard_detection`, `kidnap_status`, `stop_status`, `wheel_vels` commented out [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/config/republisher.yaml#L10-L11–L19, acc. 2026-10-31]; an observer on the robot namespace cannot see bump/cliff/kidnap stops unless that file is changed or it reads `/_do_not_use/*` on the Pi (UC-10).
7. The strongest **passive control-authority signals** are: `/function_calls` (std_msgs/String published by turtlebot4_node on every button/menu/controller function: Dock, Undock, EStop, Wall Follow, Power) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/src/turtlebot4.cpp#L662-L665, acc. 2026-10-31]; `/joy` with the L1/R1 dead-man; `/interface_buttons`; `/wheel_status.wheels_enabled`; `/navigate_to_pose/_action/status` (transient-local, so a late joiner sees the current goal state) and `/behavior_tree_log` for Nav2; `/collision_monitor_state`; `/dock_status`; and, for attribution, each `/cmd_vel` message's `publisher_gid` — which **rosbag2 does not store** (it writes only serialized data, topic, receive and send timestamps) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros2/rosbag2/blob/cb8fa198b93bfed90e2417b52b8645fb9ef89c2b/rosbag2_storage/include/rosbag2_storage/serialized_bag_message.hpp#L27, acc. 2026-10-31].
8. The only shared-control mode in the whole stack is Nav2's `AssistedTeleop` action (human twist on `cmd_vel_teleop`, collision-filtered, republished to `cmd_vel_nav`) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_behaviors/plugins/assisted_teleop.cpp#L83-L155, acc. 2026-10-31]; it is loaded by the TB4 `nav2.yaml` but nothing in TurtleBot 4 invokes it. Nav2 recoveries (ClearCostmap, Spin 1.57 rad, Wait 5 s, BackUp 0.30 m; up to 6 rounds) are `ai` time with an `x_recovery` flag, not interventions; Nav2 has no help-request path, so `ai_requested_help` can never be evidenced from Nav2 alone.
9. **No public dataset or bag recorded on TurtleBot 4, TurtleBot 4 Lite or Create 3 labels teleoperated vs autonomous time** (Hugging Face, arXiv, OpenAlex and the pinned repositories searched 2026-11-03; Evidence track Section 9) — so no human share was computed; the nearest substitutes (GNM/HuRoN on a Roomba-class base, SCAND, Marathon 2 Nav2 logs, simulated-TB4 RoboVAST) carry no usable control-authority label. Published practice for this robot class measures success rate, recoveries and collisions, never who held control over time.
10. Regulation: the EU Machinery Regulation 2023/1230 (applies from **14 January 2027**, Art. 54) requires, for control systems with self-evolving behaviour operating "with varying levels of autonomy", a tracing log of intervention-related data (Annex III 1.2.1(f), five years) and one-year retention of safety-decision data (1.2.1 second list (b)) [VERIFIED: Official Journal XHTML via publications.europa.eu Cellar, acc. 2026-11-03; Evidence track Section 13]; a stock TB4 has no machine-learning safety function, so these hooks apply only by analogy, and the AI Act's Art. 12/14 duties are not obviously triggered. Nothing in the stack enables DDS security by default (`use_security = false` in rcl) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros2/rcl/blob/18d96c9c2b98e616324ae9a2c7701dd0b0886ff0/rcl/src/rcl/security.c#L34, acc. 2026-10-31] and the Create 3 webserver is plain HTTP on port 80.

## 1. Overview: maker, purpose, price, availability, typical users, licences

_Lead note._ Two tracks cover this: the TB4 layer (Clearpath software, models, licences) and the Create 3 base (iRobot). Open Robotics / OSRF's role in the TurtleBot 4 programme is not stated in any pinned source and is left UNKNOWN rather than asserted from general knowledge. Price: neither the Clearpath product page (form-gated redirect) nor edu.irobot.com (HTTP 502 through the sandbox proxy after access was granted) yielded a list price; reseller prices are excluded by the source rules.

### 1. Platform identity, models, price, availability, licences  _[TB4 stack track]_

**Maker and purpose.** The user manual describes the TurtleBot 4 as "a ROS 2-based mobile robot intended for education and research", built on an iRobot Create 3 base with a TurtleBot 4 shell, a User Interface (UI) board and a Raspberry Pi 4B running the TurtleBot 4 software [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/overview/features.md#L14, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/overview/features.md#L16, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://turtlebot.github.io/turtlebot4-user-manual/overview/features.html, acc. 2026-10-31]. The software is Apache-2.0 and lives on the TurtleBot GitHub organisation [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/software/overview.md#L7, acc. 2026-10-31]. Launch-file copyright headers name Clearpath Robotics, Inc. [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/launch/robot.launch.py#L2, acc. 2026-10-31]; package.xml maintainers are Chris Iverach-Brereton and Hilary Luo for every package in the five repos [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/package.xml, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/package.xml, acc. 2026-10-31]. The Clearpath web page for TurtleBot 4 redirects to a documentation sign-up gate and states that Clearpath Robotics is "by Rockwell Automation" [VERIFIED: https://clearpathrobotics.com/turtlebot-4-documentation/, acc. 2026-10-31]. The involvement of Open Robotics / OSRF in the TurtleBot 4 program is not stated in any of the pinned sources [UNKNOWN: Open Robotics/OSRF role: not found in manual or code; treat as CLAIM from general knowledge, not cited here].

**Two models.** The manual's hardware table gives the differences (Lite vs Standard): size 342 x 339 x 192 mm vs 342 x 339 x 351 mm; weight 3270 g vs 3945 g; same Create 3 base, same Raspberry Pi 4B 4GB, same RPLIDAR A1M8; camera OAK-D-Lite vs OAK-D-Pro; Bluetooth controller "Not Included" vs "TurtleBot 4 Controller"; user power VBAT @1.9A / 5V / 3.3V low current (Lite) vs VBAT @300 mA, 12V @300 mA, 5V @500 mA, 3.3V @250 mA (Standard); LEDs: Create 3 lightring only (Lite) vs lightring + 2 user LEDs + 5 status LEDs; buttons: 2 Create 3 user buttons + power (both) plus 4 user buttons on the Standard; both: 26 Wh Li-ion 14.4 V, dock included, max 0.31 m/s in safe mode / 0.46 m/s without, 1.90 rad/s, 9 kg payload, 2h30-4h runtime [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/overview/features.md#L59-L63, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/overview/features.md#L115, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/overview/features.md#L130, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/overview/features.md#L175, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://turtlebot.github.io/turtlebot4-user-manual/overview/features.html, acc. 2026-10-31]. In software the model is a launch argument (`model` in {standard, lite}) that selects `standard.launch.py` or `lite.launch.py`; only the Standard launches `turtlebot4_base_node` (HMI GPIO) and the OAK-D Pro config, the Lite uses `oakd_lite` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/launch/robot.launch.py#L32, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/launch/robot.launch.py#L69-L72, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/launch/standard.launch.py#L93-L96, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/launch/lite.launch.py#L96, acc. 2026-10-31].

**Price.** [UNKNOWN: No list price is published on clearpathrobotics.com: the TurtleBot 4 product URL 301-redirects to a documentation form page with no pricing (opened 2026-10-31); store.clearpathrobotics.com is a separate host that was not reachable from this sandbox and was not requested. Reseller prices are excluded by the source rules.]

**Availability.** The manual states the Raspberry Pi image ships pre-installed with Ubuntu 24.04 Server, ROS 2 Jazzy and the TurtleBot 4 software, with images downloadable from download.ros.org [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/software/overview.md#L22, acc. 2026-10-31]. Jazzy binaries exist in the ROS 2 Jazzy distribution index for all five repos [VERIFIED: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/ros/rosdistro/master/jazzy/distribution.yaml, acc. 2026-10-31]. Galactic is end-of-life; Humble and Jazzy are supported [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/software/overview.md#L7, acc. 2026-10-31]. Clearpath lists "Global Distributors" and a support ticket channel; stock status is not published [VERIFIED: https://clearpathrobotics.com/turtlebot-4-documentation/, acc. 2026-10-31] [UNKNOWN: lead time / stock].

**Typical users.** Education and research (manual) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/overview/features.md#L14, acc. 2026-10-31]. [UNKNOWN: No user statistics in primary sources.]

**Licences (read from LICENSE files and package.xml).** Every repo carries the Apache License 2.0 text as `LICENSE` and every package.xml declares `<license>Apache 2.0</license>`: turtlebot4 (turtlebot4_description, turtlebot4_msgs, turtlebot4_navigation, turtlebot4_node, all 2.1.1) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/LICENSE, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/package.xml, acc. 2026-10-31]; turtlebot4_robot (turtlebot4_base, turtlebot4_bringup, turtlebot4_diagnostics, turtlebot4_robot, turtlebot4_tests, all 2.0.1) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/LICENSE, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/package.xml, acc. 2026-10-31]; turtlebot4_desktop (turtlebot4_desktop, turtlebot4_viz, 2.0.1) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_desktop/blob/d831a5a3d2323d2db1ce79e5b909beecbeed3dcf/LICENSE, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_desktop/blob/d831a5a3d2323d2db1ce79e5b909beecbeed3dcf/turtlebot4_viz/package.xml, acc. 2026-10-31]; turtlebot4_simulator (turtlebot4_gz_bringup, turtlebot4_gz_gui_plugins, turtlebot4_gz_toolbox, turtlebot4_simulator, 2.0.2) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_simulator/blob/b7d0f3b973258481f6cd8137bef9240fb567f566/LICENSE, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_simulator/blob/b7d0f3b973258481f6cd8137bef9240fb567f566/turtlebot4_gz_bringup/package.xml#L10, acc. 2026-10-31]; turtlebot4_setup (2.0.3) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_setup/blob/5fe58657aeff57d28cb2cfb4f0c6cc6c62a4b96c/LICENSE, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_setup/blob/5fe58657aeff57d28cb2cfb4f0c6cc6c62a4b96c/package.xml, acc. 2026-10-31]; the manual repo itself has no LICENSE file in the pinned tree [UNKNOWN: manual text licence]. Dependencies with other licences (Create 3 firmware is closed; create3_examples/create3_sim/irobot_create_msgs are BSD-3 per their LICENSE files — Create 3 track) are outside this track.

### 1. Platform (base part)  _[Create 3 base track]_

- Maker and lineage: the Create 3 is made by iRobot Corporation and is based on the Roomba i3 vacuum robot [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/faq/faq.md#L6; https://iroboteducation.github.io/create3_docs/faq/faq/, acc. 2026-10-31]; released 2022 [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/faq/faq.md#L12, acc. 2026-10-31]. TurtleBot 4 is "a derivative product of iRobot's Create 3 robot made by Clearpath Robotics, by Rockwell Automation" [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/faq/faq.md#L253; https://iroboteducation.github.io/create3_docs/faq/faq/, acc. 2026-10-31].
- Purpose: "a STEM resource for educators, students, and developers ... intended for college and professional learners" [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/faq/faq.md#L17, acc. 2026-10-31].
- Price: [UNKNOWN: the official product page edu.irobot.com was granted network access but returned HTTP 502 through the sandbox proxy on 2026-10-31; no price appears in create3_docs or the Product Information Guide].
- Licences: `irobot_create_msgs` declares `<license>BSD</license>` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/irobot_create_msgs/blob/b4986ef5dc2e07d8aa7471f29c56b617473352c5/package.xml#L8, acc. 2026-10-31] and its LICENSE file is a BSD 3-clause text ("Redistribution and use in source and binary forms ... 1. Redistributions of source code must retain...") [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/irobot_create_msgs/blob/b4986ef5dc2e07d8aa7471f29c56b617473352c5/LICENSE, acc. 2026-10-31]; create3_sim packages declare BSD [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_sim/blob/6c305843afcfe094bf8eb2a0c6997f531e526b9d/irobot_create_common/irobot_create_nodes/package.xml, acc. 2026-10-31]. The robot firmware is distributed only as `.swu` binary images from the release pages [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/releases/i_0_0.md#L2; https://iroboteducation.github.io/create3_docs/releases/i_0_0/, acc. 2026-10-31]; no firmware source repository is referenced anywhere in create3_docs — closed firmware [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/releases/overview.md#L19; https://iroboteducation.github.io/create3_docs/releases/overview/, acc. 2026-10-31].
- Availability/warranty: iRobot warrants only manufacturing defects "when first encountering this product upon delivery" because most users alter or dismantle it [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/faq/faq.md#L267, acc. 2026-10-31]; the Product Information Guide (regulatory model STEMB-Y1 — model number read from the PDF text by pypdf extraction, not on the rendered page) is the only official compliance text [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/faq/data/Create-3_Product-Information-Guide.pdf; https://iroboteducation.github.io/create3_docs/faq/faq/#notices-and-policies, acc. 2026-10-31]. Commercial availability status: [UNKNOWN: not stated in the pinned docs].

## 2. Hardware: DOF, actuators, sensors, compute, power, safety hardware

_Lead note._ The base track gives the full sensor list and the safety-hardware finding (no E-stop circuit documented anywhere, including the Product Information Guide); the stack track adds the Pi, lidar, camera, UI board and power budget and confirms that the TB4 bill of hardware has no E-stop device either. One documentation conflict is left open: the power-button hold for power-off/storage is 7 s in three iRobot sources and 10 s in one.

### 2. Hardware, sensors, power, safety hardware (full)  _[Create 3 base track]_

**Kinematics and actuators.** "The Create 3 is a differential drive robot designed for traversing relatively-flat, indoor environments. The drive wheels feature independent suspensions" [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/hw/mechanical.md#L3; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/hw/mechanical.md#L4; https://iroboteducation.github.io/create3_docs/hw/mechanical/, acc. 2026-10-31]; "an integrated front caster to act as a third point of contact" with the centre of gravity in front of the drive wheels [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/hw/mechanical.md#L56, acc. 2026-10-31]; wheel travel ≈ 30 mm [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/hw/mechanical.md#L112, acc. 2026-10-31]. Two DOF of motion (v, ω); the only other actuators are the six-RGB-LED light ring and a speaker [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/hw/overview.md#L9; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/ui.md#L39; https://iroboteducation.github.io/create3_docs/hw/overview/, acc. 2026-10-31]. Maximum recommended payload 9 kg without changing acceleration limits [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/hw/mechanical.md#L9, acc. 2026-10-31]. Simulator kinematic constants (not firmware): wheel_separation 0.233 m, wheel_radius 0.03575 m [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_sim/blob/6c305843afcfe094bf8eb2a0c6997f531e526b9d/irobot_create_common/irobot_create_control/config/control.yaml#L18; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_sim/blob/6c305843afcfe094bf8eb2a0c6997f531e526b9d/irobot_create_common/irobot_create_control/config/control.yaml#L19, acc. 2026-10-31]; 508.8 encoder ticks per wheel rotation [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/odometry.md#L36; https://iroboteducation.github.io/create3_docs/api/odometry/, acc. 2026-10-31].

| Optical mouse (floor optical flow) | 1 | `/mouse` | [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/hw/overview.md#L20; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/odometry.md#L24, acc. 2026-10-31] |

**Compute on the base.** The base runs its own ROS 2 application (nodes `motion_control`, `robot_state`, `static_transform`, `system_health`, `ui_mgr`) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/ros2.md#L117; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/ros2.md#L137; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/ros2.md#L144; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/ros2.md#L152; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/ros2.md#L159; https://iroboteducation.github.io/create3_docs/api/ros2/, acc. 2026-10-31] and a separate configuration webserver that "is running regardless of the status of that application" [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/webserver/overview.md#L7; https://iroboteducation.github.io/create3_docs/webserver/overview/, acc. 2026-10-31]. The release notes mention a "mobility board" and a "nav board" as internal processors [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/releases/g_3_1.md#L33, acc. 2026-10-31]; the processor types are [UNKNOWN: not documented]. The About page shows RAM and swap [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/webserver/about.md#L28, acc. 2026-10-31]; absolute values [UNKNOWN].

**Battery and power.** 26 Wh, 4S Li-ion smart pack, 14.4 V nominal (12.0 V min, 16.8 V max); 0 % reported at 12.0 V; self-protect disconnect at ≤ 10.8 V [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/hw/electrical.md#L5; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/hw/electrical.md#L6; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/hw/electrical.md#L7; https://iroboteducation.github.io/create3_docs/hw/electrical/, acc. 2026-10-31]. Charging only on the Home Base dock; the battery disables charging after 4 h continuous charging without reaching 100 % [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/hw/electrical.md#L24; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/hw/electrical.md#L26, acc. 2026-10-31]. Light ring flashes red below ≈ 10 % [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/hw/electrical.md#L22, acc. 2026-10-31]; since G.5.4 / H.2.4 the firmware "will internally call the `/robot_power` service once the battery's state of charge dips below 2 %" [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/hw/electrical.md#L15; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/releases/h_2_4.md#L23, acc. 2026-10-31]. Adapter board: unregulated battery port max 2 A (PTC fuse) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/hw/adapter.md#L13; https://iroboteducation.github.io/create3_docs/hw/adapter/, acc. 2026-10-31]; USB-C host port 5.13 V at 3.0 A, powered only when a proper downstream device is attached, data only when the USB/BLE toggle is at USB [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/hw/adapter.md#L19; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/hw/adapter.md#L20; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/hw/adapter.md#L22, acc. 2026-10-31]; total ≈ 28.8 W shared [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/hw/adapter.md#L26, acc. 2026-10-31]. On TurtleBot 4 the Create 3 adapter powers the Raspberry Pi 4 over USB-C (up to 3 A at 5 V) and the UI board over the 2 A-fused VBAT connector J7 [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/electrical/create3.md#L7; https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/electrical/create3.md#L15; https://turtlebot.github.io/turtlebot4-user-manual/electrical/create3.html, acc. 2026-10-31].

**Safety hardware — E-stop.** There is no hardware emergency-stop circuit documented anywhere in create3_docs or the Product Information Guide (searched for "emergency", "e-stop", "stop": no hits in the English PIG text) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/faq/data/Create-3_Product-Information-Guide.pdf, acc. 2026-10-31]. The only E-stop is a ROS 2 service: "The robot exposes a service with name `e_stop` that when sent `e_stop_on` true will turn off the robot's wheels. The robot will not respond to velocity commands when `e_stop_on` is true" [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/safety.md#L79; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/safety.md#L80; https://iroboteducation.github.io/create3_docs/api/safety/#e-stop, acc. 2026-10-31]; its effect is visible as `wheel_status.wheels_enabled` ("disabled when E-Stopped") [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/irobot_create_msgs/blob/b4986ef5dc2e07d8aa7471f29c56b617473352c5/msg/WheelStatus.msg#L8, acc. 2026-10-31] and as a half-yellow light ring ("Wheels disabled") [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/hw/face.md#L69; https://iroboteducation.github.io/create3_docs/hw/face/, acc. 2026-10-31]. On TurtleBot 4, the Create 3 power button short press is mapped to this software E-stop and a 3000 ms press to `/robot_power` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/config/turtlebot4.yaml#L43, acc. 2026-10-31]; `turtlebot4_node` toggles the service with `e_stop_on = wheels_enabled_` taken from `/wheel_status` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/src/turtlebot4.cpp#L523; https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/src/turtlebot4.cpp#L406, acc. 2026-10-31]. Conclusion: TurtleBot 4 has no hardware E-stop; the "EStop" is a firmware wheel-disable reached through DDS (VERIFIED by the above; the lead's prior belief is confirmed).

**Protective stops and limits.** (a) Backup limit: "the robot's default policy is to never move backward further than what would be safe should there be a cliff directly behind the robot"; after warnings (BACKUP_LIMIT hazard, log warning, orange lights) "the robot will suddenly brake and come to a stop. From this point, the robot will refuse any backward movement" until driven forward [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/safety.md#L37; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/safety.md#L44; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/safety.md#L44-L45; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/safety.md#L46; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/safety.md#L48; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/safety.md#L49; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/safety.md#L50, acc. 2026-10-31]; kidnap triggers it immediately [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/safety.md#L40, acc. 2026-10-31]; active only with `safety_override = none` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/safety.md#L55, acc. 2026-10-31]. (b) Speed limit 0.306 m/s unless `safety_override = full` (0.460 m/s) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/safety.md#L67; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/safety.md#L69, acc. 2026-10-31]. (c) Acceleration ramp `wheel_accel_limit`, max and default 900 mm/s² [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/safety.md#L75; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/ros2.md#L173, acc. 2026-10-31]. (d) Drive-goal cancellation: "A cliff event or a wheel stall will trigger a goal to cancel ... If there is something blocking the robot's path, the user must intervene to stop the robot, otherwise it will continue to bump until odometry slip achieves the goal position" [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/drive-goals.md#L15; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/drive-goals.md#L17; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/drive-goals.md#L18; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/drive-goals.md#L19; https://iroboteducation.github.io/create3_docs/api/drive-goals/, acc. 2026-10-31]. (e) Reflexes (Section 3).

**Wheel-drop and kidnap handling.** Wheel-drop reflex: "It will trigger as soon as the robot detects that one of its wheels is fully extended (dropped). The robot will drive the other wheel in order to return to a flat surface" [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/reflexes.md#L82; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/reflexes.md#L83; https://iroboteducation.github.io/create3_docs/api/reflexes/, acc. 2026-10-31]. Kidnap = "manually lifting the robot and, eventually, placing it back" [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/hazards.md#L87-L89, acc. 2026-10-31]. The TurtleBot 4 manual adds that motors are disabled while kidnapped and re-enable on the ground [CLAIM: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/software/sensors.md#L141; https://turtlebot.github.io/turtlebot4-user-manual/software/sensors.html, acc. 2026-10-31] — a Clearpath statement about iRobot firmware not found in create3_docs, so CLAIM.

**Power-button and button behaviours (firmware level).** Button 1 held 10 s → "standby" (charging circuitry active, payload still powered; wake with centre button 1 s) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/hw/face.md#L10; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/hw/face.md#L12, acc. 2026-10-31]; in the FAQ this is "LOW POWER MODE ... will not respond to Wi-Fi, USB or Bluetooth" [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/faq/faq.md#L87; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/faq/faq.md#L88, acc. 2026-10-31]. Centre button held 7 s → "storage mode", battery disconnected from robot and payload; only the dock powers it on again [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/hw/face.md#L22; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/hw/face.md#L23, acc. 2026-10-31]; the FAQ calls the same 7 s hold "OFF" with three white flashes and a jingle [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/faq/faq.md#L84; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/faq/faq.md#L85, acc. 2026-10-31]. Documentation conflict: electrical.md says "power down the robot by holding down the power button for 10 seconds" [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/hw/electrical.md#L33, acc. 2026-10-31] and the Product Information Guide labels the 7 s centre-button hold "STANDBY/SLEEP MODE" [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/faq/data/Create-3_Product-Information-Guide.pdf, acc. 2026-10-31]; the hold durations are therefore 7 s (three sources) vs 10 s (one source) — [UNKNOWN: which is correct on I.0.0]. Buttons 1 & 2 held 3 s → Wi-Fi access-point mode [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/setup/provision.md#L8; https://iroboteducation.github.io/create3_docs/setup/provision/, acc. 2026-10-31]. In BLE mode "Pressing the power button stops all actuators" [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/releases/g_2_2.md#L44, acc. 2026-10-31]; no equivalent statement exists for ROS 2 mode ([UNKNOWN]).

### 2. Hardware additions above the Create 3 (TB4 layer)  _[TB4 stack track]_

- **Compute:** Raspberry Pi 4B 4GB on both models [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/overview/features.md#L85, acc. 2026-10-31]; it runs the TB4 ROS nodes, sensor drivers, Bluetooth for the controller, GPIO, and the Wi-Fi link [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/software/overview.md#L22, acc. 2026-10-31].
- **Lidar:** RPLIDAR A1M8, 360 degree, 12 m range (manual figure; the Slamtec datasheet was not opened) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/overview/features.md#L208, acc. 2026-10-31]; launched as `rplidar_ros/rplidar_composition` on serial port `/dev/RPLIDAR`, frame `rplidar_link` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/launch/rplidar.launch.py#L25, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/launch/rplidar.launch.py#L29, acc. 2026-10-31]; udev rule file `udev/50-turtlebot4.rules` in turtlebot4_setup creates the symlink [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_setup/blob/5fe58657aeff57d28cb2cfb4f0c6cc6c62a4b96c/udev/50-turtlebot4.rules, acc. 2026-10-31].
- **Camera:** OAK-D-Pro (Standard) / OAK-D-Lite (Lite) from Luxonis, 4K IMX214 colour + stereo pair, Myriad X VPU; Pro adds OV9282 stereo, IR dot projector and IR LED [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/overview/features.md#L218, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/overview/features.md#L227, acc. 2026-10-31]. Launched as `depthai_ros_driver::Camera` component named `oakd` in container `oakd_container`, 30 s after the rest of the stack to avoid a USB current spike [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/launch/oakd.launch.py#L58, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/launch/standard.launch.py#L88, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/launch/standard.launch.py#L91, acc. 2026-10-31].
- **UI board (Standard only):** 4 user buttons, 128x64 user display, status and user LEDs, 4 USB-C ports [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/overview/features.md#L24, acc. 2026-10-31]; GPIO numbers in `turtlebot4.yaml`: buttons 19/16/26/20; LEDs power 17, motors 18, comms 27, wifi 24, battery 22/23, user1 25, user2 6/21; display reset 13 [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/config/turtlebot4.yaml#L76, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/config/turtlebot4.yaml#L91, acc. 2026-10-31]. Conflict: the manual's turtlebot4_robot page lists `led_red_user_2: 12` and `display_reset: 2` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/software/turtlebot4_robot.md#L202, acc. 2026-10-31]; the code default is trusted.
- **Power:** the UI board connects to the Create 3 only through the VBAT connector J7 (JST XH), fused at 2 A PTC; the Create 3 adapter board powers the Pi over USB-C (USB 2.0 data) at up to 3 A @ 5 V [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/electrical/create3.md#L7, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/electrical/create3.md#L15, acc. 2026-10-31]; total adapter budget 28.8 W [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/electrical/power_budget.md#L7, acc. 2026-10-31]; user power ports 3.3 V, 5 V, 12 V, VBAT (14.4 V) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/electrical/pcba.md#L328, acc. 2026-10-31]; ratings per model as in Section 1.
- **E-stop:** there is **no hardware emergency-stop device** in the TB4 bill of hardware: the manual's "Buttons and Switches" row lists only Create 3 user buttons x2, Create 3 power button, and (Standard) 4 user buttons [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/overview/features.md#L175, acc. 2026-10-31]; every "EStop" in the manual and code is the Create 3 `e_stop` ROS service ("cutting motor power when on") [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/software/create3.md#L154, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/src/turtlebot4.cpp#L164, acc. 2026-10-31]. The term "safe mode" (0.31 m/s cap) is a Create 3 firmware setting (Create 3 track) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/overview/features.md#L91, acc. 2026-10-31].

## 3. Software architecture: control loop and rates; layers from task to motor command; where AI and human commands enter and what arbitrates

_Lead note._ Read the three blocks as one chain: Nav2 (task → `/cmd_vel`), the TB4 layer (node graph, republisher, remaps, human entry points, **absence of any mux**), and the firmware (last-message-wins with a 0.5 s timeout, actions pre-empting `/cmd_vel`, reflexes overriding everything). The two stated open questions between tracks are resolved as follows: the Create 3 `cmd_vel` consumption behaviour the stack track deferred is documented by the base track (last message executed, `wheels_stop_threshold_sec` 0.5 s; publication rate expected ≥ 2 Hz is an inference from the timeout, the docs' example uses 20 Hz); `route_server` behaviour without parameters remains UNKNOWN for both.

### 3. Software architecture and control chain  _[TB4 stack track]_

#### 3.1 Node graph launched on the Pi

`standard.launch.py` / `lite.launch.py` wrap everything in a `GroupAction` with `PushRosNamespace(ROBOT_NAMESPACE)` (env var, default empty) and include: `robot.launch.py`, `joy_teleop.launch.py`, `rplidar.launch.py`, a 30 s-delayed `oakd.launch.py`, `robot_description.launch.py`, and (if `TURTLEBOT4_DIAGNOSTICS` == "1", the default) `diagnostics.launch.py` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/launch/standard.launch.py#L35, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/launch/standard.launch.py#L36, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/launch/standard.launch.py#L69-L72, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/launch/standard.launch.py#L107, acc. 2026-10-31]. Resulting nodes (names from code):

| Node | Package / executable | Only on | Role | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `turtlebot4_node` | turtlebot4_node/turtlebot4_node | both | buttons, display menu, LEDs, functions (dock, undock, wall follow, e-stop, power, sensor on/off) | [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/launch/robot.launch.py#L60, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/src/turtlebot4.cpp#L49, acc. 2026-10-31] |
| `turtlebot4_base_node` | turtlebot4_base/turtlebot4_base_node | standard | GPIO buttons -> `hmi/buttons`, I2C display, LEDs | [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/launch/robot.launch.py#L67, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_base/src/turtlebot4.cpp#L33, acc. 2026-10-31] |
| `create3_repub` | create3_republisher/create3_republisher (`respawn=True`) | both | relays Create 3 topics/services/actions from `<ns>/_do_not_use` to `<ns>` | [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/launch/robot.launch.py#L76, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/launch/robot.launch.py#L82, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/config/republisher.yaml#L2, acc. 2026-10-31] |
| `joy_linux_node` | joy_linux/joy_linux_node (`dev:=/dev/input/js0`) | both | Bluetooth controller -> `joy` | [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/launch/joy_teleop.launch.py#L29, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/launch/joy_teleop.launch.py#L53, acc. 2026-10-31] |
| `teleop_twist_joy_node` | teleop_twist_joy/teleop_node (`publish_stamped_twist: true`) | both | `joy` -> `cmd_vel` TwistStamped | [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/launch/joy_teleop.launch.py#L63, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/launch/joy_teleop.launch.py#L68, acc. 2026-10-31] |
| `rplidar_composition` | rplidar_ros | both | `scan` | [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/launch/rplidar.launch.py#L24, acc. 2026-10-31] |
| `oakd` in `oakd_container` | depthai_ros_driver | both (pro/lite config) | images, depth | [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/launch/oakd.launch.py#L51-L52, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/launch/oakd.launch.py#L60, acc. 2026-10-31] |
| `robot_state_publisher`, `joint_state_publisher` | robot_state_publisher, joint_state_publisher | both | URDF TF | [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_description/launch/robot_description.launch.py#L51, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_description/launch/robot_description.launch.py#L69, acc. 2026-10-31] |
| `aggregator_node`, `turtlebot4_diagnostics` | diagnostic_aggregator, turtlebot4_diagnostics | both (if enabled) | `/diagnostics_agg` | [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_diagnostics/launch/diagnostics.launch.py#L41, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_diagnostics/launch/diagnostics.launch.py#L48-L51, acc. 2026-10-31] |

The manual calls the controller nodes "joy_teleop nodes"; the code launches `joy_linux` + `teleop_twist_joy` (naming conflict only; code trusted) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/tutorials/driving.md#L85, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/launch/joy_teleop.launch.py#L53, acc. 2026-10-31].

**Why the republisher exists.** The Create 3 runs its own ROS 2 application and "has limited resources and can be overwhelmed with too many topics to discover"; the Create 3 is therefore placed in the namespace `<ROBOT_NAMESPACE>/_do_not_use`, talks only to the Pi over the USB-C Ethernet link, and `create3_republisher` re-exposes a configured subset in the robot namespace [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/software/create3.md#L189, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/software/create3.md#L191, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://turtlebot.github.io/turtlebot4-user-manual/software/create3.html#create-3-republisher, acc. 2026-10-31]. `robot.launch.py` sets `robot_namespace: '_do_not_use'` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/launch/robot.launch.py#L39, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/launch/robot.launch.py#L80, acc. 2026-10-31]. The default relay list (republisher.yaml) publishes battery_state, dock_status, imu, interface_buttons, odom, tf, tf_static, wheel_status; subscribes cmd_audio, cmd_lightring, cmd_vel (Twist), cmd_vel_stamped (TwistStamped); relays services e_stop, reset_pose, robot_power and actions audio_note_sequence, dock, drive_arc, drive_distance, led_animation, navigate_to_position, rotate_angle, undock, wall_follow; **hazard_detection, kidnap_status, stop_status, cliff_intensity, ir_intensity, ir_opcode, mouse, slip_status, wheel_ticks, wheel_vels are commented out** [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/config/republisher.yaml#L10-L11, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/config/republisher.yaml#L12-L14, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/config/republisher.yaml#L19, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/config/republisher.yaml#L23, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/config/republisher.yaml#L27-L30, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/config/republisher.yaml#L33-L36, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/config/republisher.yaml#L38-L41, acc. 2026-10-31]. Consequence for the Autonomy Meter: firmware safety reflexes are invisible on the robot namespace by default (signal T13).

**Jazzy stamped/unstamped remap.** `robot.launch.py` remaps the republisher's `cmd_vel` -> `cmd_vel_unstamped` and `cmd_vel_stamped` -> `cmd_vel`, so the robot-namespace `/cmd_vel` is `geometry_msgs/msg/TwistStamped` and legacy `Twist` publishers must use `/cmd_vel_unstamped` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/launch/robot.launch.py#L84, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/launch/robot.launch.py#L86, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/launch/robot.launch.py#L87, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/config/republisher.yaml#L33, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/config/republisher.yaml#L34, acc. 2026-10-31]. The manual's Jazzy tab publishes `TwistStamped` on `/cmd_vel` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/tutorials/driving.md#L136, acc. 2026-10-31].

#### 3.2 turtlebot4_node: buttons, menu, functions

`turtlebot4_node` (intra-process comms enabled) declares parameters `model`, `wifi.interface` (default `wlan0`), `power_saver` (default true), one string-array per button, and `menu.entries` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/src/turtlebot4.cpp#L60, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/src/turtlebot4.cpp#L73, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/src/turtlebot4.cpp#L76, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/src/turtlebot4.cpp#L115, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/src/turtlebot4.cpp#L125, acc. 2026-10-31]. It subscribes `battery_state`, `dock_status`, `wheel_status` (SensorDataQoS) and, in `Buttons`, `interface_buttons` (SensorDataQoS), `joy` (QoS 10) and on Standard `hmi/buttons` (SensorDataQoS) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/src/turtlebot4.cpp#L133-L134, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/src/turtlebot4.cpp#L138, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/src/turtlebot4.cpp#L143, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/src/buttons.cpp#L37-L38, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/src/buttons.cpp#L42, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/src/buttons.cpp#L48, acc. 2026-10-31]. It publishes `ip` and `function_calls` (std_msgs/String, KeepLast(10)) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/src/turtlebot4.cpp#L149, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/src/turtlebot4.cpp#L153, acc. 2026-10-31], and on Standard `hmi/display` (UserDisplay) and per-LED topics `hmi/led/_power|_motors|_comms|_wifi|_battery|_user1|_user2` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/src/display.cpp#L47, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/src/leds.cpp#L47, acc. 2026-10-31]. Action clients: `dock`, `undock`, `wall_follow`, `led_animation`; service clients: `e_stop`, `robot_power`, `start_motor`, `stop_motor` (RPLIDAR), `oakd/start_camera`, `oakd/stop_camera` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/src/turtlebot4.cpp#L158, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/src/turtlebot4.cpp#L164, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/src/turtlebot4.cpp#L166-L168, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/src/turtlebot4.cpp#L171-L172, acc. 2026-10-31]. The manual's interface table omits the `dock_status` subscription and the OAK-D start/stop clients [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/software/turtlebot4_common.md#L583, acc. 2026-10-31]; code trusted.

Function table (`function_callbacks_`): Dock, Undock, Wall Follow Left, Wall Follow Right, EStop, Power, RPLIDAR Start/Stop, OAKD Start/Stop, Scroll Up/Down, Select, Back, Help [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/src/turtlebot4.cpp#L179, acc. 2026-10-31]. Semantics: Dock/Undock send an empty goal [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/src/turtlebot4.cpp#L455, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/src/turtlebot4.cpp#L468, acc. 2026-10-31]; Wall Follow sends `follow_side` LEFT/RIGHT with `max_runtime` 10 s and is refused while docked [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/src/turtlebot4.cpp#L479, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/src/turtlebot4.cpp#L483-L484, acc. 2026-10-31]; **EStop toggles**: the request sets `e_stop_on = wheels_enabled_` (current wheel state from `wheel_status`), so the same button sets or clears the stop [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/src/turtlebot4.cpp#L523, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/src/turtlebot4.cpp#L406, acc. 2026-10-31]; Power calls `robot_power` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/src/turtlebot4.cpp#L547, acc. 2026-10-31]. Every function call first publishes its name on `function_calls` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/include/turtlebot4_node/buttons.hpp#L165, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/src/turtlebot4.cpp#L662-L665, acc. 2026-10-31]. Automatic behaviours: at battery <= 12 % undocked, a 60 s timer powers the robot off (cancelled if docked) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/src/turtlebot4.cpp#L351-L354, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/src/turtlebot4.cpp#L354-L357, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/include/turtlebot4_node/turtlebot4.hpp#L68, acc. 2026-10-31]; with `power_saver` the OAK-D and RPLIDAR are stopped on dock and restarted on undock [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/src/turtlebot4.cpp#L390, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/src/turtlebot4.cpp#L390-L393, acc. 2026-10-31].

Default button map (`turtlebot4.yaml`, format `[SHORT, LONG, LONG_MS]`): `create3_1: [Dock, Wall Follow Left, 2000]`, `create3_power: [EStop, Power, 3000]`, `create3_2: [Undock, Wall Follow Right, 2000]`, `hmi_1..4: Select, Back, Scroll Up, Scroll Down`; menu entries `Dock, Undock, EStop, Wall Follow Left, Wall Follow Right, Power, Help`; controller: `a: Select`, `b: EStop`, `x: Back`, `up/down: Scroll`, `l2: Wall Follow Left`, `r2: Wall Follow Right`, `home: [Dock, Undock, 3000]` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/config/turtlebot4.yaml#L39-L41, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/config/turtlebot4.yaml#L53, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/config/turtlebot4.yaml#L60-L62, acc. 2026-10-31]. Press logic: a button with a long-press duration fires SHORT on release before the duration and LONG once the duration elapses while held; without a long function SHORT fires immediately [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/include/turtlebot4_node/buttons.hpp#L125, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/include/turtlebot4_node/buttons.hpp#L141, acc. 2026-10-31]. Controller button indices read from `joy`: A=0, B=1, Y=2, X=3, L1=4, R1=5, L2=6, R2=7, Share=8, Options=9, Home=10, L3=11, R3=12; D-pad from axes 6/7 [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/src/buttons.cpp#L91, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/src/buttons.cpp#L100, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/src/buttons.cpp#L126, acc. 2026-10-31].

Timers: buttons polled every 10 ms, display 50 ms (forced refresh 1 Hz), LEDs 50 ms, Wi-Fi/IP 5 s, comms timeout 30 s (COMMS LED off if no `wheel_status`), power-off 60 s [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/include/turtlebot4_node/turtlebot4.hpp#L64-L66, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/src/turtlebot4.cpp#L249, acc. 2026-10-31]. `turtlebot4_base_node` polls the GPIO buttons every 10 ms and publishes `hmi/buttons` each cycle (100 Hz) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_base/src/turtlebot4.cpp#L62, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_base/src/buttons.cpp#L65, acc. 2026-10-31].

#### 3.3 Control-loop rates (TB4 configuration)

| Loop | Value | Source |
|---|---|---|
| joy_linux autorepeat / coalesce | 20 Hz autorepeat while input held, 1 ms coalesce (package defaults; TB4 does not override) | [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-drivers/joystick_drivers/blob/038a12722bac3983d7fee462ad074a8f18c7bd5f/joy_linux/src/joy_linux_node.cpp#L215, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-drivers/joystick_drivers/blob/038a12722bac3983d7fee462ad074a8f18c7bd5f/joy_linux/src/joy_linux_node.cpp#L216, acc. 2026-10-31] |
| teleop_twist_joy | one `cmd_vel` per `joy` message while enable held; one zero message on release | [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros2/teleop_twist_joy/blob/556a07100ee745f68619de84a95d3c84a4892ab9/src/teleop_twist_joy.cpp#L330-L333, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros2/teleop_twist_joy/blob/556a07100ee745f68619de84a95d3c84a4892ab9/src/teleop_twist_joy.cpp#L341-L344, acc. 2026-10-31] |
| Nav2 controller_server | `controller_frequency: 20.0`, MPPI `FollowPath` | [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/config/nav2.yaml#L23, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/config/nav2.yaml#L42, acc. 2026-10-31] |
| Nav2 velocity_smoother | `smoothing_frequency: 20.0`, `velocity_timeout: 1.0`, max 0.26 m/s, 1.0 rad/s | [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/config/nav2.yaml#L282, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/config/nav2.yaml#L285, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/config/nav2.yaml#L292, acc. 2026-10-31] |
| Nav2 behavior_server | `cycle_frequency: 10.0` | [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/config/nav2.yaml#L246, acc. 2026-10-31] |
| Nav2 bt_navigator | `bt_loop_duration: 10` (ms) | [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/config/nav2.yaml#L7, acc. 2026-10-31] |
| Nav2 docking_server | `controller_frequency: 50.0` | [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/config/nav2.yaml#L329, acc. 2026-10-31] |
| local / global costmap | 5 Hz / 1 Hz update | [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/config/nav2.yaml#L130, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/config/nav2.yaml#L180, acc. 2026-10-31] |
| Create 3 cmd_vel consumption rate / timeout | Create 3 track | [UNKNOWN: not in TB4 sources] |

#### 3.4 Exact topic chain from a goal to the wheels

`turtlebot4_navigation/nav2.launch.py` includes `nav2_bringup/navigation_launch.py` with `use_composition: False` and the TB4 `nav2.yaml` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/launch/nav2.launch.py#L60, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/launch/nav2.launch.py#L73, acc. 2026-10-31]. In that launch file `controller_server` and `behavior_server` are remapped `cmd_vel -> cmd_vel_nav` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_bringup/launch/navigation_launch.py#L137, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_bringup/launch/navigation_launch.py#L181, acc. 2026-10-31]; `velocity_smoother` subscribes `cmd_vel` remapped to `cmd_vel_nav` and publishes `cmd_vel_smoothed` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_bringup/launch/navigation_launch.py#L215, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_velocity_smoother/src/velocity_smoother.cpp#L155, acc. 2026-10-31]; TB4 `nav2.yaml` sets `collision_monitor.cmd_vel_in_topic: "cmd_vel_smoothed"`, `cmd_vel_out_topic: "cmd_vel"`, `state_topic: "collision_monitor_state"` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/config/nav2.yaml#L299, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/config/nav2.yaml#L300, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/config/nav2.yaml#L301, acc. 2026-10-31]; `enable_stamped_cmd_vel: true` is set for every server [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/config/nav2.yaml#L3, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/config/nav2.yaml#L22, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/config/nav2.yaml#L281, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/config/nav2.yaml#L296, acc. 2026-10-31]. Chain:

`NavigateToPose goal (RViz GoalTool / TurtleBot4Navigator)` -> `bt_navigator` -> `controller_server` -> **`/cmd_vel_nav`** (TwistStamped, 20 Hz) -> `velocity_smoother` -> **`/cmd_vel_smoothed`** -> `collision_monitor` -> **`/cmd_vel`** -> `create3_repub` (subscribed as `cmd_vel_stamped`, remapped) -> **`/_do_not_use/cmd_vel_stamped`** -> Create 3 firmware -> wheels. The lifecycle manager list also contains `route_server` in this nav2_bringup version [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_bringup/launch/navigation_launch.py#L47, acc. 2026-10-31]; TB4 `nav2.yaml` has no `route_server` block [UNKNOWN: whether route_server activates without parameters — Nav2 track].

#### 3.5 Where human commands enter, and what arbitrates

Human entry points: `teleop_twist_keyboard` publishes `cmd_vel` (TwistStamped when `stamped:=true`, as the manual instructs for Jazzy) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros2/teleop_twist_keyboard/blob/ed06da483e4acef8d0ab225557d64e6597c9bdf2/teleop_twist_keyboard.py#L143, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros2/teleop_twist_keyboard/blob/ed06da483e4acef8d0ab225557d64e6597c9bdf2/teleop_twist_keyboard.py#L156, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/tutorials/driving.md#L57, acc. 2026-10-31]; `teleop_twist_joy_node` publishes `cmd_vel` on the Pi [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros2/teleop_twist_joy/blob/556a07100ee745f68619de84a95d3c84a4892ab9/src/teleop_twist_joy.cpp#L98, acc. 2026-10-31]; `ros2 topic pub /cmd_vel` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/tutorials/driving.md#L136, acc. 2026-10-31]; RViz `initialpose`, `clicked_point`, GoalTool [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_desktop/blob/d831a5a3d2323d2db1ce79e5b909beecbeed3dcf/turtlebot4_viz/rviz/navigation.rviz#L732, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_desktop/blob/d831a5a3d2323d2db1ce79e5b909beecbeed3dcf/turtlebot4_viz/rviz/navigation.rviz#L740, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_desktop/blob/d831a5a3d2323d2db1ce79e5b909beecbeed3dcf/turtlebot4_viz/rviz/navigation.rviz#L741, acc. 2026-10-31]; display menu and Create 3 buttons via `turtlebot4_node` functions (Section 3.2); Create 3 actions `drive_distance`, `rotate_angle`, `drive_arc` callable from the CLI [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/tutorials/driving.md#L159, acc. 2026-10-31].

**Arbitration: none in software.** A grep of all five TB4 repos at the pinned commits for `twist_mux`, `cmd_vel_mux`, `mux`, `priority` finds no multiplexer or priority node; the only hit is `use_realtime_priority: false` in nav2.yaml [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/config/nav2.yaml#L31, acc. 2026-10-31] (grep executed 2026-10-31 over the extracted trees; reproducible with `grep -rn "mux\|priority"`). No launch file in turtlebot4_bringup, turtlebot4_navigation or turtlebot4_gz_bringup declares a mux node (file list: robot/standard/lite/joy_teleop/oakd/rplidar; nav2/slam/localization; sim/spawn/nodes/bridge) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/launch/standard.launch.py#L69-L72, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/launch/nav2.launch.py#L63, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_simulator/blob/b7d0f3b973258481f6cd8137bef9240fb567f566/turtlebot4_gz_bringup/launch/turtlebot4_spawn.launch.py#L127, acc. 2026-10-31]. `/cmd_vel` is a plain topic with N publishers and one effective subscriber (the republisher, then the firmware): the firmware receives every message from every publisher, interleaved in arrival order. Consequence: a human can publish `/cmd_vel` while Nav2 is active and the robot follows whichever message arrived last; Nav2 keeps publishing at 20 Hz so the human input is overridden within <= 50 ms unless the human also publishes at a higher rate (joy autorepeat is 20 Hz). The manual states both keyboard and joystick "work by sending velocity commands to the robot through the `/cmd_vel` topic" with no mention of priority [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/tutorials/driving.md#L99, acc. 2026-10-31]. Whether the Create 3 firmware itself arbitrates between stamped and unstamped inputs or applies a timeout is a Create 3 track question [UNKNOWN: firmware-side arbitration].

### 3. Control arbitration in the firmware (base part)  _[Create 3 base track]_

**cmd_vel semantics.** The robot is driven by `geometry_msgs/msg/Twist` on `/cmd_vel` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/moving-the-robot.md#L19; https://iroboteducation.github.io/create3_docs/api/moving-the-robot/, acc. 2026-10-31]; since I.0.0 also by `geometry_msgs/msg/TwistStamped` on `/cmd_vel_stamped` — "You may also continue to use the `cmd_vel` topic; don't use both at the same time" [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/releases/i_0_0.md#L25; https://iroboteducation.github.io/create3_docs/releases/i_0_0/, acc. 2026-10-31].
