"The robot will execute the last received message on the topic until a fixed timeout period has passed or a new message is received, at which point the timer is restarted" [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/moving-the-robot.md#L26, acc. 2026-10-31]. The timeout is the `/motion_control` parameter `wheels_stop_threshold_sec`: "the amount of time that the robot will keep executing the last provided velocity command (on the `/cmd_vel` topic) before considering it stale and stopping the wheels. Default value is 0.5 seconds" (added H.2.5) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/releases/h_2_5.md#L29; https://iroboteducation.github.io/create3_docs/releases/h_2_5/, acc. 2026-10-31]. Note the docs page still carries the parameter text inside an HTML comment marked "NOT YET AVAILABLE" [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/moving-the-robot.md#L30; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/moving-the-robot.md#L30-L31, acc. 2026-10-31] and the parameter is absent from the `ros2 param list` dump in ros2.md [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/ros2.md#L117, acc. 2026-10-31] — docs are stale relative to the release note; the release note is the later text. The simulator implements the same constant `wheels_stop_threshold_(0.5s)` and a 40 Hz control loop that zeroes the command when `time_diff > wheels_stop_threshold_` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_sim/blob/6c305843afcfe094bf8eb2a0c6997f531e526b9d/irobot_create_common/irobot_create_nodes/src/motion_control_node.cpp#L22; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_sim/blob/6c305843afcfe094bf8eb2a0c6997f531e526b9d/irobot_create_common/irobot_create_nodes/src/motion_control_node.cpp#L147; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_sim/blob/6c305843afcfe094bf8eb2a0c6997f531e526b9d/irobot_create_common/irobot_create_nodes/src/motion_control_node.cpp#L252, acc. 2026-10-31]. Publication rate expected from the commander: docs examples use 20 Hz [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/moving-the-robot.md#L23, acc. 2026-10-31]; no minimum is specified. Timeout for `/cmd_vel_stamped`: [UNKNOWN: not documented].

**Actions win over cmd_vel.** "these raw velocity commands have lower 'priority' than the other request-based approaches ... The request of a position control or autonomous behavior goal will block the execution of the `cmd_vel` messages, which will be ignored until the goal terminates" [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/moving-the-robot.md#L48; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/moving-the-robot.md#L49, acc. 2026-10-31]. The simulator drops the message and logs "Ignoring velocities commanded while an autonomous behavior is running!" [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_sim/blob/6c305843afcfe094bf8eb2a0c6997f531e526b9d/irobot_create_common/irobot_create_nodes/src/motion_control_node.cpp#L339-L345; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_sim/blob/6c305843afcfe094bf8eb2a0c6997f531e526b9d/irobot_create_common/irobot_create_nodes/src/motion_control_node.cpp#L345, acc. 2026-10-31] — the real firmware's log text is [UNKNOWN: not published]. Whether two action goals can be active at once, and which action wins: [UNKNOWN: not documented; the simulator has a single BehaviorsScheduler].

**Reflexes win over everything.** "Reflexes are high-priority behaviors and will temporarily override any user-provided command for their short duration" [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/reflexes.md#L5; https://iroboteducation.github.io/create3_docs/api/reflexes/, acc. 2026-10-31]. `reflexes_enabled` (bool) default true; when false no reflex runs regardless of individual settings [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/reflexes.md#L11; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/reflexes.md#L12; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/reflexes.md#L14, acc. 2026-10-31]. Individual `reflexes.REFLEX_*` parameters (all under `/motion_control`): BUMP (moves away after a bump, enabled) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/reflexes.md#L18; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/reflexes.md#L22, acc. 2026-10-31]; CLIFF (enabled) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/reflexes.md#L26; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/reflexes.md#L30, acc. 2026-10-31]; DOCK_AVOID ("will stop forward movements" near the dock; **disabled by default**) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/reflexes.md#L34; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/reflexes.md#L36; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/reflexes.md#L36-L38, acc. 2026-10-31]; GYRO_CAL (recalibrates while stationary, enabled) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/reflexes.md#L42; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/reflexes.md#L42-L45, acc. 2026-10-31]; PANIC (more aggressive manoeuvres when trapped, enabled) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/reflexes.md#L49; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/reflexes.md#L53, acc. 2026-10-31]; PROXIMITY_SLOWDOWN (reduces speed near obstacles, enabled) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/reflexes.md#L57; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/reflexes.md#L57-L59; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/reflexes.md#L61, acc. 2026-10-31]; STUCK (enabled) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/reflexes.md#L65; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/reflexes.md#L69, acc. 2026-10-31]; VIRTUAL_WALL (enabled) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/reflexes.md#L73; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/reflexes.md#L77, acc. 2026-10-31]; WHEEL_DROP (enabled) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/reflexes.md#L81; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/reflexes.md#L85, acc. 2026-10-31]. The proximity-slowdown reflex honours `safety_override` since G.3.1 [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/releases/g_3_1.md#L47, acc. 2026-10-31]. No topic reports reflex activity (none in the topic list) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/ros2.md#L15, acc. 2026-10-31].

**safety_override.** String parameter on `/motion_control`, values `none` (default, all safety on), `backup_only` (backup limit disabled), `full` (all safety off); invalid values rejected [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/safety.md#L12; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/safety.md#L15; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/safety.md#L16; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/safety.md#L17; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/safety.md#L25; https://iroboteducation.github.io/create3_docs/api/safety/, acc. 2026-10-31]. "The robot will temporarily re-enable all safety features whenever it is commanded to execute any of the built-in autonomous behaviors" [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/safety.md#L10, acc. 2026-10-31]. Read-only `max_speed` = 0.306 m/s under `none`/`backup_only`, 0.460 m/s under `full` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/safety.md#L65; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/safety.md#L67; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/safety.md#L69, acc. 2026-10-31]; the simulator hard-codes SAFETY_ON_MAX_SPEED 0.306, SAFETY_OFF_MAX_SPEED 0.46, GYRO_MAX_ROTATE_SPEED_RAD_S 1.9 and a backup buffer that warns at 0.05 m and stops at 0.15 m [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_sim/blob/6c305843afcfe094bf8eb2a0c6997f531e526b9d/irobot_create_common/irobot_create_nodes/include/irobot_create_nodes/motion_control_node.hpp#L140; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_sim/blob/6c305843afcfe094bf8eb2a0c6997f531e526b9d/irobot_create_common/irobot_create_nodes/include/irobot_create_nodes/motion_control_node.hpp#L141; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_sim/blob/6c305843afcfe094bf8eb2a0c6997f531e526b9d/irobot_create_common/irobot_create_nodes/include/irobot_create_nodes/motion_control_node.hpp#L139; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_sim/blob/6c305843afcfe094bf8eb2a0c6997f531e526b9d/irobot_create_common/irobot_create_nodes/include/irobot_create_nodes/motion_control_node.hpp#L153; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_sim/blob/6c305843afcfe094bf8eb2a0c6997f531e526b9d/irobot_create_common/irobot_create_nodes/include/irobot_create_nodes/motion_control_node.hpp#L154, acc. 2026-10-31] — the real firmware's backup distances are [UNKNOWN: not documented]. The webserver parameters file can set a boot default [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/irobot_create_msgs/blob/b4986ef5dc2e07d8aa7471f29c56b617473352c5/msg/HazardDetection.msg#L7; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/webserver/application.md#L32, acc. 2026-10-31]. Motion-control parameter list: `max_speed, min_speed, reflexes.*, reflexes_enabled, safety_override, use_sim_time, wheel_accel_limit` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/ros2.md#L118; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/ros2.md#L136, acc. 2026-10-31] plus `wheels_stop_threshold_sec` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/releases/h_2_5.md#L29, acc. 2026-10-31]; `min_speed` semantics [UNKNOWN: undocumented].

**Several cmd_vel publishers.** The firmware documentation describes a single subscription that executes "the last received message" [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/moving-the-robot.md#L26, acc. 2026-10-31]; there is no arbitration, priority or mux on the base and none in the TurtleBot 4 stack (lead grep: only `use_realtime_priority` in nav2.yaml; the TB4 `create3_republisher` simply forwards `cmd_vel`/`cmd_vel_stamped` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/config/republisher.yaml#L33; https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/config/republisher.yaml#L34, acc. 2026-10-31] with remaps `('cmd_vel','cmd_vel_unstamped'), ('cmd_vel_stamped','cmd_vel')` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/launch/robot.launch.py#L86; https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/launch/robot.launch.py#L87, acc. 2026-10-31]). With two publishers, DDS delivers both streams to the same subscription and the firmware executes whichever arrived last — interleaving at the two publishers' rates. This is a direct consequence of the documented "last received message" rule; the interleaving behaviour itself is not stated by iRobot ([UNKNOWN: exact behaviour with concurrent Twist and TwistStamped streams: 'don't use both at the same time' only]).

### 3. Control layers (Nav2 part): from task to motor command  _[Nav2 track]_

TurtleBot 4 launches Nav2 through `turtlebot4_navigation/launch/nav2.launch.py`, which includes `nav2_bringup/launch/navigation_launch.py` with `use_composition: False`, the TB4 `nav2.yaml` as `params_file`, and `use_sim_time` defaulting to `false` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/launch/nav2.launch.py#L33-L46, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/launch/nav2.launch.py#L60-L77, acc. 2026-10-31]. The Gazebo bring-up includes the same file with `use_sim_time` passed through (default `true` in the simulator) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_simulator/blob/b7d0f3b973258481f6cd8137bef9240fb567f566/turtlebot4_gz_bringup/launch/turtlebot4_spawn.launch.py#L95-L96, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_simulator/blob/b7d0f3b973258481f6cd8137bef9240fb567f566/turtlebot4_gz_bringup/launch/turtlebot4_spawn.launch.py#L260-L268, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_simulator/blob/b7d0f3b973258481f6cd8137bef9240fb567f566/turtlebot4_gz_bringup/launch/turtlebot4_spawn.launch.py#L36-L38, acc. 2026-10-31]. The TB4 `nav2.yaml` does not contain `use_sim_time`; it is injected by the launch file's `SetParameter('use_sim_time', ...)` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_bringup/launch/navigation_launch.py#L125-L128, acc. 2026-10-31].

#### 3.1 Node graph and lifecycle manager

`navigation_launch.py` starts ten lifecycle servers and one lifecycle manager. The manager's `node_names` list is, in order: `controller_server, smoother_server, planner_server, route_server, behavior_server, velocity_smoother, collision_monitor, bt_navigator, waypoint_follower, docking_server` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_bringup/launch/navigation_launch.py#L43-L54, acc. 2026-10-31]; the manager node is `lifecycle_manager_navigation` with `autostart` from the launch argument whose default is `true` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_bringup/launch/navigation_launch.py#L97-L101, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_bringup/launch/navigation_launch.py#L239-L246, acc. 2026-10-31]. Localization is a separate include (`nav2_bringup/launch/localization_launch.py`) whose manager `lifecycle_manager_localization` controls `map_server` and `amcl` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/launch/localization.launch.py#L59-L66, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_bringup/launch/localization_launch.py#L47, acc. 2026-10-31]. SLAM is launched from slam_toolbox's own launch files, not through a Nav2 lifecycle manager unless `use_lifecycle_manager:=true` is passed (default `false`) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/launch/slam.launch.py#L46-L52, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/launch/slam.launch.py#L92-L98, acc. 2026-10-31].

The lifecycle manager declares `autostart` (default `false`, overridden to `true` by the launch), `bond_timeout` 4.0 s, `bond_respawn_max_duration` 10.0 s and `attempt_respawn_reconnection` true [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_lifecycle_manager/src/lifecycle_manager.cpp#L42-L46, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://docs.nav2.org/rolling/configuration_and_development/configuration_guide/core_servers/configuring_lifecycle_manager/, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/docs.nav2.org/blob/74f751f161154075af6c2b4b9cd60bebf417cab8/docs/configuration_and_development/configuration_guide/core_servers/configuring_lifecycle_manager.md#L18-L36, acc. 2026-10-31]. It exposes `<name>/manage_nodes` (`nav2_msgs/srv/ManageLifecycleNodes`: STARTUP=0, PAUSE=1, RESUME=2, RESET=3, SHUTDOWN=4, CONFIGURE=5, CLEANUP=6) and `<name>/is_active` (`std_srvs/srv/Trigger`) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_lifecycle_manager/src/lifecycle_manager.cpp#L64-L74, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_msgs/srv/ManageLifecycleNodes.srv, acc. 2026-10-31]. If any server's bond heartbeat is missing for `bond_timeout`, the manager logs "CRITICAL FAILURE: SERVER %s IS DOWN", hard-resets all servers and starts a 1 s respawn-check timer [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_lifecycle_manager/src/lifecycle_manager.cpp#L488-L511, acc. 2026-10-31]. Each Nav2 server creates the bond on topic `bond` with heartbeat period `bond_heartbeat_period` (default 0.1 s) and heartbeat timeout 4.0 s [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_util/src/lifecycle_node.cpp#L42-L43, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_util/src/lifecycle_node.cpp#L73-L82, acc. 2026-10-31]. The TB4 `nav2.yaml` has no `lifecycle_manager` block, so these code defaults apply [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/config/nav2.yaml, acc. 2026-10-31].

#### 3.2 bt_navigator (task layer)

`bt_navigator` loads two navigator plugins, `navigate_to_pose` (`nav2_bt_navigator::NavigateToPoseNavigator`) and `navigate_through_poses` (`nav2_bt_navigator::NavigateThroughPosesNavigator`), with `bt_loop_duration: 10` (ms), `default_server_timeout: 20`, `wait_for_service_timeout: 1000`, `action_server_result_timeout: 900.0` and `error_code_names: [compute_path_error_code, follow_path_error_code]` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/config/nav2.yaml#L1-L18, acc. 2026-10-31]. The TB4 file does **not** set `default_nav_to_pose_bt_xml` or `default_nav_through_poses_bt_xml`, so the code defaults apply: `<nav2_bt_navigator share>/behavior_trees/navigate_to_pose_w_replanning_and_recovery.xml` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_bt_navigator/src/navigators/navigate_to_pose.cpp#L76-L85, acc. 2026-10-31] and `navigate_through_poses_w_replanning_and_recovery.xml` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_bt_navigator/src/navigators/navigate_through_poses.cpp#L71-L80, acc. 2026-10-31]. A goal's `behavior_tree` string field may select a different XML per goal [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_bt_navigator/src/navigators/navigate_to_pose.cpp#L98-L111, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_msgs/action/NavigateToPose.action, acc. 2026-10-31].

Nodes of the default NavigateToPose tree, in tree order [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_bt_navigator/behavior_trees/navigate_to_pose_w_replanning_and_recovery.xml, acc. 2026-10-31]:

| Line | Node | Role for the autonomy log |
|---|---|---|
| 9 | `RecoveryNode number_of_retries="6" name="NavigateRecovery"` | Root; alternates the navigation pipeline and the recovery subtree up to 6 times, then FAILURE → goal ABORTED |
| 10 | `PipelineSequence name="NavigateWithReplanning"` | Main pipeline |
| 11–12 | `ControllerSelector` (topic `controller_selector`, default `FollowPath`), `PlannerSelector` (topic `planner_selector`, default `GridBased`) | Transient-local String topics that let a human or script change the plugin (N20) |
| 13 | `RateController hz="1.0"` | Replanning at 1 Hz |
| 14–20 | `RecoveryNode number_of_retries="1" name="ComputePathToPose"` → `ComputePathToPose` / `WouldAPlannerRecoveryHelp` + `ClearEntireCostmap` (`global_costmap/clear_entirely_global_costmap`) | Planner with contextual recovery |
| 22–28 | `RecoveryNode number_of_retries="1" name="FollowPath"` → `FollowPath` / `WouldAControllerRecoveryHelp` + `ClearEntireCostmap` (`local_costmap/clear_entirely_local_costmap`) | Controller with contextual recovery |
| 30–47 | `Sequence` → `Fallback`(`WouldAControllerRecoveryHelp`, `WouldAPlannerRecoveryHelp`) → `ReactiveFallback name="RecoveryFallback"` → `GoalUpdated` / `RoundRobin name="RecoveryActions"` | Recovery subtree, entered only if the error code is recoverable; `GoalUpdated` aborts recovery when a new goal arrived |
| 38–41 | `Sequence name="ClearingActions"`: `ClearEntireCostmap` local + global | Recovery 1 |
| 42 | `Spin spin_dist="1.57"` | Recovery 2 |
| 43 | `Wait wait_duration="5.0"` | Recovery 3 |
| 44 | `BackUp backup_dist="0.30" backup_speed="0.15"` | Recovery 4 |

`DriveOnHeading` and `AssistedTeleop` are **not** in either default tree, although both plugins are loaded by the behavior server [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/config/nav2.yaml#L247, acc. 2026-10-31]. The NavigateThroughPoses tree is identical except for `RateController hz="0.333"`, a `ReactiveSequence` of `RemovePassedGoals radius="0.7"` and `ComputePathThroughPoses` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_bt_navigator/behavior_trees/navigate_through_poses_w_replanning_and_recovery.xml#L12-L17, acc. 2026-10-31]. `RecoveryNode` ticks its first child and, on FAILURE, its second child while `retry_count_ <= number_of_retries` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_behavior_tree/plugins/control/recovery_node.cpp#L31-L42, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_behavior_tree/plugins/control/recovery_node.cpp#L65, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_behavior_tree/plugins/control/recovery_node.cpp#L100, acc. 2026-10-31].

The BT action server runs the tree with `bt_->run(&tree_, on_loop, is_canceling, bt_loop_duration_)`; `on_loop` handles preemption, flushes the topic logger and calls the navigator's `onLoop` (feedback) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_behavior_tree/include/nav2_behavior_tree/bt_action_server_impl.hpp#L315-L324, acc. 2026-10-31]. On completion it maps BT SUCCEEDED/FAILED/CANCELED to action `succeeded_current`/`terminate_current`/`terminate_all` and fills `result->error_code` with the lowest non-zero code among `error_code_names` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_behavior_tree/include/nav2_behavior_tree/bt_action_server_impl.hpp#L332-L356, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_behavior_tree/include/nav2_behavior_tree/bt_action_server_impl.hpp#L358-L380, acc. 2026-10-31]. The action server's `result_timeout` is set from `action_server_result_timeout` (900 s on TB4) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_behavior_tree/include/nav2_behavior_tree/bt_action_server_impl.hpp#L149-L153, acc. 2026-10-31]. Each navigator creates a separate non-lifecycle client node named `<bt_navigator>_<action>_rclcpp_node` (e.g. `bt_navigator_navigate_to_pose_rclcpp_node`) that owns the BT nodes' subscriptions and the `behavior_tree_log` publisher [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_behavior_tree/include/nav2_behavior_tree/bt_action_server_impl.hpp#L121-L136, acc. 2026-10-31].

#### 3.3 planner_server, controller_server, smoother_server

Planner: one plugin `GridBased` = `nav2_navfn_planner::NavfnPlanner` (`tolerance 0.5`, `use_astar false`, `allow_unknown true`); `expected_planner_frequency` is commented out, so the code default 1.0 Hz applies to the overrun warning only [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/config/nav2.yaml#L218-L227, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_planner/src/planner_server.cpp#L55, acc. 2026-10-31]. The planner server exposes actions `compute_path_to_pose` and `compute_path_through_poses` and service `is_path_valid` (`nav2_msgs/srv/IsPathValid`) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_planner/src/planner_server.cpp#L164, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_planner/src/planner_server.cpp#L172, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_planner/src/planner_server.cpp#L201-L202, acc. 2026-10-31].

Controller: `controller_frequency: 20.0`, `failure_tolerance: 0.3`, progress checker `nav2_controller::SimpleProgressChecker` (`required_movement_radius 0.5`, `movement_time_allowance 10.0`), goal checker `nav2_controller::SimpleGoalChecker` (`xy_goal_tolerance 0.25`, `yaw_goal_tolerance 0.25`, `stateful true`), controller plugin `FollowPath` = `nav2_mppi_controller::MPPIController` (`vx_max 0.5`, `vx_min -0.35`, `wz_max 1.9`, `motion_model DiffDrive`, `batch_size 2000`, `time_steps 56`, `model_dt 0.05`) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/config/nav2.yaml#L20-L62, acc. 2026-10-31]. The controller server's action is `follow_path`; its loop is a `rclcpp::WallRate(controller_frequency_)` that cancels with `publishZeroVelocity()` and otherwise calls `computeAndPublishVelocity()` each cycle [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_controller/src/controller_server.cpp#L240-L247, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_controller/src/controller_server.cpp#L502-L551, acc. 2026-10-31]. It publishes velocities with a `nav2_util::TwistPublisher` on `cmd_vel` (depth 1) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_controller/src/controller_server.cpp#L227, acc. 2026-10-31], which the launch file remaps to `cmd_vel_nav` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_bringup/launch/navigation_launch.py#L129-L138, acc. 2026-10-31]. The controller also subscribes to `speed_limit` (`nav2_msgs/msg/SpeedLimit`, QoS 10) and forwards the limit to every controller plugin [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_controller/src/controller_server.cpp#L63, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_controller/src/controller_server.cpp#L254-L257, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_controller/src/controller_server.cpp#L836-L840, acc. 2026-10-31].

Smoother: `simple_smoother` = `nav2_smoother::SimpleSmoother` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/config/nav2.yaml#L229-L237, acc. 2026-10-31]; it is not referenced by the default BT XML (no `SmoothPath` node) so it is idle on TB4 unless a client calls `smooth_path` directly.

#### 3.4 behavior_server

`behavior_plugins: ["spin", "backup", "drive_on_heading", "assisted_teleop", "wait"]` with `cycle_frequency: 10.0`, `simulate_ahead_time 2.0`, `max_rotational_vel 1.0`, `min_rotational_vel 0.4`, `rotational_acc_lim 3.2` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/config/nav2.yaml#L239-L265, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://docs.nav2.org/rolling/configuration_and_development/configuration_guide/core_servers/configuring_behavior_server/, acc. 2026-10-31]. Each `TimedBehavior` creates an action server named after the plugin (`spin`, `backup`, …), a `TwistPublisher` on `cmd_vel` (remapped to `cmd_vel_nav` by the launch) and runs `onCycleUpdate()` in a `rclcpp::WallRate(cycle_frequency_)` loop [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_behaviors/include/nav2_behaviors/timed_behavior.hpp#L146-L154, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_behaviors/include/nav2_behaviors/timed_behavior.hpp#L235-L288, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_bringup/launch/navigation_launch.py#L172-L182, acc. 2026-10-31]. Preemption of a running behavior is not implemented: a pending goal aborts the current one and `stopRobot()` publishes a zero `TwistStamped` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_behaviors/include/nav2_behaviors/timed_behavior.hpp#L240-L251, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_behaviors/include/nav2_behaviors/timed_behavior.hpp#L291-L301, acc. 2026-10-31]. `Wait` publishes no velocity at all; it only waits until `wait_end_` and publishes `time_left` feedback [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_behaviors/plugins/wait.cpp#L31-L50, acc. 2026-10-31].

**AssistedTeleop** (the single shared-control mode in the stack). On configure it declares `projection_time` 1.0 s, `simulation_time_step` 0.1 s and `cmd_vel_teleop` (default topic name `cmd_vel_teleop`), subscribes to that topic with a `TwistSubscriber` (accepts `Twist` or `TwistStamped`) and to `preempt_teleop` (`std_msgs/msg/Empty`) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_behaviors/plugins/assisted_teleop.cpp#L27-L67, acc. 2026-10-31]. Each cycle it publishes `current_teleop_duration` feedback, fails with `TIMEOUT` (731) when `time_allowance` is exceeded, succeeds when `preempt_teleop` was received, projects the human twist forward for `projection_time` and either zeroes it (collision on the first step) or scales it by `time/projection_time`, then publishes the scaled `TwistStamped` on `cmd_vel` (→ `cmd_vel_nav`) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_behaviors/plugins/assisted_teleop.cpp#L83-L155, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_msgs/action/AssistedTeleop.action, acc. 2026-10-31]. The Nav2 docs describe the BT node as invoking "the AssistedTeleop ROS 2 action server, which filters teleop twist commands to prevent collisions" [VERIFIED: https://docs.nav2.org/rolling/configuration_and_development/configuration_guide/core_servers/bt_plugins/actions/AssistedTeleop/, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/docs.nav2.org/blob/74f751f161154075af6c2b4b9cd60bebf417cab8/docs/configuration_and_development/configuration_guide/core_servers/bt_plugins/actions/AssistedTeleop.md#L3-L5, acc. 2026-10-31]. Nothing in the TB4 stack starts this behavior; it exists only as an action server a client may call (N6–N8).

#### 3.5 velocity_smoother

Parameters on TB4: `smoothing_frequency 20.0`, `scale_velocities False`, `feedback OPEN_LOOP`, `max_velocity [0.26, 0.0, 1.0]`, `min_velocity [-0.26, 0.0, -1.0]`, `max_accel [2.5, 0.0, 3.2]`, `max_decel [-2.5, 0.0, -3.2]`, `odom_topic odom`, `odom_duration 0.1`, `deadband_velocity [0,0,0]`, `velocity_timeout 1.0` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/config/nav2.yaml#L279-L292, acc. 2026-10-31]. Note the smoother clamps linear speed to 0.26 m/s although the MPPI controller allows 0.5 m/s. It subscribes to `cmd_vel` (QoS depth 1; remapped to `cmd_vel_nav`) and publishes `cmd_vel_smoothed` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_velocity_smoother/src/velocity_smoother.cpp#L155-L161, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_bringup/launch/navigation_launch.py#L205-L216, acc. 2026-10-31]; the smoothing runs on a wall timer of `1000/smoothing_frequency` ms created on activation [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_velocity_smoother/src/velocity_smoother.cpp#L184-L187, acc. 2026-10-31]. **Timeout behaviour (verified in code):** in `smootherTimer()` nothing is published until a first command arrives; if `now() - last_command_time_ > velocity_timeout_` the stored command is replaced by a zero `TwistStamped` so that the output decelerates to zero, and once the last output is already zero the node sets `stopped_ = true` and returns without publishing [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_velocity_smoother/src/velocity_smoother.cpp#L312-L341, acc. 2026-10-31]. The docs state the same: after the timeout the smoother "will send a zero-ed out Twist command and stop publishing" [VERIFIED: https://docs.nav2.org/rolling/configuration_and_development/configuration_guide/core_servers/configuring_velocity_smoother/, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/docs.nav2.org/blob/74f751f161154075af6c2b4b9cd60bebf417cab8/docs/configuration_and_development/configuration_guide/core_servers/configuring_velocity_smoother.md#L58-L62, acc. 2026-10-31]. `last_command_time_` is the incoming `header.stamp` unless it is zero, in which case `now()` is used [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_velocity_smoother/src/velocity_smoother.cpp#L241-L246, acc. 2026-10-31].

#### 3.6 collision_monitor

TB4 configuration: `cmd_vel_in_topic "cmd_vel_smoothed"`, `cmd_vel_out_topic "cmd_vel"`, `state_topic "collision_monitor_state"`, `transform_tolerance 0.2`, `source_timeout 1.0`, `base_shift_correction True`, `stop_pub_timeout 2.0`, polygons `["FootprintApproach"]` of `type polygon`, `action_type "approach"`, `footprint_topic local_costmap/published_footprint`, `time_before_collision 1.2`, `simulation_time_step 0.1`, `min_points 6`, `enabled True`; one observation source `scan` of type `scan` on topic `scan`, `min_height 0.15`, `max_height 2.0` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/config/nav2.yaml#L294-L324, acc. 2026-10-31]. **No STOP, SLOWDOWN or LIMIT polygon is configured on TB4**; the only configured action is APPROACH, which scales velocity by `collision_time / time_before_collision` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_collision_monitor/src/collision_monitor_node.cpp#L583-L607, acc. 2026-10-31]. The node is message-driven: `process()` is called from the `cmd_vel_in` callback (no timer) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_collision_monitor/src/collision_monitor_node.cpp#L196-L211, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_collision_monitor/src/collision_monitor_node.cpp#L413-L421, acc. 2026-10-31]. If an enabled source has no valid data within its `source_timeout`, the action becomes `STOP` with `polygon_name "invalid source"` and zero velocity [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_collision_monitor/src/collision_monitor_node.cpp#L433-L449, acc. 2026-10-31]; the docs describe `source_timeout` as the interval after which "the robot will be stopped" [VERIFIED: https://docs.nav2.org/rolling/configuration_and_development/configuration_guide/core_servers/collision_monitor/configuring_collision_monitor_node/, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/docs.nav2.org/blob/74f751f161154075af6c2b4b9cd60bebf417cab8/docs/configuration_and_development/configuration_guide/core_servers/collision_monitor/configuring_collision_monitor_node.md#L112-L117, acc. 2026-10-31]. The state message (`nav2_msgs/msg/CollisionMonitorState`: `DO_NOTHING=0, STOP=1, SLOWDOWN=2, APPROACH=3, LIMIT=4`, `polygon_name`) is published **only when the acting polygon changes** (`robot_action.polygon_name != robot_action_prev_.polygon_name`) through `notifyActionState()` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_msgs/msg/CollisionMonitorState.msg, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_collision_monitor/src/collision_monitor_node.cpp#L510-L513, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_collision_monitor/src/collision_monitor_node.cpp#L650-L657, acc. 2026-10-31]. Output velocity: after the requested velocity becomes zero the node keeps publishing zeros for `stop_pub_timeout` (2.0 s) and then ceases publishing; the output header is copied from the input [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_collision_monitor/src/collision_monitor_node.cpp#L213-L235, acc. 2026-10-31]. A `~/toggle` service (`nav2_msgs/srv/Toggle`) sets `enabled_` and disables all polygon processing [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_collision_monitor/src/collision_monitor_node.cpp#L88-L90, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_collision_monitor/src/collision_monitor_node.cpp#L669-L677, acc. 2026-10-31].

#### 3.7 waypoint_follower, docking_server, route_server

`waypoint_follower`: `loop_rate 20`, `stop_on_failure false`, `action_server_result_timeout 900.0`, task executor `wait_at_waypoint` = `nav2_waypoint_follower::WaitAtWaypoint` with `waypoint_pause_duration 200` (ms) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/config/nav2.yaml#L267-L277, acc. 2026-10-31]. It is an action client of `navigate_to_pose` and an action server for `follow_waypoints` and `follow_gps_waypoints` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_waypoint_follower/src/waypoint_follower.cpp#L79-L90, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_waypoint_follower/src/waypoint_follower.cpp#L106, acc. 2026-10-31].

`docking_server` (opennav_docking, part of navigation2 1.3.14 under `nav2_docking/`) is launched and configured: `controller_frequency 50.0`, `initial_perception_timeout 5.0`, `wait_charge_timeout 5.0`, `dock_approach_timeout 30.0`, `max_retries 3`, one dock plugin `simple_charging_dock` = `opennav_docking::SimpleChargingDock` with `use_external_detection_pose true`, `use_battery_status false`, `use_stall_detection false`; **the `docks:` instance list is commented out**, so no dock is in the database [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/config/nav2.yaml#L326-L371, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_bringup/launch/navigation_launch.py#L228-L238, acc. 2026-10-31]. The server offers `dock_robot` and `undock_robot` actions and publishes velocity on `cmd_vel` with no remap in the launch file, i.e. **directly onto the final `cmd_vel` topic, bypassing velocity_smoother and collision_monitor** [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_docking/opennav_docking/src/docking_server.cpp#L64, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_docking/opennav_docking/src/docking_server.cpp#L74-L85, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_bringup/launch/navigation_launch.py#L228-L238, acc. 2026-10-31]. The TB4 Python helper `TurtleBot4Navigator` does not use it: it subclasses `nav2_simple_commander.BasicNavigator`, subscribes to `dock_status` (`irobot_create_msgs/msg/DockStatus`) and creates action clients for the Create 3 firmware actions `undock` and `dock` (`irobot_create_msgs/action/Undock`, `Dock`) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/turtlebot4_navigation/turtlebot4_navigator.py#L29-L32, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/turtlebot4_navigation/turtlebot4_navigator.py#L51-L69, acc. 2026-10-31]. Docking on TB4 is therefore a Create 3 firmware action in the shipped tooling; the Nav2 docking server is present but has no dock to dock to.

`route_server` (nav2_route) is in the launch's lifecycle list but has no block in the TB4 `nav2.yaml` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_bringup/launch/navigation_launch.py#L43-L54, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/config/nav2.yaml, acc. 2026-10-31]; its behaviour with default parameters was not verified (see Unknowns).

#### 3.8 Rates (from nav2.yaml, with line numbers)

| Quantity | Value | Line |
|---|---|---|
| `bt_navigator.bt_loop_duration` | 10 ms (100 Hz tick ceiling) | 7 |
| `bt_navigator.default_server_timeout` | 20 ms | 8 |
| `bt_navigator.action_server_result_timeout` | 900.0 s | 10 |
| `controller_server.controller_frequency` | 20.0 Hz | 23 |
| `progress_checker.movement_time_allowance` | 10.0 s for 0.5 m | 34–35 |
| `local_costmap.update_frequency` / `publish_frequency` | 5.0 / 2.0 Hz | 130–131 |
| `global_costmap.update_frequency` / `publish_frequency` | 1.0 / 1.0 Hz | 180–181 |
| BT replanning (`RateController hz`) | 1.0 Hz (XML, not yaml) | XML line 13 |
| `behavior_server.cycle_frequency` | 10.0 Hz | 246 |
| `waypoint_follower.loop_rate` | 20 Hz | 270 |
| `velocity_smoother.smoothing_frequency` | 20.0 Hz | 282 |
| `velocity_smoother.velocity_timeout` | 1.0 s | 292 |
| `collision_monitor.source_timeout` | 1.0 s | 303 |
| `collision_monitor.stop_pub_timeout` | 2.0 s | 305 |
| `docking_server.controller_frequency` | 50.0 Hz | 329 |

[VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/config/nav2.yaml, acc. 2026-10-31]

#### 3.9 map_server / amcl / slam_toolbox hand-off

Localization mode: `map_server` + `amcl` under `lifecycle_manager_localization` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_bringup/launch/localization_launch.py#L47, acc. 2026-10-31]; AMCL declares `set_initial_pose` false and `always_reset_initial_pose` false, so a human (or a script) must publish `initialpose` before navigation can start [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_amcl/src/amcl_node.cpp#L131, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_amcl/src/amcl_node.cpp#L221, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_amcl/src/amcl_node.cpp#L1571-L1572, acc. 2026-10-31]; the TB4 manual: "The 2D Pose Estimate tool is used in localization to set the approximate initial pose of the robot on the map. This is required for the Nav2 stack to know where to start localizing from" [VERIFIED: https://turtlebot.github.io/turtlebot4-user-manual/tutorials/navigation.html, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/tutorials/navigation.md#L220, acc. 2026-10-31]. SLAM mode replaces both with slam_toolbox (`online_sync_launch.py` or `online_async_launch.py`) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/launch/slam.launch.py#L71-L74, acc. 2026-10-31]. Both costmaps use `map_subscribe_transient_local: true` so a late-starting Nav2 picks up the latched map [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/config/nav2.yaml#L173, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/config/nav2.yaml#L211, acc. 2026-10-31].

## 4. Control modes: teleoperation methods, autonomous modes, shared control, switching

_Lead note._ There is no mode variable anywhere. "Teleop mode" and "autonomous mode" exist only as which publishers are active on `/cmd_vel` at a given moment; switching is implicit and done by whoever publishes. The one shared-control mechanism (Nav2 AssistedTeleop) is dormant in the shipped configuration.

### 4. Operating modes: teleoperation, autonomy, shared control, switching  _[TB4 stack track]_

#### 4.1 Teleoperation methods documented in the manual

1. **Keyboard teleop from the PC** over Wi-Fi DDS: `sudo apt install ros-jazzy-teleop-twist-keyboard`; `ros2 run teleop_twist_keyboard teleop_twist_keyboard --ros-args -p stamped:=true`; keys i/j/... [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/tutorials/driving.md#L51, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/tutorials/driving.md#L57, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/tutorials/driving.md#L70-L73, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://turtlebot.github.io/turtlebot4-user-manual/tutorials/driving.html, acc. 2026-10-31]. Network path: PC -> Wi-Fi -> Pi (DDS) -> republisher -> USB-C Ethernet -> Create 3 [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/setup/networking.md#L7, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/software/overview.md#L18, acc. 2026-10-31]. The manual says it is run "on your PC"; running it over SSH on the Pi is equally possible and then the publisher lives on the Pi (same node name `teleop_twist_keyboard`) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros2/teleop_twist_keyboard/blob/ed06da483e4acef8d0ab225557d64e6597c9bdf2/teleop_twist_keyboard.py#L139, acc. 2026-10-31] — the observer cannot tell PC from SSH-on-Pi by node name alone (see Section 11).
2. **TurtleBot 4 Bluetooth controller** (included with Standard only): paired in advance with the Pi; `joy_teleop.launch.py` is part of standard/lite launch; hold **L1** for normal speed or **R1** for turbo and move the left stick [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/setup/basic.md#L306, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/tutorials/driving.md#L95, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/overview/features.md#L115, acc. 2026-10-31]. Config: `axis_linear.x: 1`, `scale_linear.x: 0.2`, turbo 0.5; `axis_angular.yaw: 0`, `scale_angular.yaw: 0.5`, turbo 1.9; `enable_button: 4` (L1), `enable_turbo_button: 5` (R1) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/config/turtlebot4_controller.config.yaml#L62, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/config/turtlebot4_controller.config.yaml#L76, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/config/turtlebot4_controller.config.yaml#L77, acc. 2026-10-31]. teleop_twist_joy: `require_enable_button` default true; with enable released it sends exactly one zero command (`sent_disable_msg`) and then nothing, so **L1/R1 is a dead-man for teleop** [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros2/teleop_twist_joy/blob/556a07100ee745f68619de84a95d3c84a4892ab9/src/teleop_twist_joy.cpp#L106, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros2/teleop_twist_joy/blob/556a07100ee745f68619de84a95d3c84a4892ab9/src/teleop_twist_joy.cpp#L338-L341, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros2/teleop_twist_joy/blob/556a07100ee745f68619de84a95d3c84a4892ab9/src/teleop_twist_joy.cpp#L352, acc. 2026-10-31]. The same controller also triggers **actions** through `turtlebot4_node`: B = EStop, L2/R2 = Wall Follow, Home short = Dock / 3 s = Undock, A/X/D-pad = menu (Section 3.2) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/config/turtlebot4.yaml#L60-L62, acc. 2026-10-31]. Network path: Bluetooth -> Pi `/dev/input/js0` -> `joy` and `cmd_vel` on DDS -> Create 3 [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/launch/joy_teleop.launch.py#L29, acc. 2026-10-31]. Pairing uses `bluetoothctl` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/setup/basic.md#L313, acc. 2026-10-31].
3. **Gazebo GUI Teleop plugin** (simulation): `gui.config` loads gz plugin `Teleop` with `<topic>/cmd_vel</topic>` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_simulator/blob/b7d0f3b973258481f6cd8137bef9240fb567f566/turtlebot4_gz_bringup/gui/standard/gui.config#L117, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_simulator/blob/b7d0f3b973258481f6cd8137bef9240fb567f566/turtlebot4_gz_bringup/gui/standard/gui.config#L118, acc. 2026-10-31]; bridged by `cmd_vel_bridge` to ROS `cmd_vel` as TwistStamped [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_sim/blob/6c305843afcfe094bf8eb2a0c6997f531e526b9d/irobot_create_gz/irobot_create_gz_bringup/launch/create3_ros_gz_bridge.launch.py#L60, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_sim/blob/6c305843afcfe094bf8eb2a0c6997f531e526b9d/irobot_create_gz/irobot_create_gz_bringup/launch/create3_ros_gz_bridge.launch.py#L66, acc. 2026-10-31].
4. **Joystick from the desktop over Wi-Fi**: not a documented manual method; `joy_teleop.launch.py` can be launched on any machine (`joy_device` argument) and would publish `cmd_vel` over DDS [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/launch/joy_teleop.launch.py#L29, acc. 2026-10-31] [UNKNOWN: not described in manual].
5. **Create 3 motion actions from CLI** (`drive_distance`, `drive_arc`, `rotate_angle`) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/tutorials/driving.md#L154, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/tutorials/driving.md#L159, acc. 2026-10-31].

Latency figures: [UNKNOWN: no official latency numbers for Bluetooth or Wi-Fi teleop in the manual or code]. The manual only warns that 2.4 GHz or limited-bandwidth networks "can limit functionality including navigation and sending/receiving images" and recommends 5 GHz [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/setup/networking.md#L26, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/setup/basic.md#L94, acc. 2026-10-31].

#### 4.2 Autonomous modes

- **Nav2** via `turtlebot4_navigation/nav2.launch.py` (params `nav2.yaml`, `use_sim_time`, `namespace`) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/launch/nav2.launch.py#L30-L33, acc. 2026-10-31]; SLAM via `slam.launch.py` (slam_toolbox sync/async) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/launch/slam.launch.py#L70, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/launch/slam.launch.py#L73, acc. 2026-10-31]; localization via `localization.launch.py` with a map [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/tutorials/navigation.md#L129, acc. 2026-10-31]. The manual recommends running Nav2/SLAM on the PC because of Pi performance [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/software/overview.md#L26, acc. 2026-10-31].
- **TurtleBot4Navigator** (Python, subclass of nav2_simple_commander `BasicNavigator`): `dock()`, `undock()`, `getDockedStatus()` (subscribes `dock_status`), `startToPose()` (goToPose + feedback loop, cancels after 600 s), `startThroughPoses()`, `startFollowWaypoints()`, `createPath()` (collects poses from RViz `initialpose` clicks) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/turtlebot4_navigation/turtlebot4_navigator.py#L51, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/turtlebot4_navigation/turtlebot4_navigator.py#L58-L59, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/turtlebot4_navigation/turtlebot4_navigator.py#L63-L64, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/turtlebot4_navigation/turtlebot4_navigator.py#L68, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/turtlebot4_navigation/turtlebot4_navigator.py#L266, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/turtlebot4_navigation/turtlebot4_navigator.py#L284, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/turtlebot4_navigation/turtlebot4_navigator.py#L328, acc. 2026-10-31]. Manual tutorials: navigate to pose, through poses, follow waypoints, create path, **mail delivery** (terminal prompt selects goal, then `startToPose`), patrol loop with battery monitoring, LLM integration [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/tutorials/turtlebot4_navigator.md#L1045, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/tutorials/turtlebot4_navigator.md#L1185, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/tutorials/turtlebot4_navigator.md#L1499, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://turtlebot.github.io/turtlebot4-user-manual/tutorials/turtlebot4_navigator.html, acc. 2026-10-31]. Every example starts with `dock()` check, `setInitialPose`, `waitUntilNav2Active()`, `undock()`, then navigation [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/tutorials/turtlebot4_navigator.md#L1286, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/tutorials/turtlebot4_navigator.md#L1296, acc. 2026-10-31].
- **Create 3 firmware behaviours** started by the TB4 layer: dock, undock, wall follow (10 s) — reactive firmware autonomy under a human or script trigger (Section 3.2).
- Nav2 `docking_server` is configured (`opennav_docking::SimpleChargingDock`, `use_external_detection_pose: true`, no dock instances declared) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/config/nav2.yaml#L342, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/config/nav2.yaml#L347, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/config/nav2.yaml#L361, acc. 2026-10-31]; the TB4 tutorials use the Create 3 dock action instead [UNKNOWN: whether Nav2 docking is usable without a docks list — Nav2 track].

#### 4.3 Shared control

The TB4 `nav2.yaml` loads `nav2_behaviors::AssistedTeleop` in `behavior_plugins` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/config/nav2.yaml#L247, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/config/nav2.yaml#L257, acc. 2026-10-31]; no TB4 launch file, tutorial or config invokes it (grep for `assisted_teleop` in the manual: 0 hits; in the TB4 code: nav2.yaml only). Semantics are the Nav2 track's. There is no other blending or arbitration (Section 3.5).

#### 4.4 Mode switching

There is **no mode switch**. Nothing in the TB4 stack tracks "teleop mode" vs "autonomous mode"; the Create 3 executes the last `/cmd_vel` it received whoever sent it (Section 3.5). The only lifecycle-level switch is Nav2's own (lifecycle manager autostart; a human can cancel a goal via RViz or `cancelTask()` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/turtlebot4_navigation/turtlebot4_navigator.py#L285, acc. 2026-10-31]). Consequences for measurement: (a) control attribution must be done per message from publisher identity, not from a mode variable; (b) overlapping human and Nav2 publishers produce an interleaved command stream that the taxonomy must classify as `shared` for the overlap (uncovered case U1); (c) the EStop service is the only software action that stops both sources at once (it cuts motor power in firmware) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/software/create3.md#L154, acc. 2026-10-31].

### 4. Control paths terminating in the firmware (base part)  _[Create 3 base track]_

| Path | What it can command | Observable by a passive ROS 2 observer? | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| `/cmd_vel` (Twist), `/cmd_vel_stamped` (TwistStamped) | wheel velocities | yes (topic) — but not who | [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/ros2.md#L46; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/releases/i_0_0.md#L25, acc. 2026-10-31] |
| ROS 2 actions `/dock /undock /wall_follow /drive_distance /rotate_angle /drive_arc /navigate_to_position` | autonomous behaviours; pre-empt cmd_vel | goal/feedback/status topics (hidden action topics; naming per ROS 2 design — CLAIM, design.ros2.org not reachable) | [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/ros2.md#L95-L96; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/ros2.md#L103, acc. 2026-10-31] |
| `/led_animation`, `/audio_note_sequence` actions; `/cmd_lightring`, `/cmd_audio` topics | lights and sound only | yes | [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/ui.md#L15; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/ui.md#L27; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/ui.md#L39; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/ui.md#L58, acc. 2026-10-31] |
| `/e_stop`, `/robot_power`, `/reset_pose` services | disable wheels; power off; reset odometry | effect only (`wheel_status.wheels_enabled`; topics vanish) | [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/ros2.md#L63; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/ros2.md#L70; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_examples/blob/fe3d408a266b755b94cb7334a92f2d58dd661c13/create3_republisher/bringup/params.yaml#L38, acc. 2026-10-31] |
| Parameters (`safety_override`, `reflexes*`, `wheel_accel_limit`, `publish_*`) | change what the firmware will do and publish | `/parameter_events` | [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/ros2.md#L29; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/ros2.md#L134, acc. 2026-10-31] |
| Faceplate buttons | firmware: standby (• 10 s), storage/off (⏻ 7 s), AP mode (•+•• 3 s); application-defined short presses | `/interface_buttons` | [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/hw/face.md#L10; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/hw/face.md#L22; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/setup/provision.md#L8; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/ui.md#L7, acc. 2026-10-31] |
