### 4. Human inputs into Nav2  _[Nav2 track]_

| # | Input | Interface and type | What it does | Where verified |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 4.1 | RViz **Nav2 Goal** tool (`nav2_rviz_plugins/GoalTool`, used in TB4 `navigation.rviz` and `robot.rviz`) | in-process `GoalUpdater` → Nav2 panel → **action goal** on `navigate_to_pose` (no topic) | Starts a `NavigateToPose` goal; the panel also offers Cancel, waypoint accumulation (`follow_waypoints`) and `navigate_through_poses` | [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_rviz_plugins/src/goal_tool.cpp#L37-L49, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_rviz_plugins/src/nav2_panel.cpp#L948-L974, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_rviz_plugins/src/nav2_panel.cpp#L1299-L1326, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_rviz_plugins/src/nav2_panel.cpp#L566-L577, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_desktop/blob/d831a5a3d2323d2db1ce79e5b909beecbeed3dcf/turtlebot4_viz/rviz/navigation.rviz#L741, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_desktop/blob/d831a5a3d2323d2db1ce79e5b909beecbeed3dcf/turtlebot4_viz/rviz/robot.rviz#L348, acc. 2026-10-31] |
| 4.2 | RViz **2D Goal Pose** (`rviz_default_plugins/SetGoal`, in TB4 `model.rviz` only) and any script | topic `goal_pose` (`geometry_msgs/msg/PoseStamped`), `SystemDefaultsQoS` | `NavigateToPoseNavigator` subscribes and re-sends the pose to itself as an action goal | [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_bt_navigator/src/navigators/navigate_to_pose.cpp#L49-L52, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_bt_navigator/src/navigators/navigate_to_pose.cpp#L268-L274, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_desktop/blob/d831a5a3d2323d2db1ce79e5b909beecbeed3dcf/turtlebot4_viz/rviz/model.rviz#L331-L337, acc. 2026-10-31] |
| 4.3 | **Preemption** | a new goal while one is EXECUTING | Accepted only if the pending goal requests the same BT XML (or empty and the current is the default); the blackboard goal is replaced and `number_recoveries` reset to 0; otherwise the pending goal is terminated and the current one continues | [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_bt_navigator/src/navigators/navigate_to_pose.cpp#L196-L225, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_bt_navigator/src/navigators/navigate_to_pose.cpp#L257-L265, acc. 2026-10-31] |
| 4.4 | **Cancel** (RViz Cancel button, `BasicNavigator.cancelTask()`) | action cancel request | `is_canceling` lambda returns true; BT run returns CANCELED → `terminate_all`; controller publishes zero velocity | [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_rviz_plugins/src/nav2_panel.cpp#L976-L1012, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_simple_commander/nav2_simple_commander/robot_navigator.py#L454-L460, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_behavior_tree/include/nav2_behavior_tree/bt_action_server_impl.hpp#L303-L313, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_behavior_tree/include/nav2_behavior_tree/bt_action_server_impl.hpp#L349-L352, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_controller/src/controller_server.cpp#L512-L517, acc. 2026-10-31] |
| 4.5 | **2D Pose Estimate** | topic `initialpose` (`geometry_msgs/msg/PoseWithCovarianceStamped`) | AMCL re-initialises its filter; `BasicNavigator.setInitialPose()` publishes the same topic; `TurtleBot4Navigator.createPath()` reads it as waypoint clicks | [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_amcl/src/amcl_node.cpp#L1571-L1572, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_simple_commander/nav2_simple_commander/robot_navigator.py#L109-L110, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/turtlebot4_navigation/turtlebot4_navigator.py#L63-L66, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/turtlebot4_navigation/turtlebot4_navigator.py#L153-L156, acc. 2026-10-31] |
| 4.6 | **Publish Point** | topic `clicked_point` (`geometry_msgs/msg/PointStamped`) | No Nav2 subscriber in the TB4 configuration (only `nav2_rviz_plugins` route_tool); the TB4 manual uses it as a coordinate read-out | [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_desktop/blob/d831a5a3d2323d2db1ce79e5b909beecbeed3dcf/turtlebot4_viz/rviz/navigation.rviz#L733, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_rviz_plugins/src/route_tool.cpp#L57-L58, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/tutorials/navigation.md#L227-L237, acc. 2026-10-31] |
| 4.7 | **Python Simple Commander** (`nav2_simple_commander.BasicNavigator`) | action clients `navigate_through_poses`, `navigate_to_pose`, `follow_waypoints`, `follow_gps_waypoints`, `follow_path`, `compute_path_to_pose`, `compute_path_through_poses`, `smooth_path`, `spin`, `backup`, `drive_on_heading`, `assisted_teleop`, `dock_robot`, `undock_robot`; publisher `initialpose` | Methods `goToPose`, `goThroughPoses`, `followWaypoints`, `followPath`, `spin`, `backup`, `driveOnHeading`, `assistedTeleop(time_allowance=30)`, `dockRobotByPose`/`dockRobotByID` (names as in the pinned robot_navigator.py L383/L408; the rendered Simple Commander docs page shows them as `dockRobot(dock_pose, dock_type)`/`dockRobot(dock_id)` — code trusted, docs conflict noted, re-checked 2026-11-03), `undockRobot`, `cancelTask`, `isTaskComplete`, `getFeedback`, `getResult`, `waitUntilNav2Active`, `lifecycleStartup` (calls every `ManageLifecycleNodes` service with STARTUP), `lifecycleShutdown` (SHUTDOWN) | [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_simple_commander/nav2_simple_commander/robot_navigator.py#L75-L110, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_simple_commander/nav2_simple_commander/robot_navigator.py#L155-L460, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_simple_commander/nav2_simple_commander/robot_navigator.py#L735-L773, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://docs.nav2.org/rolling/configuration_and_development/simple_commander_api/simple_commander_api/, acc. 2026-10-31] |
| 4.8 | **AssistedTeleop** | action `assisted_teleop` (`nav2_msgs/action/AssistedTeleop`, goal `time_allowance`); input topic parameter `cmd_vel_teleop` (default `cmd_vel_teleop`); `preempt_teleop` (`std_msgs/msg/Empty`) | Human twist is collision-filtered and republished on `cmd_vel` → `cmd_vel_nav` → smoother → collision monitor → `cmd_vel` | Section 3.4 citations |
| 4.9 | **Speed limit** | topic `speed_limit` (`nav2_msgs/msg/SpeedLimit`: `percentage`, `speed_limit`, 0.0 = no limit) | Forwarded to `setSpeedLimit` of every controller plugin; normally fed by the costmap SpeedFilter, which TB4 does not configure (no `speed_filter`/`keepout_filter` layers in `nav2.yaml`) | [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_msgs/msg/SpeedLimit.msg, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_controller/src/controller_server.cpp#L836-L840, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/config/nav2.yaml#L146, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/config/nav2.yaml#L194, acc. 2026-10-31] |
| 4.10 | **Controller / planner selector** | topics `controller_selector`, `planner_selector` (`std_msgs/msg/String`, KeepLast(1) reliable transient-local) | Changes the plugin id used by the BT; only `FollowPath`/`GridBased` exist on TB4 | [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_behavior_tree/plugins/action/controller_selector_node.cpp#L41-L52, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_bt_navigator/behavior_trees/navigate_to_pose_w_replanning_and_recovery.xml#L11-L12, acc. 2026-10-31] |
| 4.11 | **Lifecycle control** | services `/lifecycle_manager_navigation/manage_nodes`, `/lifecycle_manager_localization/manage_nodes` (`nav2_msgs/srv/ManageLifecycleNodes`), `.../is_active` | PAUSE deactivates all servers (motion stops because controller/behaviors deactivate); RESUME re-activates | [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_lifecycle_manager/src/lifecycle_manager.cpp#L64-L74, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_msgs/srv/ManageLifecycleNodes.srv, acc. 2026-10-31] |
| 4.12 | **Path validity query** | service `is_path_valid` (`nav2_msgs/srv/IsPathValid`) | Read-only query; no control effect | [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_planner/src/planner_server.cpp#L201-L202, acc. 2026-10-31] |
| 4.13 | **Collision monitor toggle** | service `/collision_monitor/toggle` (`nav2_msgs/srv/Toggle`) | Disables the software safety layer | [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_collision_monitor/src/collision_monitor_node.cpp#L88-L90, acc. 2026-10-31] |
| 4.14 | **Direct behavior calls** | actions `spin`, `backup`, `drive_on_heading`, `wait`, `dock_robot`, `undock_robot` | A human-triggered fixed primitive; see Uncovered cases | Section 3.4, 3.7 citations |

Observation consequence: inputs 4.1, 4.3, 4.4, 4.7 (except `setInitialPose`), 4.8 (the goal), 4.11–4.14 travel over action or service requests, which a passive topic subscriber does not see; only their effects (action `_action/status`, `_action/feedback`, lifecycle `transition_event`) are visible. Inputs 4.2, 4.5, 4.6, 4.9, 4.10 and the teleop twist of 4.8 are topics and are directly observable.

## 5. Data and logs: interfaces, log and bag formats, rates, storage, retention

_Lead note._ Nothing on the robot records anything by default: the base keeps only a volatile system log (lost at power loss), the Pi runs no recorder (only the self-test tool spawns `ros2 bag record -a`), and Nav2 writes no file. rosbag2 (Jazzy 0.26.11, MCAP default) is the only recorder and it does not store publisher identity. Firmware topic rates are documented for some topics and UNKNOWN for others (listed in Unknowns).

### 5. Data interfaces (TB4 layer)  _[TB4 stack track]_

**Topics (robot namespace, Jazzy; manual table + code):** `turtlebot4_node`: pub `hmi/display`, `ip`, `function_calls`; sub `battery_state`, `hmi/buttons`, `hmi/display/message`, `hmi/led`, `interface_buttons`, `joy`, `wheel_status` (+ `dock_status` in code) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/software/turtlebot4_common.md#L583, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/src/turtlebot4.cpp#L138, acc. 2026-10-31]. Republished Create 3 topics: `battery_state`, `dock_status`, `imu`, `interface_buttons`, `odom`, `tf`, `tf_static`, `wheel_status`; commands `cmd_vel`/`cmd_vel_unstamped`, `cmd_audio`, `cmd_lightring` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/config/republisher.yaml#L10-L11, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/config/republisher.yaml#L27-L30, acc. 2026-10-31]. Sensors: `scan` (rplidar), `oakd/rgb/preview/image_raw` and other depthai topics [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/launch/rplidar.launch.py#L25, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_diagnostics/turtlebot4_diagnostics/diagnostics_updater.py#L82, acc. 2026-10-31]. Nav2: `cmd_vel_nav`, `cmd_vel_smoothed`, `collision_monitor_state`, costmaps, plans (Section 3.4).

**turtlebot4_msgs:** `UserButton`: `bool[4] button` (true = pressed) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_msgs/msg/UserButton.msg#L4, acc. 2026-10-31]; `UserDisplay`: `string ip`, `string battery`, `string[5] entries`, `int32 selected_entry` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_msgs/msg/UserDisplay.msg#L2-L5, acc. 2026-10-31]; `UserLed`: constants `USER_LED_1=0`, `USER_LED_2=1`, `COLOR_OFF/GREEN/RED/YELLOW = 0..3`, fields `uint8 led`, `uint8 color`, `uint32 blink_period` (ms), `float64 duty_cycle` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_msgs/msg/UserLed.msg#L9, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_msgs/msg/UserLed.msg#L20, acc. 2026-10-31].

**rosbag2 in the manual and code.** The manual uses rosbag only in the ROS 2 tests: "A rosbag is also recorded for the duration of the test" into `~/turtlebot4_test_results/Y_m_d-H_M_S` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/troubleshooting/ros2_tests.md#L22, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://turtlebot.github.io/turtlebot4-user-manual/troubleshooting/ros2_tests.html, acc. 2026-10-31]; the code runs `ros2 bag record -o <results_dir>/rosbag2/<test>-<n> -a` as a subprocess and kills it when the test ends [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_tests/turtlebot4_tests/test_tools.py#L108, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_tests/turtlebot4_tests/test_tools.py#L117, acc. 2026-10-31]. No tutorial records bags for teleop or navigation. Default storage format in Jazzy: rosbag2 0.26.x README states "The default is `mcap`" and `get_default_storage_id()` returns `"mcap"` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros2/rosbag2/blob/cb8fa198b93bfed90e2417b52b8645fb9ef89c2b/README.md#L517, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros2/rosbag2/blob/cb8fa198b93bfed90e2417b52b8645fb9ef89c2b/rosbag2_storage/src/rosbag2_storage/default_storage_id.cpp#L21-L24, acc. 2026-10-31]; sqlite3 remains selectable with `--storage sqlite3` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros2/rosbag2/blob/cb8fa198b93bfed90e2417b52b8645fb9ef89c2b/README.md#L524, acc. 2026-10-31].

**What the Pi stores by default.** The bringup runs as a `robot_upstart` systemd job `turtlebot4.service` installed by `turtlebot4_setup` (launch file `launch/<model>.launch.py`, after `network-online.target`) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_setup/blob/5fe58657aeff57d28cb2cfb4f0c6cc6c62a4b96c/turtlebot4_setup/ros_setup.py#L353, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_setup/blob/5fe58657aeff57d28cb2cfb4f0c6cc6c62a4b96c/turtlebot4_setup/ros_setup.py#L358, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_setup/blob/5fe58657aeff57d28cb2cfb4f0c6cc6c62a4b96c/turtlebot4_setup/ros_setup.py#L363, acc. 2026-10-31]; its stdout/stderr go to the journal (`sudo journalctl -u turtlebot4 -r`) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/software/turtlebot4_robot.md#L288, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://turtlebot.github.io/turtlebot4-user-manual/software/turtlebot4_robot.html, acc. 2026-10-31]. Nothing in turtlebot4_setup sets `ROS_LOG_DIR` or launches a recorder (grep of conf.py, setup.bash, ros_setup.py: no `ROS_LOG_DIR`, no `bag`) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_setup/blob/5fe58657aeff57d28cb2cfb4f0c6cc6c62a4b96c/etc/turtlebot4/setup.bash#L1, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_setup/blob/5fe58657aeff57d28cb2cfb4f0c6cc6c62a4b96c/turtlebot4_setup/conf.py#L106, acc. 2026-10-31]; ROS 2 node logs therefore follow the rclcpp default (`~/.ros/log`), which is not configured by TB4 [UNKNOWN: actual on-disk retention of ~/.ros/log under the systemd user — depends on robot_upstart/launch defaults; not verified]. Test results and test bags persist under `~/turtlebot4_test_results` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/troubleshooting/ros2_tests.md#L22, acc. 2026-10-31]. SLAM maps are saved to the current directory of the machine that calls `save_map` (PC or Pi) as `.pgm` + `.yaml` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/tutorials/generate_map.md#L155, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/tutorials/generate_map.md#L159, acc. 2026-10-31]. Retention policy: [UNKNOWN: none defined; journald default rotation applies (the SD-image build script runs journalctl --vacuum)] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/tutorials/create_sd_image.md#L19-L19, acc. 2026-10-31].

### 5. Interfaces the firmware exposes (base part)  _[Create 3 base track]_

**Topics** (`ros2 topic list -t` on the robot) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/ros2.md#L14; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/ros2.md#L37; https://iroboteducation.github.io/create3_docs/api/ros2/, acc. 2026-10-31], with documented rates:

| Topic | Type | Rate | Fields / constants (from `.msg`) |
|---|---|---|---|

`/stall_status`, listed in the TurtleBot 4 manual [CLAIM: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/software/sensors.md#L137; https://turtlebot.github.io/turtlebot4-user-manual/software/sensors.html, acc. 2026-10-31], does not exist in the firmware topic list [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/ros2.md#L15, acc. 2026-10-31]; stalls are reported as `HazardDetection.STALL`. Docs-vs-docs rate conflict: odometry.md states 62.5 Hz for `wheel_*`/`mouse`, while H.2.5 throttles them to a 25 ms minimum period (40 Hz) by default [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/releases/h_2_5.md#L33, acc. 2026-10-31]; the later release note is taken as authoritative.

https://github.com/iRobotEducation/irobot_create_msgs/blob/b4986ef5dc2e07d8aa7471f29c56b617473352c5/srv/EStop.srv#L3; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/irobot_create_msgs/blob/b4986ef5dc2e07d8aa7471f29c56b617473352c5/srv/EStop.srv#L4, acc. 2026-10-31]; `/robot_power` (`RobotPower`: empty → `success, message`; "robot may power down before response is transmitted") [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/irobot_create_msgs/blob/b4986ef5dc2e07d8aa7471f29c56b617473352c5/srv/RobotPower.srv#L1-L5; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/irobot_create_msgs/blob/b4986ef5dc2e07d8aa7471f29c56b617473352c5/action/WallFollow.action#L12, acc. 2026-10-31]; `LedAnimation` (`BLINK_LIGHTS=1, SPIN_LIGHTS=2`, `lightring`, `max_runtime`) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/irobot_create_msgs/blob/b4986ef5dc2e07d8aa7471f29c56b617473352c5/action/LedAnimation.action#L3; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/irobot_create_msgs/blob/b4986ef5dc2e07d8aa7471f29c56b617473352c5/action/LedAnimation.action#L4, acc. 2026-10-31]; `AudioNoteSequence` (`INFINITE=-1`, `iterations 1`, `note_sequence`) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/irobot_create_msgs/blob/b4986ef5dc2e07d8aa7471f29c56b617473352c5/action/AudioNoteSequence.action#L2-L3; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/irobot_create_msgs/blob/b4986ef5dc2e07d8aa7471f29c56b617473352c5/action/AudioNoteSequence.action#L6, acc. 2026-10-31]. `LightringLeds`: `LedColor[6] leds`, `bool override_system` ("Set to false to return control of lights to system") [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/irobot_create_msgs/blob/b4986ef5dc2e07d8aa7471f29c56b617473352c5/msg/LightringLeds.msg#L11; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/irobot_create_msgs/blob/b4986ef5dc2e07d8aa7471f29c56b617473352c5/msg/LightringLeds.msg#L14, acc. 2026-10-31].

**QoS.** The only firmware QoS statement is that the `/cmd_audio` subscription became `reliable` in G.4.4 / H.1.1, so best-effort publishers cannot reach it [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/ui.md#L43; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/releases/g_4_4.md#L17, acc. 2026-10-31]. QoS of the firmware publishers is otherwise [UNKNOWN: not documented]; iRobot's own example subscribers use `rclcpp::SensorDataQoS()` (best-effort, volatile) for `dock_status`, `hazard_detection`, `ir_opcode`, `odom`, `kidnap_status` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_examples/blob/fe3d408a266b755b94cb7334a92f2d58dd661c13/create3_coverage/src/create3_coverage_node.cpp#L52; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_examples/blob/fe3d408a266b755b94cb7334a92f2d58dd661c13/create3_coverage/src/create3_coverage_node.cpp#L57; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_examples/blob/fe3d408a266b755b94cb7334a92f2d58dd661c13/create3_coverage/src/create3_coverage_node.cpp#L62; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_examples/blob/fe3d408a266b755b94cb7334a92f2d58dd661c13/create3_coverage/src/create3_coverage_node.cpp#L67; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_examples/blob/fe3d408a266b755b94cb7334a92f2d58dd661c13/create3_coverage/src/create3_coverage_node.cpp#L72, acc. 2026-10-31], and the republisher subscribes `QoS(1)` volatile best-effort to all robot topics [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_examples/blob/fe3d408a266b755b94cb7334a92f2d58dd661c13/create3_republisher/republisher.cpp#L155, acc. 2026-10-31] — a best-effort/volatile subscription is compatible with any publisher, which is the correct choice for a passive observer. Parameter-event QoS overrides are exposed per node [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/ros2.md#L120, acc. 2026-10-31].

**Logging and uploads on the base.** The webserver Logs page shows the robot's system log; "the logs are lost when the robot loses power"; raw text at `/logs-raw` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/webserver/logs.md#L2; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/webserver/logs.md#L6; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/webserver/logs.md#L7; https://iroboteducation.github.io/create3_docs/webserver/logs/, acc. 2026-10-31]. A system monitor node reports CPU and RAM usage to the log and checks for multiple robots on the same topic (G.3.1) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/releases/g_3_1.md#L45, acc. 2026-10-31]; `/system_health` has a `log_period` parameter [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/ros2.md#L153, acc. 2026-10-31] (default [UNKNOWN]). Retention beyond "until power loss": [UNKNOWN]. Uploads: when on the Internet the robot can download firmware from iRobot's web site on user request [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/webserver/update.md#L8, acc. 2026-10-31], and it syncs to a global NTP server [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/setup/compute-ntp.md#L7, acc. 2026-10-31]; the robot "will not connect to the iRobot Home App" [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/faq/faq.md#L204, acc. 2026-10-31]. No statement about telemetry or analytics exists in the pinned docs — [UNKNOWN: whether the firmware sends any telemetry]. No `rosbag`/recording facility exists on the base (none in topics/services/webserver) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/ros2.md#L14; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/webserver/overview.md#L18, acc. 2026-10-31].

### 5. Interfaces Nav2 exposes on TurtleBot 4 (exact names and types)  _[Nav2 track]_

Namespace prefix: none by default (`namespace` launch argument empty) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/launch/nav2.launch.py#L44-L45, acc. 2026-10-31]; with a namespace every name below is prefixed.

#### 5.1 Action servers and their introspection topics

Every ROS 2 action server `<a>` exposes the hidden topics `<a>/_action/status` (`action_msgs/msg/GoalStatusArray`) and `<a>/_action/feedback`, plus the services `<a>/_action/send_goal`, `<a>/_action/cancel_goal`, `<a>/_action/get_result`. `GoalStatus.status` values: `STATUS_UNKNOWN=0, STATUS_ACCEPTED=1, STATUS_EXECUTING=2, STATUS_CANCELING=3, STATUS_SUCCEEDED=4, STATUS_CANCELED=5, STATUS_ABORTED=6` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros2/rcl_interfaces/blob/7aa3caf43377ea6ad615bc1040832e2c7566bfbe/action_msgs/msg/GoalStatus.msg, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros2/rcl_interfaces/blob/7aa3caf43377ea6ad615bc1040832e2c7566bfbe/action_msgs/msg/GoalStatusArray.msg, acc. 2026-10-31]. The rcl_action default status QoS is `KEEP_LAST, depth 1, RELIABLE, TRANSIENT_LOCAL` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros2/rcl/blob/18d96c9c2b98e616324ae9a2c7701dd0b0886ff0/rcl_action/include/rcl_action/default_qos.h#L26-L37, acc. 2026-10-31] and the action server applies it as `status_topic_qos` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros2/rcl/blob/18d96c9c2b98e616324ae9a2c7701dd0b0886ff0/rcl_action/src/rcl_action/action_server.c#L311, acc. 2026-10-31].

| Action name | Server | Type (result error codes) | Feedback fields |
|---|---|---|---|
| `/navigate_to_pose` | bt_navigator | `nav2_msgs/action/NavigateToPose` (`NONE=0`; `error_code` is the lowest non-zero of `compute_path_error_code`, `follow_path_error_code`) | `current_pose`, `navigation_time`, `estimated_time_remaining`, `number_of_recoveries` (int16), `distance_remaining` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_msgs/action/NavigateToPose.action, acc. 2026-10-31] |
| `/navigate_through_poses` | bt_navigator | `nav2_msgs/action/NavigateThroughPoses` | as above + `number_of_poses_remaining` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_msgs/action/NavigateThroughPoses.action, acc. 2026-10-31] |
| `/follow_waypoints`, `/follow_gps_waypoints` | waypoint_follower | `nav2_msgs/action/FollowWaypoints` (`UNKNOWN=600`, `TASK_EXECUTOR_FAILED=601`; `missed_waypoints[]`) | `current_waypoint` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_msgs/action/FollowWaypoints.action, acc. 2026-10-31] |
| `/follow_path` | controller_server | `nav2_msgs/action/FollowPath` (`UNKNOWN=100, INVALID_CONTROLLER=101, TF_ERROR=102, INVALID_PATH=103, PATIENCE_EXCEEDED=104, FAILED_TO_MAKE_PROGRESS=105, NO_VALID_CONTROL=106, CONTROLLER_TIMED_OUT=107`) | `distance_to_goal`, `speed` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_msgs/action/FollowPath.action, acc. 2026-10-31] |
| `/compute_path_to_pose`, `/compute_path_through_poses` | planner_server | `nav2_msgs/action/ComputePathToPose` (`UNKNOWN=200, INVALID_PLANNER=201, TF_ERROR=202, START_OUTSIDE_MAP=203, GOAL_OUTSIDE_MAP=204, START_OCCUPIED=205, GOAL_OCCUPIED=206, TIMEOUT=207, NO_VALID_PATH=208`) | none [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_msgs/action/ComputePathToPose.action, acc. 2026-10-31] |
| `/smooth_path` | smoother_server | `nav2_msgs/action/SmoothPath` | — (not used by the default BT) |
| `/spin` | behavior_server | `nav2_msgs/action/Spin` (`UNKNOWN=700, TIMEOUT=701, TF_ERROR=702, COLLISION_AHEAD=703`) | `angular_distance_traveled` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_msgs/action/Spin.action, acc. 2026-10-31] |
| `/backup` | behavior_server | `nav2_msgs/action/BackUp` (`UNKNOWN=710, TIMEOUT=711, TF_ERROR=712, INVALID_INPUT=713, COLLISION_AHEAD=714`) | `distance_traveled` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_msgs/action/BackUp.action, acc. 2026-10-31] |
| `/drive_on_heading` | behavior_server | `nav2_msgs/action/DriveOnHeading` (`UNKNOWN=720, TIMEOUT=721, TF_ERROR=722, COLLISION_AHEAD=723, INVALID_INPUT=724`) | `distance_traveled` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_msgs/action/DriveOnHeading.action, acc. 2026-10-31] |
| `/wait` | behavior_server | `nav2_msgs/action/Wait` (goal `time`; no named error codes) | `time_left` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_msgs/action/Wait.action, acc. 2026-10-31] |
| `/assisted_teleop` | behavior_server | `nav2_msgs/action/AssistedTeleop` (`UNKNOWN=730, TIMEOUT=731, TF_ERROR=732`; goal `time_allowance`) | `current_teleop_duration` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_msgs/action/AssistedTeleop.action, acc. 2026-10-31] |
| `/dock_robot`, `/undock_robot` | docking_server | `nav2_msgs/action/DockRobot` (`DOCK_NOT_IN_DB=901, DOCK_NOT_VALID=902, FAILED_TO_STAGE=903, FAILED_TO_DETECT_DOCK=904, FAILED_TO_CONTROL=905, FAILED_TO_CHARGE=906, UNKNOWN=999`), `UndockRobot` | `state` (`NAV_TO_STAGING_POSE=1, INITIAL_PERCEPTION=2, CONTROLLING=3, WAIT_FOR_CHARGE=4, RETRY=5`), `docking_time`, `num_retries` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_msgs/action/DockRobot.action, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_msgs/action/UndockRobot.action, acc. 2026-10-31] |

The error-code ranges are disjoint per server (100s controller, 200s planner, 600s waypoint follower, 700s behaviors, 900s docking), so a result's `error_code` identifies the failing layer without further context [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_msgs/action/FollowPath.action, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_msgs/action/ComputePathToPose.action, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_msgs/action/Spin.action, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_msgs/action/DockRobot.action, acc. 2026-10-31].

#### 5.2 Topics

| Topic | Type | Publisher → subscriber | Rate / QoS | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `/behavior_tree_log` | `nav2_msgs/msg/BehaviorTreeLog` (`timestamp`; `event_log[]` of `BehaviorTreeStatusChange`: `timestamp`, `node_name`, `uid`, `previous_status`, `current_status` in {IDLE, RUNNING, SUCCESS, FAILURE}) | `bt_navigator_navigate_to_pose_rclcpp_node` (and the `_navigate_through_poses_` twin) → anyone | one message per BT loop in which a status changed; `rclcpp::QoS(10)` = KeepLast(10), reliable, volatile | [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_msgs/msg/BehaviorTreeLog.msg, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_msgs/msg/BehaviorTreeStatusChange.msg, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_behavior_tree/include/nav2_behavior_tree/ros_topic_logger.hpp#L49-L51, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_behavior_tree/include/nav2_behavior_tree/ros_topic_logger.hpp#L91-L100, acc. 2026-10-31] |
| `/<node>/transition_event` for each of the 12 lifecycle nodes | `lifecycle_msgs/msg/TransitionEvent` | rcl_lifecycle → anyone | event-driven; `rcl_publisher_get_default_options()` | [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros2/rcl/blob/18d96c9c2b98e616324ae9a2c7701dd0b0886ff0/rcl_lifecycle/src/com_interface.c#L40, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros2/rcl/blob/18d96c9c2b98e616324ae9a2c7701dd0b0886ff0/rcl_lifecycle/src/com_interface.c#L110-L113, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros2/rcl_interfaces/blob/7aa3caf43377ea6ad615bc1040832e2c7566bfbe/lifecycle_msgs/msg/TransitionEvent.msg, acc. 2026-10-31] |
| `/bond` | bond status | every Nav2 server ↔ lifecycle manager | 10 Hz per server | [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_util/src/lifecycle_node.cpp#L73-L82, acc. 2026-10-31] |
| `/collision_monitor_state` | `nav2_msgs/msg/CollisionMonitorState` | collision_monitor | on change only; KeepLast(1) | Section 3.6 |
| `/cmd_vel_nav` | `geometry_msgs/msg/TwistStamped` | controller_server, behavior_server → velocity_smoother | 20 Hz / 10 Hz; KeepLast(1) | Section 3.3–3.5 |
| `/cmd_vel_smoothed` | `geometry_msgs/msg/TwistStamped` | velocity_smoother → collision_monitor | 20 Hz timer, stops after timeout; KeepLast(1) | Section 3.5 |
| `/cmd_vel` | `geometry_msgs/msg/TwistStamped` | collision_monitor, docking_server → TB4 bridge (TB4 track) | follows input; KeepLast(1) | Section 3.6, 3.7 |
| `/plan` | `nav_msgs/msg/Path` | planner_server | per plan (1 Hz) | [CLAIM: topic name from Nav2 convention; publisher line not read in this track] |
| `/goal_pose` | `geometry_msgs/msg/PoseStamped` | RViz SetGoal / scripts → bt_navigator | event | Section 4.2 |
| `/initialpose` | `geometry_msgs/msg/PoseWithCovarianceStamped` | RViz / BasicNavigator → amcl | event | Section 4.5 |
| `/clicked_point` | `geometry_msgs/msg/PointStamped` | RViz → (none in TB4 Nav2) | event | Section 4.6 |
| `/speed_limit` | `nav2_msgs/msg/SpeedLimit` | (speed filter / operator) → controller_server | event; QoS(10) | Section 4.9 |
| `/controller_selector`, `/planner_selector` | `std_msgs/msg/String` | operator → BT | event; KeepLast(1) reliable transient-local | Section 4.10 |
| `/cmd_vel_teleop`, `/preempt_teleop` | `TwistStamped`/`Twist`; `std_msgs/msg/Empty` | teleop → behavior_server AssistedTeleop | publisher-defined | Section 3.4 |
| `/local_costmap/published_footprint` | `geometry_msgs/msg/PolygonStamped` | local_costmap → collision_monitor (FootprintApproach) | 2 Hz publish | [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/config/nav2.yaml#L312, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/config/nav2.yaml#L131, acc. 2026-10-31] |

#### 5.3 Services relevant to control authority

`/lifecycle_manager_navigation/manage_nodes`, `/lifecycle_manager_navigation/is_active`, `/lifecycle_manager_localization/manage_nodes`, `/lifecycle_manager_localization/is_active` (Section 3.1); `/<node>/get_state`, `/<node>/change_state` on every lifecycle node (rcl_lifecycle standard, type `lifecycle_msgs/srv/GetState`, `ChangeState`) [CLAIM: standard lifecycle services, names not re-read in rcl at the pin]; `/collision_monitor/toggle` (`nav2_msgs/srv/Toggle`); `/is_path_valid`; `/local_costmap/clear_entirely_local_costmap`, `/global_costmap/clear_entirely_global_costmap` (called by the BT `ClearEntireCostmap` nodes) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_bt_navigator/behavior_trees/navigate_to_pose_w_replanning_and_recovery.xml#L18, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_bt_navigator/behavior_trees/navigate_to_pose_w_replanning_and_recovery.xml#L26, acc. 2026-10-31].

#### 5.4 Logging to disk

Nothing in the TB4 `nav2.yaml` or in `navigation_launch.py` writes a log file: the only logging-related items are `--log-level` arguments and `RCUTILS_LOGGING_BUFFERED_STREAM=1` for stdout [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_bringup/launch/navigation_launch.py#L77-L79, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_bringup/launch/navigation_launch.py#L121-L123, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/config/nav2.yaml, acc. 2026-10-31]. The BT topic logger writes to a topic, and its `RCLCPP_DEBUG` line goes to the console logger only [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_behavior_tree/include/nav2_behavior_tree/ros_topic_logger.hpp#L78-L86, acc. 2026-10-31]. Groot monitoring is off by default (`enable_groot_monitoring` false, port 1667) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_bt_navigator/src/navigators/navigate_to_pose.cpp#L54-L64, acc. 2026-10-31]. Therefore the only persistent record of a Nav2 session is whatever `ros2 bag record` (rosbag2) captures of the topics above; action goal requests and service calls are not topics and are not recorded unless service introspection is enabled (not configured here). [Absence of file logging: VERIFIED by inspection of the two files above; the claim "Nav2 writes nothing to disk by default" is otherwise an inference from the code read in this track.]

## 6. Control-authority signals

_Lead note._ Seventy-two signals are catalogued in `signal_map.yaml` with name, source, type, rate, QoS where known, taxonomy mapping (state / reason / scope / event type / actor), uncovered cases, reliability, passive observation, how it could be faked, tag, URLs and version. The consolidated index follows; the per-track blocks give the detailed tables and the attribution rules. All motion signals apply to scope `base` (Section 14E). The mappings use only the v0.2 enum values; where a track annotated an event type, the annotation was moved to a `note` field.

### 6.0 Consolidated signal index (from `signal_map.yaml`)

### 6. Control-authority signals from the base (base part)  _[Create 3 base track]_

Details, mappings and faking notes are in `base_signals.yaml` (ids B1–B20). Summary table (rates as documented; "thr." = subject to `raw_kinematics_min_pub_period_ms`, default 25 ms [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/releases/h_2_5.md#L33, acc. 2026-10-31]):

| Id | Signal | Type | Rate | What it tells the meter | Reliability / how missed | Taxonomy use |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| B1/B2 | `/cmd_vel`, `/cmd_vel_stamped` | Twist / TwistStamped | commander's | a velocity was requested; executed only if no action goal and no e-stop and within 0.5 s [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/moving-the-robot.md#L26; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/moving-the-robot.md#L49; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/safety.md#L80, acc. 2026-10-31] | no source identity in message; DDS publisher node name is the only attribution | `ai` / `human_*` by publisher identity + device registry |
| B3 | `/wheel_vels` (with `/odom`, `/wheel_ticks`) | WheelVels | 62.5 Hz, thr. | actual wheel motion; "executed velocity" proxy (no dedicated cmd_vel echo topic exists [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/ros2.md#L15, acc. 2026-10-31]) | can be turned off (`-1`) | detect reflexes, pushes, hidden paths → `unknown` |
| B4 | `/hazard_detection` | HazardDetectionVector | on change | BUMP/CLIFF/STALL/WHEEL_DROP/BACKUP_LIMIT/OBJECT_PROXIMITY [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/irobot_create_msgs/blob/b4986ef5dc2e07d8aa7471f29c56b617473352c5/msg/HazardDetection.msg#L8, acc. 2026-10-31] | event-driven; publication can be disabled | `safety_stop` when BACKUP_LIMIT + backward command, or goal cancelled on CLIFF/STALL [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/drive-goals.md#L15, acc. 2026-10-31] |
