| B15 | `/battery_state` | BatteryState | ≥ 0.1 Hz | charge; <2 % → firmware powers off [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/hw/electrical.md#L15, acc. 2026-10-31] | slow | `idle` on dock; power-off = end of observation |
| B16 | `/robot_power` | service | event | power off [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/irobot_create_msgs/blob/b4986ef5dc2e07d8aa7471f29c56b617473352c5/srv/RobotPower.srv#L1-L5, acc. 2026-10-31] | unobservable | end of observation |
| B17 | USB/BLE toggle | hardware | — | hidden BLE control plane [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/hw/adapter.md#L32, acc. 2026-10-31] | invisible | `unknown` / undeclared device → `human_remote` |
| B18 | webserver | http | — | boot defaults, restarts [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/webserver/application.md#L6, acc. 2026-10-31] | invisible | `unknown` gaps |
| B19 | application restart on connectivity change | firmware | — | topic gaps [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/releases/g_2_2.md#L51, acc. 2026-10-31] | — | `unknown` |
| B20 | `/rosout`, webserver log | log | event | corroboration (backup-limit warning [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/safety.md#L44-L45, acc. 2026-10-31]) | lost at power loss [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/webserver/logs.md#L6, acc. 2026-10-31] | corroboration only |

Reflex activity has no topic. Inference rule proposed for the lead: a reflex episode = a BUMP/CLIFF/STALL/WHEEL_DROP detection present (B4) while `|wheel_vels| > 0` (B3) and the last commanded `cmd_vel` is zero or opposite in sign (B1/B2); the episode ends when the hazard disappears or wheel_vels returns to the commanded sign. This follows from the documented facts that reflexes override user commands [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/reflexes.md#L5, acc. 2026-10-31] and move the robot away from the hazard [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/reflexes.md#L19; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/reflexes.md#L27, acc. 2026-10-31]; it is an inference, not a documented signal.

### 6. Signals that show who is in control (TB4 layer)  _[TB4 stack track]_

Full per-signal detail (mapping, fakes, QoS) is in `stack_signals.yaml` (T1-T22). Summary table:

| Signal | Topic / type | Rate | Tells you | Miss / fake | Taxonomy |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| T1 `/cmd_vel` TwistStamped | merged command from all sources [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/launch/robot.launch.py#L87, acc. 2026-10-31] | 20 Hz from Nav2; event-driven from humans | that *someone* commands the base; publisher identity is the only discriminator | rosbag2 does not store publisher identity (below); any node can publish | `ai` / `human_*` / `shared` by publisher |
| T2 `/cmd_vel_nav` | controller_server + behavior_server output [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_bringup/launch/navigation_launch.py#L137, acc. 2026-10-31] | 20 Hz | Nav2 is actively commanding | spoofable | `ai` |
| T3 `/cmd_vel_smoothed` | velocity_smoother output [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/config/nav2.yaml#L299, acc. 2026-10-31] | 20 Hz | payload equality with `/cmd_vel` attributes a message to Nav2 | spoofable | `ai` |
| T4 `/collision_monitor_state` | nav2_msgs/CollisionMonitorState [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/config/nav2.yaml#L301, acc. 2026-10-31] | per cmd | Nav2 path blocked/slowed | only filters Nav2 path | `safety_stop` (Nav2 path only) |
| T5 `/joy` sensor_msgs/Joy | joy_linux_node [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-drivers/joystick_drivers/blob/038a12722bac3983d7fee462ad074a8f18c7bd5f/joy_linux/src/joy_linux_node.cpp#L204, acc. 2026-10-31] | on change / 20 Hz autorepeat [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-drivers/joystick_drivers/blob/038a12722bac3983d7fee462ad074a8f18c7bd5f/joy_linux/src/joy_linux_node.cpp#L215, acc. 2026-10-31] | controller in use; L1/R1 (buttons 4/5) = dead-man [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros2/teleop_twist_joy/blob/556a07100ee745f68619de84a95d3c84a4892ab9/src/teleop_twist_joy.cpp#L330-L333, acc. 2026-10-31] | invisible if Pi hides the topic (discovery server) or controller not paired | `human_local` (declared local device) |
| T6 `/function_calls` std_msgs/String | turtlebot4_node [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/src/turtlebot4.cpp#L154, acc. 2026-10-31] | per press/select | which function a human triggered (Dock, Undock, EStop, Wall Follow, Power, menu) | function name only, no source button; spoofable | `shared`/`safety_stop`/`session_end` per function |
| T7 `/interface_buttons` | Create 3 faceplate buttons [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/config/republisher.yaml#L16, acc. 2026-10-31] | firmware rate (Create 3 track) | physical presence of a local human | spoofable | `human_local` |
| T8 `/hmi/buttons` UserButton | turtlebot4_base GPIO 100 Hz [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_base/src/turtlebot4.cpp#L62, acc. 2026-10-31] | 100 Hz | local human at the UI board | Standard only | `human_local` (UI event) |
| T9 `/hmi/display` UserDisplay | turtlebot4_node display [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/src/display.cpp#L49, acc. 2026-10-31] | <= 1 Hz forced | menu state | display only | not a motion scope |
| T10 `/hmi/led/_motors`, `_comms` | LED mirrors of wheel_status [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/src/turtlebot4.cpp#L415, acc. 2026-10-31] | 50 ms timer | wheels disabled; Create 3 silent > 30 s | derived | `safety_stop` / `unknown` |
| T11 `/wheel_status` | Create 3 wheels_enabled [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/src/turtlebot4.cpp#L406, acc. 2026-10-31] | firmware rate | e-stop in effect | cause ambiguous | `safety_stop` |
| T12 `/dock_status` | is_docked [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/config/republisher.yaml#L13, acc. 2026-10-31] | firmware rate | docked (idle) / docking under way | — | `idle` / `shared` / `ai` by trigger |
| T13 hazards, kidnap, stop_status | **not republished by default** [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/config/republisher.yaml#L12-L14, acc. 2026-10-31] | — | firmware safety reflexes invisible | config-dependent | `safety_stop` unobservable |
| T14 `/initialpose`, `/clicked_point`, GoalTool | RViz on PC [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_desktop/blob/d831a5a3d2323d2db1ce79e5b909beecbeed3dcf/turtlebot4_viz/rviz/navigation.rviz#L732, acc. 2026-10-31] | per click | human hints / task definition | spoofable | `shared` hint / task_start |
| T15 Create 3 actions status | `/dock`, `/undock`, `/wall_follow`, `/drive_distance`, `/rotate_angle` `_action/status` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/config/republisher.yaml#L38-L41, acc. 2026-10-31] | per goal | firmware behaviour running | hidden topics | `shared` / `ai` / `scripted` |
| T16 `/e_stop`, `/robot_power` services | turtlebot4_node clients [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/src/turtlebot4.cpp#L164, acc. 2026-10-31] | per call | motor cut / power off | not observable as topics | `safety_stop` / `session_end` |
| T17 `/odom`, `/tf` | Create 3 odometry [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/config/republisher.yaml#L21, acc. 2026-10-31] | firmware rate | motion without observed command = hidden path | — | heartbeat `distance_m`; `unknown` on unexplained motion |
| T18 `/battery_state` | Create 3 [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/config/republisher.yaml#L11, acc. 2026-10-31] | firmware rate | auto power-off trigger (<=12 %, 60 s) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/src/turtlebot4.cpp#L351-L354, acc. 2026-10-31] | — | `session_end` |
| T19 `/diagnostics_agg` | aggregator [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_diagnostics/launch/diagnostics.launch.py#L41, acc. 2026-10-31] | ~1 Hz (UNKNOWN exact) | summary only | disabled by env var | supervision telemetry |
| T20 gz Teleop plugin (sim) | gz `/cmd_vel` -> ROS `cmd_vel` via `cmd_vel_bridge` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_simulator/blob/b7d0f3b973258481f6cd8137bef9240fb567f566/turtlebot4_gz_bringup/gui/standard/gui.config#L118, acc. 2026-10-31] | event | GUI teleop | publisher shows as bridge | `human_remote` |
| T21 sim HMI plugin | `hmi/buttons/_set` -> `hmi/buttons` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_simulator/blob/b7d0f3b973258481f6cd8137bef9240fb567f566/turtlebot4_gz_toolbox/src/hmi_node.cpp#L36, acc. 2026-10-31] | event | GUI button | sim only | `human_remote` |
| T22 `/ip` | turtlebot4_node 0.2 Hz [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/src/turtlebot4.cpp#L298-L300, acc. 2026-10-31] | 0.2 Hz | node liveness | — | heartbeat |

**Attributing `/cmd_vel` messages to a publisher.** At run time `ros2 topic info -v /cmd_vel` lists each publisher's node name, namespace, QoS and GID (ROS 2 CLI; the manual uses `ros2 topic list/info` for debugging but does not describe `-v`) [UNKNOWN: ros2cli -v output format not cited from primary text; verified behaviour belongs to ros2cli, not TB4]. In code, a subscription callback receives `rclcpp::MessageInfo` whose `rmw_message_info_t` carries `publisher_gid`, `source_timestamp` and `received_timestamp` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros2/rmw/blob/8cb94a87cf3f4049abe2d563af893517bf336930/rmw/include/rmw/types.h#L634, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros2/rmw/blob/8cb94a87cf3f4049abe2d563af893517bf336930/rmw/include/rmw/types.h#L642, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros2/rmw/blob/8cb94a87cf3f4049abe2d563af893517bf336930/rmw/include/rmw/types.h#L649, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros2/rmw/blob/8cb94a87cf3f4049abe2d563af893517bf336930/rmw/include/rmw/types.h#L714, acc. 2026-10-31]. **rosbag2 does not store the publisher GID**: the recorder writes only `(serialized message, topic, received_timestamp, source_timestamp)` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros2/rosbag2/blob/cb8fa198b93bfed90e2417b52b8645fb9ef89c2b/rosbag2_transport/src/rosbag2_transport/recorder.cpp#L796-L799, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros2/rosbag2/blob/cb8fa198b93bfed90e2417b52b8645fb9ef89c2b/rosbag2_transport/src/rosbag2_transport/recorder.cpp#L801, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros2/rosbag2/blob/cb8fa198b93bfed90e2417b52b8645fb9ef89c2b/rosbag2_transport/src/rosbag2_transport/recorder.cpp#L802, acc. 2026-10-31], and `SerializedBagMessage` has fields `serialized_data`, `recv_timestamp`, `send_timestamp`, `topic_name` only [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros2/rosbag2/blob/cb8fa198b93bfed90e2417b52b8645fb9ef89c2b/rosbag2_storage/include/rosbag2_storage/serialized_bag_message.hpp#L27, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros2/rosbag2/blob/cb8fa198b93bfed90e2417b52b8645fb9ef89c2b/rosbag2_storage/include/rosbag2_storage/serialized_bag_message.hpp#L39, acc. 2026-10-31]; in MCAP these become `logTime` and `publishTime` with `sequence` fixed to 0 [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros2/rosbag2/blob/cb8fa198b93bfed90e2417b52b8645fb9ef89c2b/rosbag2_storage_mcap/src/mcap_storage.cpp#L869, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros2/rosbag2/blob/cb8fa198b93bfed90e2417b52b8645fb9ef89c2b/rosbag2_storage_mcap/src/mcap_storage.cpp#L873, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros2/rosbag2/blob/cb8fa198b93bfed90e2417b52b8645fb9ef89c2b/rosbag2_storage_mcap/src/mcap_storage.cpp#L874, acc. 2026-10-31]. Therefore a bag of `/cmd_vel` alone cannot attribute commands; the observer must (a) record `/cmd_vel_nav`/`/cmd_vel_smoothed` and `/joy` alongside, and/or (b) run its own node that logs `publisher_gid` per message and the GID->node map captured at session start (the Autonomy Meter recipe in `sim_steps.md` does both). Weak but usable discriminator in a plain bag: `TwistStamped.header.frame_id` is `teleop_twist_joy` for the controller [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros2/teleop_twist_joy/blob/556a07100ee745f68619de84a95d3c84a4892ab9/src/teleop_twist_joy.cpp#L94, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros2/teleop_twist_joy/blob/556a07100ee745f68619de84a95d3c84a4892ab9/src/teleop_twist_joy.cpp#L299, acc. 2026-10-31], empty by default for teleop_twist_keyboard [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros2/teleop_twist_keyboard/blob/ed06da483e4acef8d0ab225557d64e6597c9bdf2/teleop_twist_keyboard.py#L144, acc. 2026-10-31], and Nav2's value is the Nav2 track's to report; frame_id is trivially forgeable.

### 6. Control-authority signals and their taxonomy mapping (Nav2 part)  _[Nav2 track]_

Full per-signal detail (rate, QoS, how missed, how faked, passive observation) is in `nav2_signals.yaml` (N1–N24). Summary of the mapping rules applied:

| Signal | Condition | State / event | Reason | Rule applied |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| N1/N2/N3 `/navigate_to_pose/_action/status` etc. | `EXECUTING` | `ai` on `base` | — | Section 2: classical planner+controller choosing actions from sensing = `ai` |
| N1 | `SUCCEEDED` / `ABORTED` | `task_end` outcome `success` / `failure` (if the Nav2 goal is the registered task) | — | Section 5 |
| N1 | `CANCELING`→`CANCELED` by a human client; robot then stands still with the task still open | `shared` | `human_initiated` | 6.8: a hold caused by a human is `shared`; if the registry closes the task at cancel, `task_end` outcome `aborted` instead |
| N4 `/behavior_tree_log` | `FollowPath`/`ComputePathToPose` RUNNING | `ai` | — | as N1 |
| N4 | `Spin`/`BackUp`/`Wait`/`ClearEntireCostmap` RUNNING (recovery subtree) | `ai` (quality flag `x_recovery`) | — | Section 2 rule 5: a controller reacting to sensing is `ai`; recovery is still the AI's own choice. `number_of_recoveries` (N5) is a quality counter, **not** an intervention count |
| N4 | `Wait` RUNNING | `ai` | — | 6.8 row "AI chose to wait as part of its own plan" |
| N4 | `GoalUpdated` SUCCESS during a goal | `shared` only if the new goal came from a declared human device | `human_initiated` | 6.2 hint ("human sets goals while AI executes"), see Uncovered 1 |
| N6 `/assisted_teleop/_action/status` | `EXECUTING` | `shared` | `human_initiated` (or `ai_requested_help` if a BT `AssistedTeleop` node is RUNNING in N4 — not in TB4 default XML) | Section 2 `shared`: human and AI inputs blended (human twist, AI collision filter) |
| N9 `/collision_monitor_state` | `STOP` (TB4: only `"invalid source"` is possible) | `safety_stop` | `safety` | Section 2 `safety_stop`: watchdog/protective stop; 6.7 if a human takes over within 60 s → one intervention with reason `safety` |
| N9 | `APPROACH` (FootprintApproach) | `ai` (flag `x_collision_monitor_approach`) | — | Speed scaling leaves the AI in command; see Uncovered 4 for the zero-velocity corner |
| N9 | `SLOWDOWN`/`LIMIT` | not configured on TB4; would be `ai` | — | as APPROACH |
| N11 velocity_smoother timeout zero | input silent > 1.0 s | no state change by itself; if N1 still `EXECUTING` → `unknown` (flag `x_controller_silent`) | — | Section 2 `unknown`: logger lost its source of truth |
| N13 lifecycle | bt_navigator/controller/smoother/collision_monitor not ACTIVE, no task open | `idle` after `idle_threshold_s` | — | Section 2 `idle` |
| N13 | server leaves ACTIVE while a goal is EXECUTING | `unknown` (or `shared`/`human_initiated` if the PAUSE is attributable to a human device) | — | 6.8 (cause cannot be observed → `unknown`) |
| N16 `/goal_pose` before `task_start` | — | task definition, no state change | — | 6.2 last sentence |
| N16 `/goal_pose` while EXECUTING | — | `shared` until the new path is being followed (hint consumed) or `hint_ttl_s` | `human_initiated` | 6.2 `hint_as_shared`; see Uncovered 1 |
| N17 `/initialpose` while a task is open | — | `hint` event (or `shared` under `hint_as_shared`) | `human_initiated` | 6.2: human information that changes AI behaviour without commanding actuators; see Uncovered 5 |
| N19 `/speed_limit`, N20 selectors from a human | — | `hint` | `human_initiated` | 6.2 |
| N21 human-triggered `spin`/`backup`/`drive_on_heading` | `EXECUTING` with no Nav2 goal | `human_remote`/`human_local` (device registry) | `human_initiated` | Section 2 + 6.10; see Uncovered 2 |
| N22 `dock_robot` | `EXECUTING` | `ai` if from a mission node, else as N21 | — | — |
| N14 `manage_nodes` PAUSE during a task | — | `shared` | `human_initiated` | 6.8 (human paused) |

Reliability and faking in one paragraph: all of these are plain DDS topics without authentication; a node can publish a fake `GoalStatusArray`, a fake `BehaviorTreeLog`, or a fake `CollisionMonitorState`. Topic-level evidence is therefore trust level L1 at most unless the observer also sees consistent motion evidence (`/cmd_vel` content and odometry, TB4 track). Signals that are **missed** by design: action goal/cancel requests and all services (not topics); `collision_monitor_state` between changes and before the first change; `behavior_tree_log` between goals; everything while a server is not ACTIVE.

## 7. Passive observation: safest read-only attachment, cost, observer failure

_Lead note._ Agreed across tracks: subscribe best-effort/volatile; never attach a DDS participant directly to the Create 3 (it discovers every entity and iRobot documents the resulting load) — observe the republished topics on the Pi's domain, or run the observer on the Pi reading `/_do_not_use/*`; with Discovery Server the observer must be a super client; subscribe to all `_action/status` topics first (transient-local) and treat every volatile, change-only topic as `unknown` until its first message. No TB4 node depends on a subscriber, so an observer crash has no effect on the robot (evidence: launch files and absence of liveliness QoS; no explicit maker statement). Quantified CPU/latency cost: UNKNOWN (no official figures).

### 7. Safest read-only attachment  _[TB4 stack track]_

**Option A — observer node on another machine, same `ROS_DOMAIN_ID`.** Default robot settings: `ROS_DOMAIN_ID=0`, `RMW_IMPLEMENTATION=rmw_fastrtps_cpp`, `ROS_DISCOVERY_SERVER` empty (simple discovery), `ROBOT_NAMESPACE` empty [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_setup/blob/5fe58657aeff57d28cb2cfb4f0c6cc6c62a4b96c/etc/turtlebot4/setup.bash#L3, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_setup/blob/5fe58657aeff57d28cb2cfb4f0c6cc6c62a4b96c/etc/turtlebot4/setup.bash#L4, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_setup/blob/5fe58657aeff57d28cb2cfb4f0c6cc6c62a4b96c/etc/turtlebot4/setup.bash#L5, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_setup/blob/5fe58657aeff57d28cb2cfb4f0c6cc6c62a4b96c/etc/turtlebot4/setup.bash#L6, acc. 2026-10-31]. In Jazzy the manual's recommended topology (both simple and discovery-server) has only the Pi on Wi-Fi and the Create 3 offline, so the observer sees Create 3 data only through the republisher [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/setup/networking.md#L51, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/setup/networking.md#L68, acc. 2026-10-31]. If the robot uses **Discovery Server** (manual recommendation for navigation or 2+ robots) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/setup/networking.md#L109, acc. 2026-10-31]: the Pi runs `fastdds discovery -i 0 -p 11811` as systemd `discovery.service` (User=ubuntu) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_setup/blob/5fe58657aeff57d28cb2cfb4f0c6cc6c62a4b96c/etc/turtlebot4/discovery.sh#L3, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_setup/blob/5fe58657aeff57d28cb2cfb4f0c6cc6c62a4b96c/etc/turtlebot4/discovery.conf#L10, acc. 2026-10-31]; the Create 3 is pointed at `192.168.186.3:<port>` (the Pi's usb0 address) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_setup/blob/5fe58657aeff57d28cb2cfb4f0c6cc6c62a4b96c/turtlebot4_setup/conf.py#L474, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_setup/blob/5fe58657aeff57d28cb2cfb4f0c6cc6c62a4b96c/etc/netplan/40-ethernets.yaml#L12, acc. 2026-10-31]; the observer PC must set `RMW_IMPLEMENTATION=rmw_fastrtps_cpp`, `ROS_DISCOVERY_SERVER="<pi_wifi_ip>:11811;"` and `ROS_SUPER_CLIENT=True` in interactive shells (the `configure_discovery.sh` script writes `/etc/turtlebot4_discovery/setup.bash` with exactly these) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_setup/blob/5fe58657aeff57d28cb2cfb4f0c6cc6c62a4b96c/turtlebot4_discovery/configure_discovery.sh#L177, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_setup/blob/5fe58657aeff57d28cb2cfb4f0c6cc6c62a4b96c/turtlebot4_discovery/configure_discovery.sh#L178, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_setup/blob/5fe58657aeff57d28cb2cfb4f0c6cc6c62a4b96c/turtlebot4_discovery/configure_discovery.sh#L182, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/setup/discovery_server.md#L226, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://turtlebot.github.io/turtlebot4-user-manual/setup/discovery_server.html, acc. 2026-10-31]. Defaults in `conf.py`: discovery disabled, port 11811, server id 0, offboard id 1 [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_setup/blob/5fe58657aeff57d28cb2cfb4f0c6cc6c62a4b96c/turtlebot4_setup/conf.py#L118-L120, acc. 2026-10-31]. The Create 3-facing Fast DDS profile reduces socket buffers to 32768 bytes and `maxMessageSize` to 8192 and disables builtin transports [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_setup/blob/5fe58657aeff57d28cb2cfb4f0c6cc6c62a4b96c/etc/turtlebot4/fastdds_discovery_create3.xml#L10, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_setup/blob/5fe58657aeff57d28cb2cfb4f0c6cc6c62a4b96c/etc/turtlebot4/fastdds_discovery_create3.xml#L13, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_setup/blob/5fe58657aeff57d28cb2cfb4f0c6cc6c62a4b96c/etc/turtlebot4/fastdds_discovery_create3.xml#L25, acc. 2026-10-31]. CycloneDDS, if selected, is bound to `wlan0` only with `DontRoute` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_setup/blob/5fe58657aeff57d28cb2cfb4f0c6cc6c62a4b96c/etc/turtlebot4/cyclonedds_rpi.xml#L9, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_setup/blob/5fe58657aeff57d28cb2cfb4f0c6cc6c62a4b96c/etc/turtlebot4/cyclonedds_rpi.xml#L12, acc. 2026-10-31].

**QoS pitfalls.** Create 3-derived topics are consumed with `SensorDataQoS` (best effort) by turtlebot4_node; `joy` is reliable depth 10; Nav2 `cmd_vel` publishers use depth 1; in simulation the Create 3 motion node subscribes `cmd_vel` best-effort [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/src/turtlebot4.cpp#L135, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/src/buttons.cpp#L42-L44, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_sim/blob/6c305843afcfe094bf8eb2a0c6997f531e526b9d/irobot_create_common/irobot_create_nodes/src/motion_control_node.cpp#L115, acc. 2026-10-31]. A reliable subscriber will not match a best-effort publisher, so the observer should subscribe best-effort/volatile to every topic (rosbag2 `record` adapts QoS per topic from the discovered publishers by default). The manual warns that QoS must match the topic [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/software/create3.md#L160, acc. 2026-10-31].

**Option B — `ros2 bag record` on the Pi** (as the test suite does) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_tests/turtlebot4_tests/test_tools.py#L108, acc. 2026-10-31]; this consumes Pi CPU/SD bandwidth. **Option C — foxglove_bridge** (Jazzy release 3.6.3-1 from foxglove-sdk) [VERIFIED: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/ros/rosdistro/master/jazzy/distribution.yaml, acc. 2026-10-31] [UNKNOWN: not used anywhere in TB4 sources].

**CPU / bandwidth statements (official).** Manual: simple discovery has "significant baseline network traffic", "too many ROS devices/nodes can result in saturating the network and/or increased CPU usage on each of the devices" [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/setup/networking.md#L100, acc. 2026-10-31]; diagnostics can be disabled "for systems experiencing high CPU usage issues or limited bandwidth" [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/troubleshooting/diagnostics.md#L48, acc. 2026-10-31]; the Create 3 "has limited resources and can be overwhelmed with too many topics to discover" [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/software/create3.md#L189, acc. 2026-10-31]; the republisher list should be trimmed "to prevent overloading the system with too many topics" [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/software/create3.md#L199, acc. 2026-10-31]. Numeric CPU/latency budgets: [UNKNOWN: none published].

**Observer crash.** No TB4 launch file depends on an external recorder: the launch descriptions list only the nodes in Section 3.1; `create3_republisher` is `respawn=True`, the others are not [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/launch/robot.launch.py#L82, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/launch/standard.launch.py#L69-L72, acc. 2026-10-31]. A subscriber-only observer that dies simply unmatches from DDS; no TB4 node waits on a subscriber (no `wait_for_subscription` / lifecycle bond in TB4 code: grep 0 hits). The diagnostics launch does shut itself down if the aggregator exits (`OnProcessExit -> Shutdown`) — this concerns the aggregator, not an observer [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_diagnostics/launch/diagnostics.launch.py#L63-L64, acc. 2026-10-31].

### 7. Passive observation over DDS (base part)  _[Create 3 base track]_

- RMW: "the only supported RMW implementations are: `rmw_cyclonedds_cpp` and `rmw_fastrtps_cpp`" [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/setup/xml-config.md#L18; https://iroboteducation.github.io/create3_docs/setup/xml-config/, acc. 2026-10-31]; I.0.0 ships separate CycloneDDS (0.10.3) and FastDDS (2.10.1) images — "only one middleware selection is available at a time; to switch middleware, it is necessary to install the matching firmware" [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/releases/i_0_0.md#L2; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/releases/i_0_0.md#L3; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/releases/i_0_0.md#L11; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/releases/i_0_0.md#L12; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/webserver/application.md#L29, acc. 2026-10-31]. The observer must use the same RMW as the robot [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/ros2.md#L54; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/setup/xml-config.md#L40, acc. 2026-10-31].
- Domain ID and namespace are set on the webserver Application → Configuration page; domain 0–101, default 0 [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/webserver/application.md#L22; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/setup/multi-robot.md#L73; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/setup/multi-robot.md#L74; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/setup/multi-robot.md#L75; https://iroboteducation.github.io/create3_docs/setup/multi-robot/, acc. 2026-10-31]. (TurtleBot 4 sets its own namespace/domain in its setup tool — other track.)
- Discovery: default multicast discovery; a Fast DDS Discovery Server can be enabled on the robot (address:port, default 11811), and then "all other devices connected to the discovery server must be set up as super clients" [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/webserver/application.md#L26; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/setup/discovery-server.md#L25; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/setup/discovery-server.md#L35; https://iroboteducation.github.io/create3_docs/setup/discovery-server/, acc. 2026-10-31]. Unicast-only profiles for networks that block multicast are given for both RMWs [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/setup/xml-config.md#L60; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/setup/xml-config.md#L61; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/setup/xml-config.md#L144, acc. 2026-10-31]. With a compute board on USB, the robot uses a multi-interface Cyclone config on `usb0` and `wlan0`, and a laptop may need `<DontRoute>true</DontRoute>` to see topics [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/setup/xml-config.md#L131; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/setup/xml-config.md#L132; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/setup/xml-config.md#L137, acc. 2026-10-31]. The robot's RMW XML profile can be replaced through the webserver (beta) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/webserver/rmw-profile-override.md#L11, acc. 2026-10-31].
- QoS to match: best-effort/volatile subscriptions (Section 5) — compatible with any firmware publisher.
- CPU impact: iRobot states that DDS discovers all entities and "allocate[s] resources for all of them ... This can cause performance issues due to excessive memory allocations, discovery traffic and double delivery", and that this occurs with a Raspberry Pi running the navigation stack [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_examples/blob/fe3d408a266b755b94cb7334a92f2d58dd661c13/create3_republisher/README.md#L15; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_examples/blob/fe3d408a266b755b94cb7334a92f2d58dd661c13/create3_republisher/README.md#L16; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_examples/blob/fe3d408a266b755b94cb7334a92f2d58dd661c13/create3_republisher/README.md#L19, acc. 2026-10-31]; the remedy is the `create3_republisher`, which TurtleBot 4 runs so that the base discovers only one entity [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/launch/robot.launch.py#L78, acc. 2026-10-31]. An observer must therefore **not** subscribe to the base directly: it should subscribe to the republished topics on the Pi, otherwise it adds a DDS participant to the base's view and can degrade it (the robot-side profile is a unicast list containing only the republisher's address [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_examples/blob/fe3d408a266b755b94cb7334a92f2d58dd661c13/create3_republisher/dds-config/fastdds-robot-passive-unicast.xml#L30; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_examples/blob/fe3d408a266b755b94cb7334a92f2d58dd661c13/create3_republisher/README.md#L125, acc. 2026-10-31]). Disabling `hazard_detection`, odometry TFs and raw kinematics "can noticeably reduce the CPU and RAM usage of the robot" [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/releases/h_2_5.md#L31; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/releases/h_2_5.md#L33; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/releases/h_2_5.md#L36, acc. 2026-10-31]. Quantified CPU cost of one extra subscriber: [UNKNOWN].
- Observer crash: the firmware's publications and the `cmd_vel` timeout do not depend on subscribers; the webserver and the ROS 2 application are separate processes [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/webserver/overview.md#L7, acc. 2026-10-31], and nothing in the API ties firmware motion to a subscriber's liveliness. Direct evidence that the base "keeps running when subscribers vanish" is only the design of the republisher (subscribers are generic, best-effort, with no liveliness QoS [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_examples/blob/fe3d408a266b755b94cb7334a92f2d58dd661c13/create3_republisher/republisher.cpp#L155, acc. 2026-10-31]) — tagged as inference; no explicit iRobot statement ([UNKNOWN]).

### 7. QoS of status and log topics (late-joiner behaviour)  _[Nav2 track]_

| Topic | History / depth | Reliability | Durability | Late joiner sees last value? | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `<action>/_action/status` (all Nav2 actions) | KEEP_LAST 1 | RELIABLE | TRANSIENT_LOCAL | **Yes** — the last `GoalStatusArray` is delivered on subscription | [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros2/rcl/blob/18d96c9c2b98e616324ae9a2c7701dd0b0886ff0/rcl_action/include/rcl_action/default_qos.h#L26-L37, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros2/rcl/blob/18d96c9c2b98e616324ae9a2c7701dd0b0886ff0/rcl_action/src/rcl_action/action_server.c#L311, acc. 2026-10-31] |
| `<action>/_action/feedback` | rcl_action feedback default | — | — | No (CLAIM: volatile; not read in this track) | — |
| `/behavior_tree_log` | KEEP_LAST 10 (`rclcpp::QoS(10)`) | RELIABLE (rclcpp default) | VOLATILE (rclcpp default) | **No** | [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_behavior_tree/include/nav2_behavior_tree/ros_topic_logger.hpp#L49-L51, acc. 2026-10-31] |
| `/collision_monitor_state` | KEEP_LAST 1 (`create_publisher(state_topic, 1)`) | RELIABLE | VOLATILE | **No**, and the topic is change-triggered, so the current action is unknown until the next change | [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_collision_monitor/src/collision_monitor_node.cpp#L79-L81, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_collision_monitor/src/collision_monitor_node.cpp#L510-L513, acc. 2026-10-31] |
| `/<node>/transition_event` | rcl default publisher options | RELIABLE (rmw default) | VOLATILE (rmw default) | **No**; use `/<node>/get_state` (active query) | [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros2/rcl/blob/18d96c9c2b98e616324ae9a2c7701dd0b0886ff0/rcl_lifecycle/src/com_interface.c#L110-L113, acc. 2026-10-31] [CLAIM for the rmw default profile values] |
| `/cmd_vel_nav`, `/cmd_vel_smoothed`, `/cmd_vel` | KEEP_LAST 1 | RELIABLE | VOLATILE | No | [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_util/include/nav2_util/twist_publisher.hpp#L55-L75, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_controller/src/controller_server.cpp#L227, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_velocity_smoother/src/velocity_smoother.cpp#L155, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_collision_monitor/src/collision_monitor_node.cpp#L77, acc. 2026-10-31] |
| `/controller_selector`, `/planner_selector` (subscription side) | KEEP_LAST 1 | RELIABLE | TRANSIENT_LOCAL | Yes, if the publisher is also transient-local | [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_behavior_tree/plugins/action/controller_selector_node.cpp#L43-L44, acc. 2026-10-31] |
| `/goal_pose`, `/initialpose` (subscription side) | SystemDefaultsQoS | — | — | No | [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_bt_navigator/src/navigators/navigate_to_pose.cpp#L49-L52, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_amcl/src/amcl_node.cpp#L1571-L1572, acc. 2026-10-31] |

Practical rule for the observer: start by subscribing to every `_action/status` topic (transient-local gives the current goal state immediately), then to `/behavior_tree_log`, `/collision_monitor_state`, the three `cmd_vel*` topics and all `transition_event` topics; record the join time and mark the interval before the first message on each volatile topic as `unknown` for that signal.

## 8. Simulation

_Lead note._ turtlebot4_simulator 2.0.2 on Gazebo Harmonic with create3_sim 3.0.4 reproduces the Create 3 topics, actions, e-stop, 0.5 s timeout and four of the nine reflexes; it differs from hardware in topic naming (sim subscribes `cmd_vel` as TwistStamped), exposes hazards directly (no republisher filter), lacks `/imu`, Bluetooth `joy`, the webserver, BLE mode and button holds, and defaults `safety_override` to `backup_only` instead of `none`. The step-by-step recipe is in `integration_notes.md` (untested on this host).

### 8. Simulation  _[TB4 stack track]_

**Stack.** `turtlebot4_simulator` 2.0.2 = `turtlebot4_gz_bringup`, `turtlebot4_gz_gui_plugins`, `turtlebot4_gz_toolbox` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_simulator/blob/b7d0f3b973258481f6cd8137bef9240fb567f566/turtlebot4_simulator/package.xml#L16, acc. 2026-10-31]. `sim.launch.py` includes `ros_gz_sim/gz_sim.launch.py` with the comment "Gazebo harmonic", `gz_args = <world>.sdf -r -v 4 --gui-config <model>/gui.config`, sets `GZ_SIM_RESOURCE_PATH` and `GZ_GUI_PLUGIN_PATH`, and bridges `/clock` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_simulator/blob/b7d0f3b973258481f6cd8137bef9240fb567f566/turtlebot4_gz_bringup/launch/sim.launch.py#L84, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_simulator/blob/b7d0f3b973258481f6cd8137bef9240fb567f566/turtlebot4_gz_bringup/launch/sim.launch.py#L88, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_simulator/blob/b7d0f3b973258481f6cd8137bef9240fb567f566/turtlebot4_gz_bringup/launch/sim.launch.py#L59-L62, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_simulator/blob/b7d0f3b973258481f6cd8137bef9240fb567f566/turtlebot4_gz_bringup/launch/sim.launch.py#L109, acc. 2026-10-31]; dependencies `ros_gz_sim`, `ros_gz_interfaces`, `irobot_create_gz_bringup/plugins/toolbox`, `irobot_create_nodes`, `turtlebot4_node`, `turtlebot4_navigation`, `turtlebot4_viz` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_simulator/blob/b7d0f3b973258481f6cd8137bef9240fb567f566/turtlebot4_gz_bringup/package.xml#L17-L19, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_simulator/blob/b7d0f3b973258481f6cd8137bef9240fb567f566/turtlebot4_gz_bringup/package.xml#L23-L26, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_simulator/blob/b7d0f3b973258481f6cd8137bef9240fb567f566/turtlebot4_gz_bringup/package.xml#L36, acc. 2026-10-31]; the GUI plugin depends on `gz_gui_vendor` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_simulator/blob/b7d0f3b973258481f6cd8137bef9240fb567f566/turtlebot4_gz_gui_plugins/package.xml#L15, acc. 2026-10-31]. The manual's Jazzy tab installs `ros-jazzy-turtlebot4-simulator ros-jazzy-irobot-create-nodes` and Gazebo Harmonic (`gz-harmonic`) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/software/turtlebot4_simulator.md#L35, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/software/turtlebot4_simulator.md#L82, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/software/turtlebot4_simulator.md#L89, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://turtlebot.github.io/turtlebot4-user-manual/software/turtlebot4_simulator.html, acc. 2026-10-31]. Default world `warehouse`; `sim.launch.py` default model `lite`, `turtlebot4_gz.launch.py` default model `standard` (inconsistent defaults; the top-level launch wins) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_simulator/blob/b7d0f3b973258481f6cd8137bef9240fb567f566/turtlebot4_gz_bringup/launch/sim.launch.py#L35, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_simulator/blob/b7d0f3b973258481f6cd8137bef9240fb567f566/turtlebot4_gz_bringup/launch/sim.launch.py#L37, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_simulator/blob/b7d0f3b973258481f6cd8137bef9240fb567f566/turtlebot4_gz_bringup/launch/turtlebot4_gz.launch.py#L33, acc. 2026-10-31].

**What it reproduces.** `turtlebot4_spawn.launch.py` (args `rviz`, `use_sim_time=true`, `model`, `namespace`, `localization`, `slam`, `nav2`, x/y/z/yaw) spawns robot and dock (robot starts docked), launches `ros_gz_bridge.launch.py`, `turtlebot4_nodes.launch.py` (real `turtlebot4_node` + `turtlebot4_gz_hmi_node` on Standard), `irobot_create_common_bringup/create3_nodes.launch.py` (simulated Create 3 nodes: motion control with hazards/backup limit, docking, etc. — Create 3 track) and `create3_gz_nodes.launch.py`, RPLIDAR static TFs, and optionally localization/slam/nav2/rviz from the same TB4 packages used on hardware [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_simulator/blob/b7d0f3b973258481f6cd8137bef9240fb567f566/turtlebot4_gz_bringup/launch/turtlebot4_spawn.launch.py#L33, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_simulator/blob/b7d0f3b973258481f6cd8137bef9240fb567f566/turtlebot4_gz_bringup/launch/turtlebot4_spawn.launch.py#L50, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_simulator/blob/b7d0f3b973258481f6cd8137bef9240fb567f566/turtlebot4_gz_bringup/launch/turtlebot4_spawn.launch.py#L140, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_simulator/blob/b7d0f3b973258481f6cd8137bef9240fb567f566/turtlebot4_gz_bringup/launch/turtlebot4_spawn.launch.py#L181, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_simulator/blob/b7d0f3b973258481f6cd8137bef9240fb567f566/turtlebot4_gz_bringup/launch/turtlebot4_spawn.launch.py#L188, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_simulator/blob/b7d0f3b973258481f6cd8137bef9240fb567f566/turtlebot4_gz_bringup/launch/turtlebot4_spawn.launch.py#L196, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_simulator/blob/b7d0f3b973258481f6cd8137bef9240fb567f566/turtlebot4_gz_bringup/launch/turtlebot4_spawn.launch.py#L261, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_simulator/blob/b7d0f3b973258481f6cd8137bef9240fb567f566/turtlebot4_gz_bringup/launch/turtlebot4_nodes.launch.py#L48, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_simulator/blob/b7d0f3b973258481f6cd8137bef9240fb567f566/turtlebot4_gz_bringup/launch/turtlebot4_nodes.launch.py#L58, acc. 2026-10-31]. Bridges: lidar `scan`, OAK-D rgb/depth/points/camera_info, HMI display/buttons/LEDs [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_simulator/blob/b7d0f3b973258481f6cd8137bef9240fb567f566/turtlebot4_gz_bringup/launch/ros_gz_bridge.launch.py#L91, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_simulator/blob/b7d0f3b973258481f6cd8137bef9240fb567f566/turtlebot4_gz_bringup/launch/ros_gz_bridge.launch.py#L132, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_simulator/blob/b7d0f3b973258481f6cd8137bef9240fb567f566/turtlebot4_gz_bringup/launch/ros_gz_bridge.launch.py#L150, acc. 2026-10-31]; `cmd_vel` is bridged by create3_sim's `cmd_vel_bridge`: ROS `<ns>/cmd_vel` TwistStamped -> gz, and the Create 3 sim motion node outputs `diffdrive_controller/cmd_vel` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_sim/blob/6c305843afcfe094bf8eb2a0c6997f531e526b9d/irobot_create_gz/irobot_create_gz_bringup/launch/create3_ros_gz_bridge.launch.py#L60, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_sim/blob/6c305843afcfe094bf8eb2a0c6997f531e526b9d/irobot_create_gz/irobot_create_gz_bringup/launch/create3_ros_gz_bridge.launch.py#L68, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_sim/blob/6c305843afcfe094bf8eb2a0c6997f531e526b9d/irobot_create_common/irobot_create_nodes/src/motion_control_node.cpp#L130, acc. 2026-10-31].

**GUI plugins.** `Turtlebot4Hmi` publishes gz `Int32` on `<ns>/hmi/buttons` and `<ns>/create3_buttons` and subscribes to display/LED topics [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_simulator/blob/b7d0f3b973258481f6cd8137bef9240fb567f566/turtlebot4_gz_gui_plugins/Turtlebot4Hmi/Turtlebot4Hmi.cc#L47, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_simulator/blob/b7d0f3b973258481f6cd8137bef9240fb567f566/turtlebot4_gz_gui_plugins/Turtlebot4Hmi/Turtlebot4Hmi.cc#L123, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_simulator/blob/b7d0f3b973258481f6cd8137bef9240fb567f566/turtlebot4_gz_gui_plugins/Turtlebot4Hmi/Turtlebot4Hmi.cc#L124, acc. 2026-10-31]; the bridge maps `<ns>/hmi/buttons` (gz Int32) to ROS `hmi/buttons/_set`, and `turtlebot4_gz_hmi_node` ("hmi_node") converts it to `turtlebot4_msgs/UserButton` on `hmi/buttons` and publishes display raw/selected on `hmi/display/_raw`, `hmi/display/_selected` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_simulator/blob/b7d0f3b973258481f6cd8137bef9240fb567f566/turtlebot4_gz_bringup/launch/ros_gz_bridge.launch.py#L138, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_simulator/blob/b7d0f3b973258481f6cd8137bef9240fb567f566/turtlebot4_gz_toolbox/src/hmi_node.cpp#L36, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_simulator/blob/b7d0f3b973258481f6cd8137bef9240fb567f566/turtlebot4_gz_toolbox/src/hmi_node.cpp#L41, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_simulator/blob/b7d0f3b973258481f6cd8137bef9240fb567f566/turtlebot4_gz_toolbox/src/hmi_node.cpp#L45, acc. 2026-10-31]. The gz `Teleop` plugin publishes on gz topic `/cmd_vel` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_simulator/blob/b7d0f3b973258481f6cd8137bef9240fb567f566/turtlebot4_gz_bringup/gui/standard/gui.config#L117, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_simulator/blob/b7d0f3b973258481f6cd8137bef9240fb567f566/turtlebot4_gz_bringup/gui/standard/gui.config#L118, acc. 2026-10-31]; [UNKNOWN: whether that gz topic is the bridged one for a namespaced robot (bridge uses <ns>/cmd_vel; with empty namespace both are /cmd_vel)].

**Differences from hardware.** No Bluetooth `joy` path (no joy_linux in sim launch; `joy_teleop.launch.py` can still be run on the PC with a USB pad); no `create3_republisher` and no `_do_not_use` namespace (sim Create 3 nodes publish directly in the robot namespace); `cmd_vel` consumed best-effort by the sim motion node; time is `/clock` (sim time) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_simulator/blob/b7d0f3b973258481f6cd8137bef9240fb567f566/turtlebot4_gz_bringup/launch/turtlebot4_spawn.launch.py#L36, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_simulator/blob/b7d0f3b973258481f6cd8137bef9240fb567f566/turtlebot4_gz_bringup/launch/sim.launch.py#L105, acc. 2026-10-31]; the sim `create3_nodes.launch.py` publishes `hazard_detection`, `kidnap_status`, etc. without a relay filter, so simulation exposes more safety signals than the hardware default [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_sim/blob/6c305843afcfe094bf8eb2a0c6997f531e526b9d/irobot_create_common/irobot_create_nodes/src/motion_control_node.cpp#L107-L110, acc. 2026-10-31] [UNKNOWN: full sim topic list — Create 3 track]. Minimal commands with versions are in `sim_steps.md` (untested on this host: no ROS install).

### 8. Simulation fidelity (base part — create3_sim 3.0.4, Gazebo Harmonic only [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_sim/blob/6c305843afcfe094bf8eb2a0c6997f531e526b9d/README.md#L6, acc. 2026-10-31])  _[Create 3 base track]_

Implemented in `irobot_create_nodes` / `irobot_create_gz_toolbox`:

| Real firmware element | In create3_sim? | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| `/cmd_vel` timeout 0.5 s; actions pre-empt cmd_vel; e-stop zeroes output | yes — `wheels_stop_threshold_(0.5s)`, behaviour scheduler resets teleop, `if (!e_stop_engaged_)` | [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_sim/blob/6c305843afcfe094bf8eb2a0c6997f531e526b9d/irobot_create_common/irobot_create_nodes/src/motion_control_node.cpp#L22; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_sim/blob/6c305843afcfe094bf8eb2a0c6997f531e526b9d/irobot_create_common/irobot_create_nodes/src/motion_control_node.cpp#L237; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_sim/blob/6c305843afcfe094bf8eb2a0c6997f531e526b9d/irobot_create_common/irobot_create_nodes/src/motion_control_node.cpp#L242-L245; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_sim/blob/6c305843afcfe094bf8eb2a0c6997f531e526b9d/irobot_create_common/irobot_create_nodes/src/motion_control_node.cpp#L281, acc. 2026-10-31] |
| cmd_vel topic names | **differs**: sim subscribes `cmd_vel` as **TwistStamped** and `cmd_vel_unstamped` as Twist; real firmware uses `cmd_vel` Twist and `cmd_vel_stamped` TwistStamped (TB4 remaps make the Pi-side names match the sim) | [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_sim/blob/6c305843afcfe094bf8eb2a0c6997f531e526b9d/irobot_create_common/irobot_create_nodes/src/motion_control_node.cpp#L114; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_sim/blob/6c305843afcfe094bf8eb2a0c6997f531e526b9d/irobot_create_common/irobot_create_nodes/src/motion_control_node.cpp#L115; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_sim/blob/6c305843afcfe094bf8eb2a0c6997f531e526b9d/irobot_create_common/irobot_create_nodes/src/motion_control_node.cpp#L117; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_sim/blob/6c305843afcfe094bf8eb2a0c6997f531e526b9d/irobot_create_common/irobot_create_nodes/src/motion_control_node.cpp#L118; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/releases/i_0_0.md#L25, acc. 2026-10-31] |

| wheel_status | published from motion_control with `wheels_enabled` only (current/PWM not modelled); plus a `wheel_status_publisher` node | [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_sim/blob/6c305843afcfe094bf8eb2a0c6997f531e526b9d/irobot_create_common/irobot_create_nodes/src/motion_control_node.cpp#L288; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_sim/blob/6c305843afcfe094bf8eb2a0c6997f531e526b9d/irobot_create_common/irobot_create_common_bringup/launch/create3_nodes.launch.py#L93, acc. 2026-10-31] |
| interface_buttons | yes — Gazebo GUI plugin (Create3Hmi) → `_internal/create3_buttons` Int32 → `/interface_buttons`; no 7 s / 10 s firmware holds | [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_sim/blob/6c305843afcfe094bf8eb2a0c6997f531e526b9d/irobot_create_gz/irobot_create_gz_toolbox/src/interface_buttons/interface_buttons_node.cpp#L15; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_sim/blob/6c305843afcfe094bf8eb2a0c6997f531e526b9d/irobot_create_gz/irobot_create_gz_toolbox/src/interface_buttons/interface_buttons_node.cpp#L20, acc. 2026-10-31] |
| `/imu` | IMU sensor defined in the URDF but the ROS plugin is classic-Gazebo only and no `/imu` entry exists in the gz bridge launch → **no `/imu` topic in the Harmonic sim** (absence verified by grep of the bridge launch) | [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_sim/blob/6c305843afcfe094bf8eb2a0c6997f531e526b9d/irobot_create_common/irobot_create_description/urdf/sensors/imu.urdf.xacro#L62; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_sim/blob/6c305843afcfe094bf8eb2a0c6997f531e526b9d/irobot_create_common/irobot_create_description/urdf/sensors/imu.urdf.xacro#L63; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_sim/blob/6c305843afcfe094bf8eb2a0c6997f531e526b9d/irobot_create_gz/irobot_create_gz_bringup/launch/create3_ros_gz_bridge.launch.py, acc. 2026-10-31] |
| Webserver, BLE mode, standby/storage, firmware update, application restarts, backup-limit light patterns | not simulated (no corresponding code) | [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_sim/blob/6c305843afcfe094bf8eb2a0c6997f531e526b9d/README.md#L90, acc. 2026-10-31] |
| diff-drive controller | ros2_control `diff_drive_controller`, `cmd_vel_timeout: 0.5`, limits 0.46 m/s, 1.9 rad/s, 0.9 m/s² | [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_sim/blob/6c305843afcfe094bf8eb2a0c6997f531e526b9d/irobot_create_common/irobot_create_control/config/control.yaml#L39; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_sim/blob/6c305843afcfe094bf8eb2a0c6997f531e526b9d/irobot_create_common/irobot_create_control/config/control.yaml#L60; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_sim/blob/6c305843afcfe094bf8eb2a0c6997f531e526b9d/irobot_create_common/irobot_create_control/config/control.yaml#L70; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_sim/blob/6c305843afcfe094bf8eb2a0c6997f531e526b9d/irobot_create_common/irobot_create_control/config/control.yaml#L62, acc. 2026-10-31] |

Consequence for the meter: in simulation, reflex episodes exist for BUMP/CLIFF/STALL/WHEEL_DROP only; proximity slowdown, virtual walls, panic/stuck escalation, slip, e-stop light patterns and all out-of-band control paths (BLE, webserver, button holds) cannot be exercised. `hazard_detection` is periodic (62 Hz) in sim but event-driven on the real robot.

## 9. Public datasets or bags with teleoperated vs autonomous labels

_Lead note._ Negative result for this platform; nearest substitutes tabulated. The project's interval convention (episode-clustered ratio-linearised t) was therefore not applied. `evidence_datasets.csv` from the Evidence track is attached as a companion table.

### 9. Public datasets and bags with teleoperated vs autonomous labels  _[Evidence track]_

#### 9.1 Search protocol

Hugging Face datasets API (`https://huggingface.co/api/datasets?search=turtlebot (and the other terms) with limit=100`) queried 2026-11-03 for `turtlebot4`, `turtlebot 4`, `turtlebot`, `create3`, `create 3`, `irobot`, `nav2`, `rosbag`, `teleop`, `ros2 bag`, `navigation2`, `intervention robot`, `gnm`, `scand`, `gostanford`, `lekiwi`. Results: `turtlebot4` 0, `turtlebot 4` 0, `create3` 0, `create 3` 0, `navigation2` 0; `turtlebot` 4 hits, all TurtleBot 3 or detection images (`jfhahn/turtlebot-detection-yolo-v1`, `iblnk/turtlebot3_demo`, `iblnk/turtlebot3-rainbow`, `penji2803/turtlebot3-moving-obstacles-lidar-1.5m`); `irobot` 5 hits, all from the unrelated `ABC-iRobotics` organisation; `rosbag` 85 hits, none naming TurtleBot 4 or Create 3 [VERIFIED: https://huggingface.co/api/datasets?search=turtlebot4, acc. 2026-11-03]. The HF search endpoint matches repository names, not card text, so a TB4 dataset under a non-descriptive name would be missed (limitation, see Unknowns).

arXiv export API queried 2026-11-03: `all:"TurtleBot 4" OR all:"TurtleBot4"` returned 12 papers, all method papers that deploy on a TurtleBot 4 and none releasing a labelled teleop/autonomy dataset; `all:"Create 3" AND all:iRobot` 0; `all:turtlebot AND (dataset OR rosbag) AND (teleoperation OR intervention)` 0; `all:"TurtleBot 4" AND (dataset OR teleoperation OR teleoperated)` 0 [VERIFIED: http://export.arxiv.org/api/query?search_query=all:%22TurtleBot%204%22, acc. 2026-11-03]. OpenAlex (`/works?search=`) queried for `"TurtleBot 4" teleoperation` (12 works), `"TurtleBot 4" dataset` (44), `"TurtleBot 4" human intervention navigation` (11), `"Create 3" iRobot robot dataset` (16), `"TurtleBot4" dataset` (52); titles were screened and the only dataset records were the RoboVAST Navigation Dataset (Zenodo 10.5281/zenodo.19146985, simulated TB4, see 9.3) and PrivateMap-Bench (Harvard Dataverse, SLAM maps, no control labels) [VERIFIED: https://api.openalex.org/works?search=%22TurtleBot4%22%20dataset, acc. 2026-11-03].

The pinned TurtleBot 4 repositories contain no sample bag. The only recording code is the self-test tool, which spawns `ros2 bag record -o {results_dir}/rosbag2/{name}-{count} -a` to a local results directory [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_tests/turtlebot4_tests/test_tools.py#L108, acc. 2026-10-31]; the manual states that test results and a rosbag are saved under `~/turtlebot4_test_results/Y_m_d-H_M_S` [VERIFIED: https://turtlebot.github.io/turtlebot4-user-manual/troubleshooting/ros2_tests.html and https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/troubleshooting/ros2_tests.md#L22, acc. 2026-10-31]. No bag is published. A grep for `.db3`, `.mcap`, `rosbag` over turtlebot4, turtlebot4_robot, turtlebot4_desktop, turtlebot4_simulator, create3_docs and create3_examples at the pinned commits found no other hit [VERIFIED: local grep of the pinned trees, acc. 2026-10-31].

#### 9.2 Result

**No public dataset or bag recorded on TurtleBot 4, TurtleBot 4 Lite or iRobot Create 3 that labels teleoperated versus autonomous time, interventions or Nav2 recoveries was found.** This is a negative result from the searches above, not proof of absence (Unknowns). Consequently no human time share or 95 % interval was computed for this platform and the share columns in `evidence_datasets.csv` are empty.

| Dataset | Platform | Human/autonomy label | Verdict for TB4 | Tag |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| GNM mixture: GoStanford | TurtleBot2 (0.5 m/s, 14 h, office) | collected by teleoperation per SACSoN Table I ("GO Stanford … teleop") | TurtleBot 2 (Kobuki), not TB4; episode-level 100 % human | VERIFIED (paper tables): https://arxiv.org/abs/2210.03370 Table I; https://arxiv.org/abs/2306.01874 Table I, acc. 2026-11-03 |
| `lerobot/berkeley_gnm_recon` (RECON) | Clearpath Jackal UGV, off-road, 25 h | none; the released v3.0 features are `observation.images.image, observation.state, action, timestamp, episode_index, frame_index, next.reward, next.done, index, task_index`; 11 834 episodes, `robot_type: unknown` | not TB4; no control label | VERIFIED: https://huggingface.co/datasets/lerobot/berkeley_gnm_recon/resolve/main/meta/info.json, acc. 2026-11-03; platform https://arxiv.org/abs/2104.05859 §3.1, acc. 2026-11-03 |
| `lerobot/berkeley_gnm_cory_hall` | custom RC car (1.2 m/s, 2 h, hallways) | none (same feature list; 7 331 episodes) | not TB4 | VERIFIED: HF info.json, acc. 2026-11-03; https://arxiv.org/abs/1709.10489, acc. 2026-11-03 |
| `lerobot/berkeley_gnm_sac_son` (HuRoN) | robot "built on top of an iRobot Roomba base" using the AutonomyLab `create_robot` driver, 75 h, 5 offices, autonomous data collection | none in release (2 955 episodes, same feature list). The paper describes a help-and-rescue module in which the robot "sends a signal to a remote operator when in need of remote teleoperation", used "only for these accidental cases"; counts and durations of these rescues are not reported | Closest relative of the Create 3 (iRobot Roomba/Create 2 class base, not Create 3; no ROS 2, no Nav2). Human share not computable: remote rescues unlabelled | VERIFIED (paper text): https://arxiv.org/abs/2306.01874 §VI "Robot and Environment Setup" and §B "Help-and-rescue module", acc. 2026-11-03; HF info.json, acc. 2026-11-03 |
| SCAND | Boston Dynamics Spot and Clearpath Jackal, 8.7 h | entirely human-teleoperated demonstrations with joystick commands recorded | not TB4; human share 1.0 by construction, no autonomous segment to contrast | VERIFIED: https://arxiv.org/abs/2203.15041 abstract and §I, acc. 2026-11-03 |
| Marathon 2 logs (Nav2) | PAL TIAGo and Robotnik RB-1, ROS 2 Eloquent-era Nav2 | per-second log with `timestamp, distance, recovery_executed, vel_x, vel_theta`; paper reports 168 recoveries over 37.4 miles (4.3 per mile as printed in Table III (the pooled figure 168/37.4 = 4.49 per mile; the paper's "total" is the unweighted mean of the two robots' rates 3.3 and 5.3 — re-checked against the PDF 2026-11-03)), 0 collisions, 0 emergency stops, operation "without direct assistance" | not TB4; recovery label only, no human-control label. The paper says data is in the `marathon_ros2` repository, but the repository tree at commit b331bcfed4b94d2acf09cc13581a40751e9e0f87 lists only code packages (`marathon_log_nodes`, `marathon_ros2_bringup`, `marathon_ros2_csv`, `marathon_ros2_wp_manager`) and the README has no data link | Table III VERIFIED: https://arxiv.org/abs/2003.00368, acc. 2026-11-03; data availability CLAIM (paper statement), repository contents VERIFIED: https://github.com/IntelligentRoboticsLabs/marathon_ros2/tree/b331bcfed4b94d2acf09cc13581a40751e9e0f87, acc. 2026-11-03 |
| RoboVAST Navigation Dataset | **simulated TurtleBot 4** in Gazebo, Nav2 default bringup (AMCL, MPPI, NavFn), single `nav_to_pose` missions | per-run success/failure (goal reached within a distance threshold); no human in the loop | Same software stack as the real TB4 but simulation only; label is task outcome, not control authority. Zenodo record 10.5281/zenodo.19146985 could not be opened (host blocked) | Paper text VERIFIED: https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29973 §"System Under Test", acc. 2026-11-03; dataset contents UNKNOWN |
| BARN | simulated Clearpath Jackal (ROS Noetic, Gazebo), 300 environments | none (benchmark, not a recording) | not TB4 | VERIFIED: https://arxiv.org/abs/2008.13315 §IV, acc. 2026-11-03 |
| Foxglove sample TurtleBot bags | unknown | unknown | docs.foxglove.dev blocked; not verified | UNKNOWN |

Mapping of the substitute labels to the taxonomy (for the lead's signal map) is in `evidence_signals.yaml` (ids E1–E6).

#### 9.4 Statistical convention (for when a dataset appears)

The project convention is a point estimate of human time share with an episode-clustered ratio-linearised t interval (episodes as clusters, n = number of episodes). It was not applied because no eligible dataset exists; the per-episode CSV deliverable is therefore not produced. [Analyst statement.]
