### 14D. Every mode and phase mapped to a state

_Lead note._ Where two tracks map the same phase differently, the decision is recorded in the Uncovered cases: human-triggered dock/undock/wall-follow = `shared`/`human_initiated` for the action's duration (UC-3); human EStop = `safety_stop`/`safety` with `x_trigger` (UC-7); reflex = `ai:create3_reflex` or `safety_stop` by whether motion is evasive or inhibited (UC-4).

### 14D. Base modes / phases → taxonomy state  _[Create 3 base track]_

| Mode / phase | Observable as | State (scope `base`) | Reason | Rule |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Docked and charging, no task | `dock_status.is_docked`, `battery_state` | `idle` after `idle_threshold_s` | — | definitions §2 |
| Docked while a task is open (AI mission paused to charge) | same + open task | `ai` (AI chose to wait) | — | §6.8 |
| Docked while a task is open because a human drove it there | same + human cmd_vel/button history | `human_local`/`human_remote` | `human_initiated` | §6.8 |
| Undock / dock action requested by software | action goal; `dock_status` | `ai` (actor `ai:create3:dock`) | — | §2 (`ai`) |
| Undock / dock / wall-follow requested by a human (TB4 button, CLI) | `/interface_buttons` + action goal | `shared` | `human_initiated` | §2 ("human sets goals while AI executes") |
| Drive-goal action (drive_distance etc.) | action goal | `ai` or `shared` as above | — | — |
| Reflex in progress | inference (B3/B4) | **uncovered** — proposed `ai` (actor `ai:create3_reflex`) if ≥ `min_reflex_duration_s`, else ignore | — | see Uncovered |
| Kidnapped (lifted) | `kidnap_status.is_kidnapped` | `human_local` | `human_initiated` (`planned` if a declared reset between tasks) | §2, §6.3 |
| Wheel drop (one wheel) without kidnap | WHEEL_DROP hazard | reflex case | — | — |
| Backup limit reached and backward motion commanded | BACKUP_LIMIT + `cmd_vel.linear.x < 0` + `wheel_vels ≈ 0` | `safety_stop` | `safety` | §2, §6.8 |
| Backup limit present, robot driving forward | BACKUP_LIMIT only | no change | — | — |
| Stall / cliff cancels a drive goal | STALL/CLIFF + goal aborted | `safety_stop` | `safety` | §6.8 (safety function stopped motion) |
| Stall / cliff under cmd_vel control with reflexes disabled | hazard present | **uncovered** — firmware inhibition [UNKNOWN] | — | see Uncovered |
| e-stop engaged (`wheels_enabled=false`) | `wheel_status` | `safety_stop` | `safety` | §6.8 |
| e-stop cleared, then human drives within 60 s | `wheel_status` → human cmd_vel | one intervention, reason `safety`, `related_seq` to the stop | `safety` | §6.7 |
| cmd_vel timeout stop (no message for 0.5 s), commander alive | `wheel_vels` → 0, publisher still in graph | hold → state of current commander (no transition) | — | §6.8 |
| cmd_vel timeout after link loss (publisher gone) | publisher vanished from graph | **uncovered** — proposed `safety_stop`/`safety` (watchdog) | `safety` | see Uncovered |
| Speed limited by proximity slowdown / `max_speed` | OBJECT_PROXIMITY hazard; `max_speed` param | no state change; `x_speed_limited` | — | see Uncovered |
| Standby / low-power (• 10 s) | buttons then all topics gone | **uncovered** — proposed `idle` if no task (declared) else `unknown`; propose `session_end` with `x_cause` | — | see Uncovered |
| Storage mode / power off (⏻ 7 s, `/robot_power`, battery < 2 %) | all topics gone, Pi loses power | end of observation (proposed `session_end`, `x_cause`) | — | §2 rule 4 |
| Firmware update / application restart / reboot | all topics gone, reappear | `unknown` (gap rule) | — | §2 rule 2 |
| BLE mode selected | no ROS 2 over USB | `unknown`; if BLE app declared as device → `human_remote` | `human_initiated` | §6.10 |
| AP mode (•+•• 3 s) | light ring cyan/blue; Wi-Fi topics may drop | `unknown` if topics drop | — | §2 |
| E-stop reset | n/a — software service, no reset phase | — | — | — |

### 14D. TB4-layer modes / phases -> taxonomy state  _[TB4 stack track]_

| Phase | Observable | State (v0.2) | Note |
|---|---|---|---|
| Boot / standby after `turtlebot4.service` starts | `/ip` heartbeats, display shows IP+battery+menu (Standard) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/src/display.cpp#L230-L232, acc. 2026-10-31] | `idle` after `idle_threshold_s` (no task) | session_start should be emitted by the observer, not inferred |
| Docked, power_saver stops camera and lidar | `/dock_status.is_docked` true; `/function_calls` absent; sensor topics stop [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/src/turtlebot4.cpp#L390-L393, acc. 2026-10-31] | `idle` | flag `x_power_saver` |
| Undock via menu / button / controller Home 3 s | `/function_calls` "Undock" then `undock/_action/status` | `shared` (human_initiated) while the firmware executes | uncovered U3 |
| Undock from TurtleBot4Navigator script | `undock/_action/status` without `/function_calls` | `ai` (part of an autonomous task) or `scripted` if the whole run is a fixed script | uncovered U3 |
| Wall Follow Left/Right (menu/button), 10 s | `/function_calls` "Wall Follow ...", `wall_follow/_action/status` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/src/turtlebot4.cpp#L483-L484, acc. 2026-10-31] | `shared` (human_initiated) | reactive firmware behaviour under human trigger; uncovered U3 |
| EStop via menu/button/controller B | `/function_calls` "EStop", `/wheel_status.wheels_enabled` false [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/src/turtlebot4.cpp#L523, acc. 2026-10-31] | `safety_stop` (reason `safety`) | human-caused pause conflicts with 6.8 (`shared`) — uncovered U4 |
| EStop cleared (same button) | `/function_calls` "EStop", wheels_enabled true | return to prior state; if a human resumes within 60 s it is one intervention (6.7) | reason for the return transition undefined — uncovered U5 |
| Power menu item / 3 s power button / auto low-battery | `/function_calls` "Power"; topics stop [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/src/turtlebot4.cpp#L546, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/src/turtlebot4.cpp#L354-L357, acc. 2026-10-31] | `session_end` | automatic power-off is machine-initiated — uncovered U6 |
| SLAM session, human teleop driving to build the map | `/cmd_vel` from teleop nodes; slam_toolbox running [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/tutorials/generate_map.md#L130, acc. 2026-10-31] | `human_local`/`human_remote` (task "mapping") | mapping is human work with AI perception; no state for "AI maps while human drives" — uncovered U7 |
| Localization-only (AMCL running, no goal) | Nav2 active, `/cmd_vel_nav` silent | `idle` between tasks; `ai` hold if a task is open (6.8) | — |
| Nav2 waiting for a goal | lifecycle active, no action goal | `idle` (no task) | "robot ready" is not active time |
| Nav2 executing NavigateToPose | `/cmd_vel_nav` 20 Hz, `/cmd_vel` from collision_monitor | `ai` | goal click = task_start if first, else hint (U8) |
| Nav2 recovery behaviours (spin/backup/wait) | `/cmd_vel_nav` from behavior_server; BT log (Nav2 track) | `ai` | `wait` is an AI-chosen hold (6.8 row 4) |
| Nav2 goal cancelled by human (RViz / cancelTask) | action status CANCELED [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_navigation/turtlebot4_navigation/turtlebot4_navigator.py#L285, acc. 2026-10-31] | `shared` -> `idle`/`human_*` depending on what follows | cancel is a discrete human decision: approval-like event — uncovered U9 |
| Scripted test motions (`ros_tests.py` drive 0.25 m, rotate pi/2 x4; dock/undock test) | `drive_distance`/`rotate_angle` action goals from node `turtlebot4_tests`; test rosbag [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_tests/turtlebot4_tests/ros_tests.py#L419, acc. 2026-10-31] | `scripted` (actor `script:turtlebot4_tests`) | test also asks the human to place the robot on the dock (reset, `human_local`, reason planned) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_tests/turtlebot4_tests/ros_tests.py#L362, acc. 2026-10-31] |
| Mail-delivery / patrol examples | terminal prompt chooses goal, then `startToPose` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/tutorials/turtlebot4_navigator.md#L1185, acc. 2026-10-31] | goal selection = task definition (if before task_start) or hint; execution `ai` | — |
| Human publishes `/cmd_vel` while Nav2 drives | two publishers on `/cmd_vel` | `shared` (human_initiated) for the overlap | uncovered U1 |

### 14D. Nav2 phases → taxonomy state (scope `base`)  _[Nav2 track]_

| Nav2 phase | Observable | State | Reason / event | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Servers UNCONFIGURED / INACTIVE (before `STARTUP`, after `PAUSE`/`RESET`) | N13 `transition_event`, `is_active` false | `idle` if no task open (after `idle_threshold_s`); `unknown` if a task is open and the cause is not attributable; `shared`/`human_initiated` if a human PAUSE is attributable | — | 6.8 |
| ACTIVE, no goal | N1 status not EXECUTING, no `/cmd_vel_nav` | `idle` after `idle_threshold_s` when no task is open; while a task is open a declared `idle` is converted to `unknown` | — | Section 2, 6.8 |
| Goal ACCEPTED→EXECUTING, `ComputePathToPose` RUNNING | N1, N4 | `ai` | `task_start` if this goal is the registered task | — |
| `FollowPath` RUNNING | N1, N4, N23, `/cmd_vel_nav` at 20 Hz | `ai` | — | — |
| Replanning at 1 Hz (`RateController`) | N4 `ComputePathToPose` RUNNING/SUCCESS cycles | `ai` | — | — |
| Contextual recovery (`ClearEntireCostmap` after a recoverable error code) | N4, N5 `number_of_recoveries` += 1 | `ai` | flag `x_recovery` | Not an intervention |
| Recovery subtree: `Spin` / `Wait` / `BackUp` RUNNING | N4, N21 status EXECUTING, N5 | `ai` | flag `x_recovery` | Wait = AI chose to wait (6.8) |
| `GoalUpdated` SUCCESS (new goal during recovery or execution) | N4; new goal_id on N1 | `ai` if the new goal came from a mission node; `shared`/`human_initiated` (hint) if from a declared human device | — | Uncovered 1 |
| `CANCELING` → `CANCELED` | N1; controller zero velocity | `task_end` outcome `aborted` if the task is closed; otherwise `shared`/`human_initiated` (human hold) | — | — |
| `ABORTED` (6 retries exhausted or non-recoverable error code) | N1 status 6, result `error_code` | `task_end` outcome `failure`; robot then stands still — if the task registry keeps the task open this is a hold with no observable cause → `unknown` | — | Uncovered 3: Nav2 has no `help_request` |
| `SUCCEEDED` | N1 status 4 | `task_end` outcome `success` | — | — |
| Preemption accepted (same BT) | N1 new goal_id EXECUTING, `number_of_recoveries` reset | `ai` or `shared` per the goal's origin | — | Uncovered 1 |
| AssistedTeleop EXECUTING | N6, N7 twists, N10 | `shared` | `human_initiated` | The only shared-control mode in the stack |
| AssistedTeleop TIMEOUT / preempt / cancel | N6 terminal, zero twist | back to the surrounding state (`ai`/`idle`) | — | — |
| Collision monitor `STOP` ("invalid source" only on TB4) | N9, `/cmd_vel` zero | `safety_stop` | `safety` | 6.7 pairing with a takeover within 60 s |
| Collision monitor `APPROACH` | N9, `/cmd_vel` scaled | `ai` (flag) | — | Uncovered 4 when scaled to zero |
| `SLOWDOWN` / `LIMIT` | not configured | (`ai`) | — | — |
| Velocity smoother timeout zero (controller silent > 1 s) | `/cmd_vel_smoothed` → 0 then silence | no change by itself; `unknown` if N1 still EXECUTING | — | Uncovered 6 |
| Waypoint following: `WaitAtWaypoint` 200 ms pause | N3 EXECUTING, no `/cmd_vel_nav` | `ai` | — | 6.8 "AI chose to wait" |
| Docking server `NAV_TO_STAGING_POSE`/`INITIAL_PERCEPTION`/`CONTROLLING`/`WAIT_FOR_CHARGE`/`RETRY` | N22 feedback `state` | `ai` (or per N21 if human-triggered) | — | Bypasses smoother/collision monitor; no docks configured on TB4 |
| Planner failure with no recovery left | N1 ABORTED with `error_code` 2xx | `task_end` failure | — | No "ask for help" exists; Uncovered 3 |
| Bond timeout → lifecycle manager RESET | N15 gap, N13 events | `unknown` | — | — |

### 14E. Body parts that can be human-driven while others run on AI

_Lead note._ One actuated motion scope (`base`). Light ring, audio, display, LEDs, camera and lidar motor are not scopes; they can be human-driven while the base runs under AI, but that is not control of motion and is logged only as `x_ui_event`. Nothing on the robot can be human-driven *in motion* while the base is under AI except through contention on `/cmd_vel` (UC-1) or a reflex (UC-4).

### 14E. Body parts  _[Create 3 base track]_

The Create 3 has one motion scope: the differential-drive base (two wheels) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/hw/mechanical.md#L3, acc. 2026-10-31]. The light ring and speaker are actuators but not motion; they are commandable independently (`/cmd_lightring` with `override_system`, `/cmd_audio`, `led_animation`, `audio_note_sequence`) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/ui.md#L15; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/ui.md#L39; https://github.com/iRobotEducation/irobot_create_msgs/blob/b4986ef5dc2e07d8aa7471f29c56b617473352c5/msg/LightringLeds.msg#L14, acc. 2026-10-31] and a human may drive them while the base runs under AI without affecting base control. Recommendation: declare scope `base` only; do **not** declare `leds`/`audio` as scopes (they are not actuated motion and would dilute the scope-weighted share under §6.6). The firmware itself uses the light ring for safety signalling (orange = backup limit, yellow = wheels disabled) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/hw/face.md#L69, acc. 2026-10-31], and overriding it hides those cues [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/ui.md#L13, acc. 2026-10-31] — record `override_system=true` periods as `x_lightring_overridden` for the trust review. Nothing on the base can be human-driven while the wheels run under AI except through the ignored-command path (cmd_vel during an action is dropped [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/moving-the-robot.md#L49, acc. 2026-10-31]) or a reflex overriding the AI.

### 14E. Body parts / scopes  _[TB4 stack track]_

The TurtleBot 4 has exactly one actuated motion scope: the differential-drive **base** (two 72 mm wheels on the Create 3) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4-user-manual/blob/4455a75dde3f052b5abf615dade22bece84120b0/overview/features.md#L75-L76, acc. 2026-10-31]. Everything the TB4 layer adds is non-motion: display (`hmi/display`), LEDs (`hmi/led`, Create 3 lightring via `cmd_lightring`), audio (`cmd_audio`), camera and lidar power (`oakd/start_camera`, `start_motor`) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/src/display.cpp#L49, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/src/leds.cpp#L43, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/config/republisher.yaml#L31, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4_robot/blob/28e115f86930bb3273f1a1aa4738dbd8747513f0/turtlebot4_bringup/config/republisher.yaml#L32, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_node/src/turtlebot4.cpp#L166-L168, acc. 2026-10-31]. The URDF has no actuated joints other than the wheels in the TB4 additions (sensor plate, standoffs, camera bracket are fixed links) [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_description/urdf/standard/turtlebot4.urdf.xacro, acc. 2026-10-31] [VERIFIED: https://github.com/turtlebot/turtlebot4/blob/7fd29fb420e906f3aca4a904adb54b69b11c7c00/turtlebot4_description/urdf/sensors/camera_bracket.urdf.xacro, acc. 2026-10-31]. Recommendation: declare `scopes: ["base"]` (or `whole_robot`); do **not** declare `display`, `leds`, `camera` as scopes — log their events as `x_ui_event` extensions. The RPLIDAR motor (`start_motor`/`stop_motor`) spins a sensor, not a body part, and is excluded.

## Uncovered cases — consolidated decisions (taxonomy v0.2)

Each track flagged the cases its rules did not cover (their original lists are kept verbatim below under "Track lists"). The lead consolidated them into the following numbered cases with a decision that the reference loader and `integration_notes.md` apply. Items marked **v0.3 candidate** need a change to `definitions.md`; the others are loader rules or extension fields (`x_*`) that v0.2 already permits.

1. **UC-1 Message-level contention on `/cmd_vel` (no mux anywhere).** Sources: stack U1, base 13, Nav2 9. Decision: while a human publisher and an AI publisher (Nav2 collision_monitor, docking_server, or a mission script) have both emitted a non-zero command on the robot-side `/cmd_vel` within `contention_window_s` (new loader parameter, default 1 s), the scope is `shared`, reason `human_initiated`, flag `cmd_vel_contention`; the span is one intervention episode (Section 3 of definitions). If per-message attribution is unavailable (UC-2), the span is `unknown`. **v0.3 candidate:** add `contention_window_s` to the parameter table.
2. **UC-2 Attribution needs live publisher identity.** Source: stack U2. Decision: the observer MUST subscribe live and log `publisher_gid` per `/cmd_vel` message and the GID→node/namespace/host map in `session_start.x_publishers`; a pipeline rebuilt from a rosbag2 file alone (no GID) is capped at trust level L1 for all `/cmd_vel`-derived states and must fall back to the topic-level rule (`/cmd_vel` payload equal to the latest `/cmd_vel_smoothed` → Nav2; otherwise human/script) with flag `attribution_topic_level`.
3. **UC-3 Human-triggered firmware or Nav2 primitives (dock, undock, wall-follow, drive goals; Nav2 spin/backup/drive_on_heading/dock_robot).** Sources: base 5, stack U3, Nav2 2, Evidence 4. Decision: when the trigger is a human event (`/function_calls`, `/interface_buttons`, `/hmi/buttons`, a declared GUI/CLI device) within `simultaneity_window_s` before the goal, the whole action is `shared` with reason `human_initiated` and `actor_id` = the human (definitions Section 2: "human sets goals while AI executes"), with `x_firmware_behavior`/`x_primitive` naming the action; when the trigger is a mission node (TurtleBot4Navigator, Nav2 BT) it is `ai` with `actor_id ai:create3:<action>`; when it is part of a fixed script (`turtlebot4_tests`) it is `scripted`. The Evidence track's alternative (hint event + `ai`) is not adopted because the `shared` row of Section 2 is explicit. Requester identity is only visible from the DDS graph; if unknown, `unknown` with flag `requester_unknown`.
4. **UC-4 Firmware reflexes and inhibiting safety functions.** Sources: base 1, 3; Evidence 3. Decision: inhibiting functions (BACKUP_LIMIT brake with a backward command and near-zero `wheel_vels`; cliff/stall cancelling a drive goal; `wheels_enabled=false`) are `safety_stop`/`safety`. Evasive reflex motion (BUMP, CLIFF, WHEEL_DROP, STUCK, PANIC) lasting ≥ `min_reflex_duration_s` (new parameter, default 1 s) is a transition to `ai` with `actor_id ai:create3_reflex` and back, counted in `x_reflex_count`, not as an intervention; shorter episodes cause no transition. Detection is inferential (hazard present + `wheel_vels` ≠ 0 while the commanded velocity is zero or opposite) and impossible on hardware unless UC-10 is applied. **v0.3 candidate:** a `safety_event` counter beside `safety_stops`.
5. **UC-5 Speed scaling (proximity slowdown, `max_speed`, collision-monitor APPROACH/SLOWDOWN/LIMIT).** Sources: base 2, Nav2 4. Decision: no transition; heartbeat field `x_speed_limited: true`. Exception: collision-monitor APPROACH that scales the output to exactly zero for longer than `gap_tolerance_s` is `safety_stop`/`safety` with `x_approach_zero: true`.
6. **UC-6 Stops caused by silence (firmware 0.5 s timeout; velocity_smoother 1.0 s timeout).** Sources: base 4, Nav2 6. Decision: if the commanding publisher is still in the DDS graph the stop is a hold and keeps the commander's state (definitions 6.8); if the publisher has left the graph it is `safety_stop`/`safety` (watchdog); if a Nav2 goal is still EXECUTING while `/cmd_vel_smoothed` has gone silent, `unknown` with `x_controller_silent: true` after `gap_tolerance_s`.
7. **UC-7 Human EStop used as a pause, and its release.** Sources: stack U4, U5; base 11. Decision: entering is `safety_stop`/`safety` with `x_trigger: human_button` (motor power is physically cut, which is what the state measures); release is a transition to the state of the next observed commander within `gap_tolerance_s`, otherwise `unknown` with `related_seq` to the stop; a human command within `takeover_window_s` makes stop + takeover one intervention with reason `safety` (6.7). Reports MAY print `safety_stops_human_triggered` separately. Who cleared the stop is recorded as `x_estop_cleared_by` when the service client identity is visible.
8. **UC-8 Nav2 goals, re-goals, cancels, pose estimates and speed limits from a human.** Sources: Nav2 1, 5; stack U8, U9; Evidence 5. Decision: (a) the first `NavigateToPose` goal after idle is the task definition → `task_start`, no hint; (b) a further goal while a goal is EXECUTING is a hint (`hint_as_shared`: `shared`/`human_initiated` until the new path is being followed or `hint_ttl_s`); (c) a cancel is logged as a `hint` event with `x_kind: cancel` (counts as an intervention, disqualifies the task) and the standstill that follows with the task still open is `shared`/`human_initiated` (6.8) until the next command, or `task_end` outcome `aborted` if the registry closes the task on cancel; (d) `/initialpose` and `/speed_limit` published by a human during a task are hints; `/initialpose` published by a mission script (`BasicNavigator.setInitialPose`) is not; (e) a human 2D Pose Estimate before `task_start` is a between-task human action: `transition` to `human_remote` (RViz device) with reason `planned` and `plan_item_id initial_pose` if pre-registered, else `human_initiated`, ended by the next event — it counts in `reset_human_time`. Who sent a goal is only visible from the DDS graph of `/navigate_to_pose/_action/send_goal` clients; the registry must declare whether a task is "reach pose P" (re-goals are hints) or "operator-directed navigation" (re-goals are the task).
9. **UC-9 No help request exists in Nav2 or the firmware.** Source: Nav2 3. Decision: `ai_requested_help` cannot be evidenced on this platform; a human transition within `help_latency_max_s` after a Nav2 `ABORTED` result is `human_initiated` with `x_after_abort: true` and the error code in `x_nav2_error_code`, reported as a separate count.
10. **UC-10 Firmware safety signals are unobservable with the shipped configuration.** Sources: stack U10 (T13), base 7. Decision: an integration MUST either (i) uncomment `hazard_detection`, `kidnap_status`, `stop_status`, `wheel_vels` (and `ir_opcode`, `slip_status` if wanted) in `republisher.yaml` and declare the change in `session_start.x_config_changes`, or (ii) run the observer on the Pi subscribing to `/_do_not_use/*`; otherwise the report is marked `incomplete` for `safety_stop` metrics and the Unknown bounds apply.
11. **UC-11 Physical buttons, controller and keyboard are "undeclared devices".** Sources: base 7, stack 14C. Decision: the device registry template for TurtleBot 4 pre-declares `create3_button_1`, `create3_button_2`, `create3_button_power`, `hmi_button_1..4`, `manual_handling` (lifting/carrying) as `local`; `tb4_controller` (Bluetooth, terminates at the Pi) as `local`; `teleop_twist_keyboard` must be declared with its host (PC → `remote`, SSH on the Pi → still `remote` under 6.10 unless the operator is declared co-located); the Gazebo GUI teleop and RViz as `remote`. An undeclared publisher is `human_remote` (6.10).
12. **UC-12 Machine-initiated end of observation.** Sources: base 8, stack U6. Decision: when all base topics vanish, the recorder emits `session_end` with `x_cause` ∈ {`standby`, `power_off`, `low_battery_poweroff` (firmware < 2 % or turtlebot4_node ≤ 12 %/60 s), `firmware_update`, `lost`} chosen from the preceding evidence (`/function_calls` "Power", `/interface_buttons` hold pattern, `/battery_state`); if the robot returns, the gap is `unknown` (rule 2) instead.
13. **UC-13 Human-driven SLAM mapping.** Source: stack U7. Decision: `human_local`/`human_remote` per device; `task_definition_id: mapping` so the report excludes mapping from the autonomy numerator explicitly. The AI's perception contribution is not a control state.
14. **UC-14 Zero-velocity "disable" message from teleop_twist_joy.** Source: stack U11. Decision: a zero command from a human publisher ends the human episode at its `ts`; it never opens one.
15. **UC-15 UI-only events (menu scroll, display, LEDs, lightring override, audio).** Sources: stack U12, base 14E. Decision: never a transition; `x_ui_event` on a heartbeat; `override_system=true` lightring periods are recorded as `x_lightring_overridden` because the firmware uses the ring for safety cues.
16. **UC-16 Change-only and volatile state topics; late joiners.** Source: Nav2 7. Decision: each such signal (`/collision_monitor_state`, `/behavior_tree_log`, `transition_event`) is `unknown` for that signal until its first message; the loader derives the scope state from the signals that are known and records `x_signals_unknown`.
17. **UC-17 Lifecycle PAUSE by a human during a task.** Source: Nav2 10. Decision: `shared`/`human_initiated` if the CLI/GUI is a declared device whose call is visible, else `unknown`.
18. **UC-18 Waypoint task with `stop_on_failure: false`.** Source: Nav2 11. Decision: `outcome: success` with `x_missed_waypoints: n`; the task registry defines whether missed waypoints are a failure.
19. **UC-19 Safety-configuration changes.** Source: base 12. Decision: `session_start` carries `x_safety_config` (`safety_override`, `reflexes_enabled`, `reflexes.*`, `publish_hazard_msgs`, `raw_kinematics_min_pub_period_ms`, collision-monitor `enabled`); every change seen on `/parameter_events` is a heartbeat with the new `x_safety_config`.
20. **UC-20 Kidnap gating.** Source: base 10. Decision: `is_kidnapped` true for ≥ 1 s with both WHEEL_DROP hazards → `human_local` (`planned` reset if between tasks and pre-registered, else `human_initiated`); otherwise `unknown`.
21. **UC-21 Ignored commands.** Source: base 6. Decision: a human `/cmd_vel` published while a firmware action runs is dropped by the firmware; log `x_ignored_command` on a heartbeat (no transition, no intervention) so attempted takeovers remain visible.
22. **UC-22 Passive assistance (bystander steps aside on request; Marathon 2).** Source: Nav2 8. **v0.3 candidate:** optional `assistance` event with `scope`, `actor_id`, `dwell_s`, counted like `hint_as_event`.
23. **UC-23 Datasets without a device registry or with undocumented human paths.** Source: Evidence 1, 2. Decision: a dataset-level device registry (one declared device for the whole dataset, `x_ts_reconstructed: true`) is allowed; when a paper documents a human path the data does not label, the dataset is trust L1 with human share lower bound 0 and no upper bound.
24. **UC-24 Regulatory "intervention" ≠ taxonomy intervention.** Source: Evidence 7. Decision: the standard text should state that its interventions are control hand-overs and that the Machinery Regulation 1.1.9/1.2.1(f) "intervention" (tampering with safety hardware/software) is a distinct artefact.
25. **UC-25 Planned maintenance (firmware update, application restart) during a session.** Source: base 9. Decision: `idle` if declared and no task is open, else `unknown`.
26. **UC-26 Nav2 docking server publishing straight to `/cmd_vel`.** Source: Nav2 3.7/9. Decision: treated as one more publisher under UC-1; its goals are attributed under UC-3. On TB4 it has no dock configured, so this is latent.

### Track lists (verbatim)

### Uncovered cases (taxonomy)  _[Create 3 base track]_

1. **Firmware reflex during AI or human control.** A reflex is an autonomous *manoeuvre* (moves the robot away) that overrides the current commander for a short time [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/reflexes.md#L5, acc. 2026-10-31]. v0.2 has `safety_stop` only for inhibited motion and `ai` for controllers "with no human command input"; a reflex during human teleop is neither. Proposed: new parameter `min_reflex_duration_s` (default 1 s); a reflex episode shorter than that is ignored (state unchanged); a longer one is a transition to `ai` with `actor_id = ai:create3_reflex` and back, counted as a `safety_event`, not an intervention. Detection is inferential (Section 6).
2. **Speed scaling (proximity slowdown, `max_speed`).** The firmware scales the commanded velocity [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/reflexes.md#L57-L59, acc. 2026-10-31]; it is neither `shared` nor `safety_stop`. Proposed: no transition; heartbeat field `x_speed_limited: true`.
3. **BACKUP_LIMIT present without a backward command** is not an inhibition; the `safety_stop` rule must condition on commanded direction and near-zero wheel velocity (B4).
4. **cmd_vel timeout stop.** Documented purpose is both "an easy way to stop the robot" and loss-of-connectivity protection [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/moving-the-robot.md#L27, acc. 2026-10-31]; the same observable (wheels stop 0.5 s after the last message) is a hold by the commander or a watchdog stop. Proposed: `safety_stop`/`safety` only when the commanding publisher has left the DDS graph; otherwise the hold takes the commander's state (§6.8).
5. **Firmware action requested by a human versus by software.** Firmware exposes no requester identity; proposed: the observer samples action-client node names from the ROS graph when a goal status changes and applies the device registry; if unknown, `shared` with `actor_id` null is invalid under §3, so fall back to `unknown` and flag `requester_unknown`.
6. **Ignored commands.** cmd_vel published while an action runs is dropped by the firmware [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/api/moving-the-robot.md#L49, acc. 2026-10-31]; a human trying to take over is not in control. Proposed: log a `hint`-like extension event `x_ignored_command` (no transition, no intervention) so that attempted takeovers are visible.
7. **Physical buttons are 'undeclared devices'.** §6.10 would class faceplate button presses as `human_remote`; proposed: the device registry template pre-declares `create3_button_1`, `create3_button_2`, `create3_button_power` as `local`, and `manual_handling` (lifting/carrying) as `local`.
8. **Base-initiated end of observation.** Standby (• 10 s), storage/off (⏻ 7 s), `/robot_power`, firmware power-off at < 2 % battery [VERIFIED: https://github.com/iRobotEducation/create3_docs/blob/9fb1510bbceb0e01c4764ddc458da59487519dca/docs/hw/electrical.md#L15, acc. 2026-10-31] and firmware updates remove the data source without a `session_end`. Proposed: the recorder emits `session_end` with `x_cause ∈ {standby, power_off, low_battery_poweroff, firmware_update}` when all base topics vanish and the preceding evidence matches; otherwise the gap is `unknown` (§2 rule 2) and, if the robot never returns, the session is closed by the recorder with `x_cause: lost`.
9. **Planned maintenance** (firmware update, application restart) during a session: no `maintenance` state; proposed `idle` if declared and no task is open, else `unknown`.
10. **Kidnap false positives / non-human lifts.** Proposed gating: `is_kidnapped` ≥ 1 s and both WHEEL_DROP hazards before `human_local`; otherwise `unknown`.
11. **E-stop set and cleared by software** (same node): §6.7 counts the stop; nothing records who cleared it. Proposed `x_estop_cleared_by` from the DDS client identity when available.
12. **Safety configuration changes** (`safety_override`, `reflexes_enabled`, `publish_hazard_msgs`) alter which safety signals can exist; proposed required `x_safety_config` on `session_start` and on every change (from `/parameter_events`).
13. **Two cmd_vel publishers** (no mux anywhere): the firmware executes the last message of either stream. Proposed: when two publishers are present on the robot-side topic, the scope is `unknown` unless per-message attribution is available (e.g. the observer subscribes to each publisher's own upstream topic before the republisher).

### Uncovered cases (taxonomy)  _[TB4 stack track]_

- **U1 — Message-level contention on `/cmd_vel` with no arbitration.** Two or more publishers interleave; the firmware obeys the last message. v0.2 has no rule for sub-second alternation between `ai` and `human_*`. Proposed handling: while at least one human publisher and Nav2 both emitted a non-zero command within the last `contention_window_s` (default 1 s), the scope is `shared` with reason `human_initiated` and flag `cmd_vel_contention`; the episode counts as one intervention. Evidence: Section 3.5.
- **U2 — Attribution needs live publisher identity.** rosbag2 drops `publisher_gid`; a replay-only pipeline cannot assign `/cmd_vel` to an actor. Proposed handling: the observer MUST log `publisher_gid` and the GID->node/host map in `session_start.x_publishers`; bags without it are capped at trust level L1 for `/cmd_vel`-derived states. Evidence: Section 6.
- **U3 — Human-triggered firmware autonomy (dock, undock, wall follow).** A human presses a button; the firmware then executes a reactive behaviour for seconds. By Section 2 the execution is `ai` (reacts to sensing); by the `shared` definition ("human sets goals while AI executes") it is `shared`. Proposed handling: `shared`/`human_initiated` for the duration of the action when triggered through `/function_calls` or `/interface_buttons`; `ai` when triggered by a script/Nav2 with no human event within `simultaneity_window_s`. Add `x_firmware_behavior: <name>`.
- **U4 — Deliberate human e-stop as a pause.** Section 6.8 says a human-caused hold is `shared`; Section 3 says transitions into `safety_stop` always carry `safety`; the TB4 EStop button is routinely used as a pause. Proposed handling: keep `safety_stop`/`safety` (motor power is cut, which is the physical fact) and add `x_trigger: human_button`; the report MAY separate `safety_stops_human_triggered`.
- **U5 — E-stop release.** No reason value exists for "stop cleared"; the next state is whichever publisher resumes. Proposed handling: transition to `unknown` with `related_seq` pointing at the stop, resolved by the next observed command within `gap_tolerance_s`; if a human resumes within 60 s, apply 6.7.
- **U6 — Machine-initiated session end.** Auto power-off at <= 12 % battery after 60 s is neither human nor AI work. Proposed handling: `session_end` with `x_cause: low_battery`; time after it is unobserved (Section 2 rule 4).
- **U7 — Human-driven SLAM mapping.** The human drives, the AI builds the map. Current rules yield `human_*` (correct for control) but the AI contribution is invisible. Proposed handling: keep `human_*`; record `task_definition_id: mapping` so the report can exclude mapping from the autonomy numerator explicitly.
- **U8 — Same topic for task definition and hints.** An RViz goal before `task_start` defines the task; a goal during a task is a hint. Proposed handling: the observer emits `task_start` on the first NavigateToPose goal after idle and `hint` for subsequent goals until the action result.
- **U9 — Human cancel of a Nav2 goal.** A cancel is a discrete decision that stops AI work; it is neither approval nor hint. Proposed handling: log as `hint` (`hint_as_event`) with `x_kind: cancel`; it counts as an intervention and disqualifies the task.
- **U10 — Firmware safety reflexes unobservable by default.** With the default `republisher.yaml`, bump/cliff/wheel-drop/kidnap stops are not visible on the robot namespace, so `safety_stop` intervals are misreported as `ai`. Proposed handling: integration MUST either enable `hazard_detection`, `kidnap_status`, `stop_status` in `republisher.yaml` (a robot-side change, declared in `session_start.x_config_changes`) or run the observer on the Pi reading `/_do_not_use/*`; otherwise the report is marked `incomplete` for safety metrics.
- **U11 — Zero-velocity "disable" message.** teleop_twist_joy sends one zero command when the dead-man is released. Proposed handling: a zero command from a human publisher ends the human episode at its `ts`; it does not open one.
- **U12 — UI-only events (menu scroll, display, LEDs).** Not control of a motion scope; v0.2 has no event type for them. Proposed handling: `x_ui_event` extension on a `heartbeat`, never a transition.

### Uncovered cases (taxonomy)  _[Nav2 track]_

1. **Who sent the goal.** `NavigateToPose` goals from RViz (human) and from a mission script (AI/plan) are indistinguishable on `_action/status`; the send_goal request is a service call, invisible to a passive subscriber, and the RViz Nav2 Goal tool does not use the `/goal_pose` topic [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_rviz_plugins/src/nav2_panel.cpp#L1299-L1326, acc. 2026-10-31]. Section 2 says "human sets goals while AI executes" is `shared`, while 6.2 says the instruction before `task_start` is the task definition. Proposed handling: (a) the task registry declares whether the task is "reach pose P" (then every re-goal during execution is a hint → `shared` until the new path is followed) or "operator-directed navigation for T minutes" (then re-goals are the task definition and the state stays `ai`); (b) the observer records the ROS graph's action clients of `/navigate_to_pose/_action/send_goal` as the actor registry; a goal that appears while the only connected client is a declared human GUI node is attributed to that device.
2. **Human-triggered motion primitives** (`spin`, `backup`, `drive_on_heading`, `dock_robot`): live human command only at the trigger, then a fixed, collision-checked macro. 6.9 would call the motion `scripted`, Section 2 would call it `human_*`. Proposed: `human_remote`/`human_local` (per device registry) for the whole primitive with `x_primitive` naming it, because the human chose the action and its parameters.
3. **No help request exists.** Nav2 fails a goal (`ABORTED`) after 6 recovery rounds; nothing in the default trees emits a request for human help, so `ai_requested_help` can never be evidenced from Nav2 alone, and a subsequent human action is always `human_initiated`. Proposed: a loader rule that an `ABORTED` with a 1xx/2xx `error_code` followed within `help_latency_max_s` by a human transition is annotated `x_after_abort: true` and reported as a separate count, without changing the reason.
4. **APPROACH scaled to zero.** `processApproach` multiplies the command by `collision_time / time_before_collision`; with `collision_time` 0 the output is exactly zero but the state stays `APPROACH`, not `STOP` [VERIFIED: https://github.com/ros-navigation/navigation2/blob/d5d768b7b38a05e7cfd2b6c693b786b402161bed/nav2_collision_monitor/src/collision_monitor_node.cpp#L594-L606, acc. 2026-10-31]. Proposed: `APPROACH` with zero `/cmd_vel` for longer than `gap_tolerance_s` is logged as `safety_stop`/`safety` with `x_approach_zero: true`.
5. **Localization corrections and speed limits as hints.** `/initialpose` and `/speed_limit` change AI behaviour without commanding actuators or changing the goal. 6.2 covers "a corrected sub-goal" and "a clicked point" but not a belief correction. Proposed: treat both as hints (`hint_as_event`, `dwell_s` 0) and print the sensitivity; exclude `/initialpose` messages published by `BasicNavigator.setInitialPose()` from a mission script (actor is AI).
6. **Controller silent while the goal is EXECUTING.** The velocity smoother zeroes and stops; the cause (controller stall, TF loss) is not observable. Proposed: `unknown` with `x_controller_silent: true` after `gap_tolerance_s`.
7. **Change-only state topics.** `/collision_monitor_state` is published only on change and is volatile; an observer that joins late cannot know the current action. Proposed: an explicit `unknown` for the N9 signal until its first message, plus inference from `/cmd_vel_smoothed` vs `/cmd_vel`.
8. **Passive assistance** (a bystander moves out of the goal pose on request, Marathon 2) is human help that is neither a transition, an approval nor a hint. Proposed: new optional event `assistance` with `scope`, `actor_id`, `dwell_s`, counted like `hint_as_event`.
9. **Simultaneous publishers on `/cmd_vel`.** The Nav2 docking server and the collision monitor both publish to `/cmd_vel` with no arbiter; with a teleop source (TB4 track) three writers can interleave. The taxonomy requires one actor per scope per instant. Proposed: attribute each message to its publisher GID; human and AI messages within `simultaneity_window_s` → `shared`/`human_initiated`.
10. **Lifecycle PAUSE by a human during a task** is a human-caused hold (`shared`) but the `transition_event` carries no actor; proposed `unknown` unless the GUI/CLI used is instrumented.
11. **Waypoint task with `stop_on_failure: false`.** A task may `SUCCEED` with `missed_waypoints` non-empty; the taxonomy's `outcome` has no partial success. Proposed: `outcome: success` plus `x_missed_waypoints: n`, or registry-level definition of success.

### Uncovered cases (taxonomy)  _[Evidence track]_

1. **Co-located teleoperator in a dataset without a device registry (SCAND, GoStanford).** §6.10 says human control through an undeclared device is `human_remote`, but SCAND demonstrators walk with the robot. Proposed handling: allow a dataset-level device registry (one declared device for the whole dataset) with `x_ts_reconstructed: true`, so that re-coded datasets can be `human_local`; otherwise report both codings.
2. **Remote rescue inside an autonomous data-collection run (HuRoN help-and-rescue).** The robot signals the operator, then is teleoperated. This maps to `help_request` → `human_remote` with reason `ai_requested_help`, but the released dataset has no timestamps for either event, so only `unknown` can be asserted. Proposed handling: when a paper documents a human path that the released data does not label, the loader marks the whole dataset `trust L1, labels incomplete` and the report states that the human share is a lower bound of zero with no upper bound.
3. **Create 3 reflex overriding a human command.** Reflexes "temporarily override any user-provided command"; during a bump reflex while a human teleoperates, the base is neither commanded by the human nor by a planner. §6.8 says holds take the state of whoever caused them, but a reflex is motion, not a hold, and is a reactive safety behaviour that is not a stop. Proposed handling: code reflex intervals as `safety_stop` with reason `safety` only if motion is inhibited (PROXIMITY_SLOWDOWN, BACKUP_LIMIT brake), and as `ai` with `actor_id = ai:create3_reflex` for evasive motion (BUMP, CLIFF, WHEEL_DROP, STUCK, PANIC); add a note that this is a sub-second `ai` interval inside human time and should be reported in `x_reflex_count` rather than as an intervention.
4. **Button-triggered dock/undock on the base.** A human presses Create 3 button 1/2 (`human_local` action lasting < 1 s) and the base then executes a sensor-driven docking behaviour. Proposed handling: the press is a `hint` event (hint_as_event) from a local device and the docking motion is `ai` with `actor_id = ai:create3_dock`; the task is not autonomous if the press occurs inside an open task.
5. **RViz 2D Pose Estimate before task start.** The pose estimate is required by Nav2 before any goal; if issued before `task_start` it is neither a hint nor a reset under current rules. Proposed handling: treat it as a `planned` human action between tasks (`human_remote`/`human_local` transition of zero or measured dwell, reason `planned`, `plan_item_id = initial_pose`), so that it counts in `reset_human_time` and is visible.
6. **Nav2 recoveries.** They are AI behaviours and currently vanish into `ai` time. Proposed handling: record them as `x_recovery` annotations on `heartbeat` events with a cumulative count so that `recoveries_per_active_hour` can be reported beside `safety_stops_per_active_hour`.
7. **Regulatory "intervention" vs taxonomy intervention.** Machinery Regulation 1.1.9/1.2.1(f) use "intervention" for tampering with safety hardware/software, not for a human taking control. Proposed handling: the standard should state that its interventions are control hand-overs and that the 1.2.1(f) tracing log is a distinct artefact.

## Unknowns

Cross-cutting (lead): list price and availability of TurtleBot 4 and Create 3 from an official page; live message rates, latencies and CPU cost on a Raspberry Pi 4 (nothing was measured; all rates are configured values); whether `route_server` starts cleanly with the TB4 `nav2.yaml`; whether any TurtleBot 4 / Create 3 bag with teleop labels exists under a non-descriptive name; the texts of ISO 13482, ISO 3691-4, ANSI/RIA R15.08-1, UL 3300, OMG DDS-Security 1.1 and 47 CFR Part 15 (hosts not reachable). Per-track lists follow verbatim.

### Unknowns  _[Create 3 base track]_

- Create 3 list price and current availability (official page unreachable: HTTP 502 via proxy after access grant).
- Firmware publisher QoS profiles (reliability/durability/depth) for every topic; only `cmd_audio` subscription (reliable) is documented.
- Publication rates of `/hazard_detection` (event-driven), `/kidnap_status`, `/dock_status`, `/interface_buttons`, `/ir_intensity`, `/cliff_intensity`; edge-vs-periodic semantics of `/interface_buttons`.
- Timeout for `/cmd_vel_stamped`; behaviour when Twist and TwistStamped streams arrive concurrently; whether two action goals can be active.
- Exact firmware log strings (only the simulator's are visible); `log_period` default; log retention beyond power loss.
- Whether the firmware inhibits cmd_vel toward a detected cliff/bump when reflexes are disabled.
- Backup-limit distances in the real firmware (simulator: warn 0.05 m, stop 0.15 m).
- Power-button hold duration conflict: 7 s (face.md, FAQ, Product Information Guide) vs 10 s (electrical.md); which applies on I.0.0.
- Whether ROS 2 topics continue over Wi-Fi while the USB/BLE toggle is at BLE.
- Whether the firmware reacts to any handheld IR remote (no remote opcodes defined).
- Webserver authentication (none described) and `.swu` signature verification.
- SROS2 / DDS-Security support (not mentioned).
- Any telemetry sent by the base (no statement either way).
- Whether the application-restart-on-connectivity-change behaviour persists in I.0.0.
- Internal processors ("mobility board", "nav board") and base RAM.
- Hidden action topic names (`<action>/_action/status`, `/_action/feedback`): ROS 2 design doc not reachable from the sandbox — CLAIM.
- TurtleBot 4 manual statements "Motors will be disabled in this [kidnap] state" and the `/stall_status` topic: not confirmed by iRobot docs (the latter contradicts the firmware topic list).

### Unknowns  _[TB4 stack track]_

- List price and availability/lead time of TurtleBot 4 Standard and Lite from the maker (official page is a documentation gate; store subdomain not reached).
- Role of Open Robotics / OSRF in the TurtleBot 4 program (not stated in pinned sources).
- Create 3 firmware-side handling of `/cmd_vel`: consumption rate, timeout, arbitration between `cmd_vel` and `cmd_vel_unstamped`, publication rates of `interface_buttons`, `wheel_status`, `dock_status`, `battery_state`, `odom` (Create 3 track).
- Official latency figures for Bluetooth teleop and Wi-Fi DDS teleop (none published).
- Numeric CPU load / bandwidth budgets for the Pi (only qualitative statements).
- Whether `route_server` (listed in nav2_bringup lifecycle nodes) starts cleanly with the TB4 `nav2.yaml`, which has no `route_server` block (Nav2 track).
- Whether Nav2 `docking_server` is usable with no `docks` list configured (Nav2 track).
- Exact publish rule of the LED topics `hmi/led/_*` (publisher created in `Turtlebot4Led::create_publisher`; spin logic not fully read).
- `diagnostic_updater` publish period as used by `turtlebot4_diagnostics` (not set in TB4 code; library default).
- Raspberry Pi 4 RTC absence: inferred from official docs describing the RTC as a Pi 5 feature; no explicit Pi 4 statement opened.
- On-disk retention of ROS node logs under the `turtlebot4.service` user and journald rotation on the shipped image.
- gz `Teleop` GUI plugin publish rate and whether its topic `/cmd_vel` matches a namespaced robot's bridge.
- Licence of the user-manual text itself (no LICENSE file in the gh-pages tree).
- `ros2 topic info -v` output format (ros2cli) was not cited from primary text.

### Unknowns  _[Nav2 track]_

- **nav2_route `route_server` with no parameters on TB4**: the launch starts it and the lifecycle manager will try to configure it, but its behaviour without a graph file (configure failure → manager abort?) was not verified; if it fails, `autostart` of the whole navigation group may fail. UNKNOWN; needs a live launch or reading `nav2_route/src/route_server.cpp`.
- **rmw default QoS profile values** (depth 10, reliable, volatile) assumed for `transition_event` and action feedback: CLAIM from general ROS 2 knowledge, not re-read at a pinned `rmw` commit.
- **BehaviorTree.CPP version shipped with Jazzy** and the exact clock used for `BehaviorTreeStatusChange.timestamp`: the `high_resolution_clock` call was read on the BT.CPP master branch, not at the Jazzy release; CLAIM.
- **bond `Status` message type and QoS**: not read at a pinned bondcpp commit; CLAIM.
- **`/plan` and `/local_plan` publishers**: topic names taken from Nav2 convention; publisher lines not read in this track.
- **Whether `rviz2` on the TB4 user PC can be instrumented to publish `device_id`**: outside the sources read.
- **Live-system behaviour** (actual message rates under load on the Raspberry Pi 4, whether `controller_frequency` 20 Hz is met): not measured; all rates above are configured values, not observed ones.

### Unknowns  _[Evidence track]_

- Whether any TurtleBot 4 / Create 3 bag with teleop labels exists under a non-descriptive name on Hugging Face, Zenodo, Dataverse or institutional repositories (HF search matches names only; zenodo.org blocked).
- Contents of the RoboVAST Navigation Dataset (Zenodo 10.5281/zenodo.19146985) beyond what the arXiv paper states.
- Whether the Marathon 2 CSV logs are retrievable anywhere (not in the repository tree at commit b331bcf).
- HuRoN: number and duration of remote rescues; whether the authors' raw (non-LeRobot) release contains them.
- Create 3 FCC ID and any functional-safety rating of its reflexes/safety features (no statement in create3_docs).
- Whether the Create 3 webserver has any authentication (docs silent; base track to confirm).
- Text of ISO 13482, ISO 3691-4, ANSI/RIA R15.08-1, UL 3300, OMG DDS-Security 1.1, Fast DDS discovery-server docs, 47 CFR Part 15, ROS 2 DDS-Security design article (all hosts blocked).
- Whether a commercially sold education robot used in a university lab falls under Machinery Regulation Art. 2(2)(m) (legal interpretation).
- Peer-review status of the BARN challenge reports (only arXiv versions opened).
